- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `server/app/rally/writes.py`**

```python
"""Writes (spec §6.3). Every write passes the guard before any Rally write is made (spec §11)."""
import asyncio

from ..domain import Issue, IssueCreate, IssuePatch, RankRequest
from .client import RallyError
from .issues import IssueReads
from .mapping import (ISSUE_FETCH, STATUS_WRITE, TYPE_KEY, TYPE_PATH, issue_from_rally, issue_patch_to_rally, oid,
                      or_query, priority_to_rally, quoted)


class IssueWrites(IssueReads):
    async def tag_refs(self, names: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        if not names:
            return []
        rows = await self.client.query("/tag", await self.params(
            fetch="Name", query=or_query([f"Name = {quoted(n)}" for n in names])), limit=len(names) * 2)
        by_name = {r["Name"]: f"/tag/{oid(r)}" for r in rows}
        missing = [n for n in names if n not in by_name]
        if missing:
            raise RallyError(422, f"Unknown label: {missing[0]}")
        return [by_name[n] for n in names]

    async def _demo_targets(self, project_id: int | None, cycle_id: int | None) -> None:
        """While the guard is on, a write may only point at [DEMO] projects and cycles of the demo team."""
        if not self.guard.active:
            return
        if project_id is not None:
            rows = await self.by_ids("/portfolioitem/feature", "ObjectID,Name,Project", [project_id])
            self.guard.check_project(rows[0]["Name"] if rows else None, oid(rows[0]["Project"]) if rows else None)
        if cycle_id is not None:
            rows = await self.by_ids("/iteration", "ObjectID,Project", [cycle_id])
            self.guard.check_cycle(oid(rows[0]["Project"]) if rows else None)

    async def update_issue(self, issue_id: int, patch: IssuePatch) -> Issue:
        current = issue_from_rally(await self.find_issue_row(str(issue_id)))
        self.guard.check_existing(name=current.title, tags=current.labels, team_id=current.team_id)
        changes = patch.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
        self.guard.check_patch(changes)
        await self._demo_targets(changes.get("project_id"), changes.get("cycle_id"))
        refs = await self.tag_refs(changes["labels"]) if "labels" in changes else None
        fields = issue_patch_to_rally(patch, current.kind, refs)
        body = await self.client.post(f"/{TYPE_PATH[current.kind]}/{issue_id}", {TYPE_KEY[current.kind]: fields},
                                      {"fetch": ISSUE_FETCH})
        return issue_from_rally(body["OperationResult"]["Object"])

    async def rank_issue(self, issue_id: int, req: RankRequest) -> Issue:
        target_id = req.above if req.above is not None else req.below
        if target_id == issue_id:
            raise ValueError("an issue can't be ranked relative to itself")
        current_row, target_row = await asyncio.gather(self.find_issue_row(str(issue_id)), self.find_issue_row(str(target_id)))
        current, target = issue_from_rally(current_row), issue_from_rally(target_row)
        self.guard.check_existing(name=current.title, tags=current.labels, team_id=current.team_id)
        side = "rankAbove" if req.above is not None else "rankBelow"
        body = await self.client.post(f"/{TYPE_PATH[current.kind]}/{issue_id}", {TYPE_KEY[current.kind]: {}},
                                      {side: f"/{TYPE_PATH[target.kind]}/{target.id}", "fetch": ISSUE_FETCH})
        return issue_from_rally(body["OperationResult"]["Object"])

    async def create_issue(self, data: IssueCreate) -> Issue:
        """Creates a story (defects can't be created, spec §17)."""
        self.guard.check_team(data.team_id)
        self.guard.check_create(data.title)
        await self._demo_targets(data.project_id, data.cycle_id)
        labels = list(dict.fromkeys(data.labels))
        if self.guard.active and self.settings.demo_tag not in labels:
            labels.append(self.settings.demo_tag)
        fields: dict = {"Name": data.title, "Project": f"/project/{data.team_id}",
                        "PortfolioItem": f"/portfolioitem/feature/{data.project_id}", "Description": data.description,
                        **STATUS_WRITE[data.status]}
        if data.cycle_id is not None:
            fields["Iteration"] = f"/iteration/{data.cycle_id}"
        if data.priority:
            field, value = priority_to_rally("story", data.priority)
            fields[field] = value
        if data.assignee_id is not None:
            fields["Owner"] = f"/user/{data.assignee_id}"
        if data.estimate is not None:
            fields["PlanEstimate"] = data.estimate
        if labels:
            fields["Tags"] = [{"_ref": r} for r in await self.tag_refs(labels)]
        body = await self.client.post("/hierarchicalrequirement/create", {"HierarchicalRequirement": fields},
                                      {"fetch": ISSUE_FETCH})
        created = body["CreateResult"]["Object"]
        if data.position == "bottom":  # Rally ranks new items at the top by default
            body = await self.client.post(f"/hierarchicalrequirement/{created['ObjectID']}", {"HierarchicalRequirement": {}},
                                          {"rankTo": "BOTTOM", "fetch": ISSUE_FETCH})
            created = body["OperationResult"]["Object"]
        return issue_from_rally(created)
```

In the create test with `status="todo"`, the `STATUS_WRITE["todo"]` entry has no Iteration, so the `"Iteration": "/iteration/301"` value comes from `cycle_id`. For a backlog create, `STATUS_WRITE` supplies `"Iteration": None`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (append to `writes.py`; extend the imports with `Comment, CommentCreate, Label, SubIssue, SubIssueCreate, SubIssuePatch, TagCreate` from domain and `POST_FETCH, SUB_ISSUE_FETCH, comment_from_rally, short_ref, sub_issue_from_rally, sub_issue_patch_to_rally, tag_names` from mapping)

```python
    async def _sub_issue_row(self, sub_id: int) -> dict:
        rows = await self.client.query("/task", await self.params(fetch=SUB_ISSUE_FETCH, query=f"(ObjectID = {int(sub_id)})"), limit=1)
        if not rows:
            raise RallyError(404, f"Sub-issue {sub_id} was not found")
        return rows[0]

    def _guard_sub_issue(self, row: dict) -> None:
        self.guard.check_existing(name=row["Name"], tags=tag_names(row.get("Tags")), team_id=oid(row["Project"]))

    async def create_sub_issue(self, issue_id: int, data: SubIssueCreate) -> SubIssue:
        parent = issue_from_rally(await self.find_issue_row(str(issue_id)))
        self.guard.check_existing(name=parent.title, tags=parent.labels, team_id=parent.team_id)
        self.guard.check_create(data.title)
        fields: dict = {"Name": data.title, "WorkProduct": f"/{TYPE_PATH[parent.kind]}/{parent.id}",
                        "Project": f"/project/{parent.team_id}", "State": "Defined"}
        if data.assignee_id is not None:
            fields["Owner"] = f"/user/{data.assignee_id}"
        if data.hours is not None:
            fields["Estimate"] = fields["ToDo"] = data.hours
        if self.guard.active:
            fields["Tags"] = [{"_ref": r} for r in await self.tag_refs([self.settings.demo_tag])]
        body = await self.client.post("/task/create", {"Task": fields}, {"fetch": SUB_ISSUE_FETCH})
        return sub_issue_from_rally(body["CreateResult"]["Object"])

    async def update_sub_issue(self, sub_id: int, patch: SubIssuePatch) -> SubIssue:
        self._guard_sub_issue(await self._sub_issue_row(sub_id))
        self.guard.check_patch(patch.model_dump(exclude_unset=True))
        body = await self.client.post(f"/task/{sub_id}", {"Task": sub_issue_patch_to_rally(patch)}, {"fetch": SUB_ISSUE_FETCH})
        return sub_issue_from_rally(body["OperationResult"]["Object"])

    async def delete_sub_issue(self, sub_id: int) -> None:
        self._guard_sub_issue(await self._sub_issue_row(sub_id))
        await self.client.delete(f"/task/{sub_id}")

    async def add_comment(self, issue_id: int, data: CommentCreate) -> Comment:
        issue = issue_from_rally(await self.find_issue_row(str(issue_id)))
        self.guard.check_existing(name=issue.title, tags=issue.labels, team_id=issue.team_id)
        body = await self.client.post("/conversationpost/create", {"ConversationPost": {
            "Artifact": f"/{TYPE_PATH[issue.kind]}/{issue.id}", "Text": data.html}}, {"fetch": POST_FETCH})
        return comment_from_rally(body["CreateResult"]["Object"])

    async def delete_comment(self, post_id: int) -> None:
        """Deletes a comment or an update (an update is a post too, planning ruling 2)."""
        rows = await self.client.query("/conversationpost", await self.params(fetch=POST_FETCH, query=f"(ObjectID = {int(post_id)})"), limit=1)
        if not rows:
            raise RallyError(404, f"Comment {post_id} was not found")
        if self.guard.active:
            path = short_ref(rows[0]["Artifact"]["_ref"])
            body = await self.client.get(path, {"fetch": "Name,Tags,Project"})
            item = next(iter(body.values()))
            if path.startswith("/portfolioitem/"):
                self.guard.check_portfolio(item["Name"], oid(item["Project"]))
            else:
                self.guard.check_existing(name=item["Name"], tags=tag_names(item.get("Tags")), team_id=oid(item["Project"]))
        await self.client.delete(f"/conversationpost/{post_id}")

    async def create_tag(self, data: TagCreate) -> Label:
        self.guard.check_tag_create()
        name = data.name.strip()
        rows = await self.client.query("/tag", await self.params(fetch="Name", query=f"(Name = {quoted(name)})"), limit=1)
        if rows:
            return Label(name=rows[0]["Name"])
        body = await self.client.post("/tag/create", {"Tag": {"Name": name}}, {"fetch": "Name"})
        return Label(name=body["CreateResult"]["Object"]["Name"])
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `server/app/rally/portfolio.py`**

```python
"""Projects (Features), initiatives (Capabilities) and PIs (Releases) for the PM layer (spec §6.2, §9)."""
import asyncio
from datetime import timedelta

from ..domain import (Initiative, InitiativeDetail, InitiativeList, Project, ProjectDetail, ProjectList, Release, Update)
from ..pm import initiative_health, project_health, split_posts, update_from_comment
from .base import CHUNK, dedupe, unique_users
from .client import RallyError
from .issues import KEY_RE, IssueReads, open_projects_query
from .mapping import (INITIATIVE_FETCH, ISSUE_FETCH, PROJECT_FETCH, RELEASE_FETCH, and_query, comment_from_rally,
                      initiative_from_rally, issue_from_rally, oid, or_query, project_from_rally, quoted,
                      release_from_rally)

BLOCKED_TTL = timedelta(seconds=60)  # planning ruling 7
UPDATE_MARK = "Mira update"


class PortfolioReads(IssueReads):
    async def blocked_counts(self) -> dict[int, int]:
        """Blocked open stories per project, from one workspace-wide query cached for a minute."""
        now = self.clock()
        if self._blocked and now - self._blocked[0] < BLOCKED_TTL:
            return self._blocked[1]
        rows = await self.client.query("/hierarchicalrequirement", await self.params(
            fetch="Feature", query='((Blocked = true) AND (ScheduleState < "Accepted"))'), limit=2000)
        counts: dict[int, int] = {}
        for r in rows:
            fid = oid(r.get("Feature"))
            if fid:
                counts[fid] = counts.get(fid, 0) + 1
        self._blocked = (now, counts)
        return counts

    async def latest_updates(self, type_path: str, ids) -> dict[int, Update]:
        """The newest Mira update on each item."""
        wanted = sorted(set(ids))
        if not wanted:
            return {}
        base = await self.params(fetch="ObjectID,Text,User,CreationDate,Artifact", order="CreationDate DESC")
        batches = await asyncio.gather(*(
            self.client.query("/conversationpost", {**base, "query": and_query([
                f"Text contains {quoted(UPDATE_MARK)}", or_query([f"Artifact = /{type_path}/{i}" for i in wanted[n:n + CHUNK]])])},
                limit=400)
            for n in range(0, len(wanted), CHUNK)))
        latest: dict[int, Update] = {}
        for row in (r for batch in batches for r in batch):
            update = update_from_comment(comment_from_rally(row))
            artifact = oid(row["Artifact"])
            if update and artifact not in latest:
                latest[artifact] = update
        return latest

    async def capability_states(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        if self._capability_states is None:
            rows = await self.client.query("/state", await self.params(
                fetch="Name,OrderIndex", query='(TypeDef.ElementName = "Capability")', order="OrderIndex"), limit=50)
            self._capability_states = {r["Name"]: int(r["OrderIndex"]) for r in rows}
        return self._capability_states

    async def find_portfolio_row(self, type_path: str, fetch: str, ref: str) -> dict:
        text = str(ref).strip()
        if text.isdigit():
            query = f"(ObjectID = {int(text)})"
        elif KEY_RE.fullmatch(text):
            query = f"(FormattedID = {quoted(text.upper())})"
        else:
            raise ValueError("give an ID like F1234")
        rows = await self.client.query(f"/{type_path}", await self.params(fetch=fetch, query=query), limit=1)
        if not rows:
            raise RallyError(404, f"{text.upper()} was not found")
        return rows[0]

    async def _with_health(self, rows: list[dict]) -> list[Project]:
        projects = [project_from_rally(r) for r in dedupe(rows)]
        blocked, latest = await asyncio.gather(self.blocked_counts(),
                                               self.latest_updates("portfolioitem/feature", [p.id for p in projects]))
        today = self.clock().date()
        return [p.model_copy(update={"latest_update": latest.get(p.id),
                                     "health": project_health(p, today=today, blocked=blocked.get(p.id, 0), latest=latest.get(p.id))})
                for p in projects]

    async def list_projects(self, team_ids: list[int], initiative_id: int | None = None) -> ProjectList:
        if initiative_id is not None:
            rows = await self.client.query("/portfolioitem/feature", await self.params(
                fetch=PROJECT_FETCH, query=f"(Parent = /portfolioitem/capability/{int(initiative_id)})", order="PlannedEndDate"), limit=500)
        else:
            if not team_ids:
                raise ValueError("give teams or an initiative")
            now = self.clock()
            teams = await self.teams(team_ids)
            batches = await asyncio.gather(*(self.client.query("/portfolioitem/feature", await self.params(
                t, fetch=PROJECT_FETCH, query=open_projects_query(now), order="PlannedEndDate"), limit=1000) for t in teams))
            rows = [r for batch in batches for r in batch]
        projects = await self._with_health(rows)
        initiative_rows, states, teams = await asyncio.gather(
            self.by_ids("/portfolioitem/capability", INITIATIVE_FETCH, [p.initiative_id for p in projects if p.initiative_id]),
            self.capability_states(), self.teams(p.team_id for p in projects))
        return ProjectList(projects=projects, initiatives=[initiative_from_rally(r, states) for r in initiative_rows], teams=teams)

    async def project_detail(self, ref: str) -> ProjectDetail:
        row = await self.find_portfolio_row("portfolioitem/feature", PROJECT_FETCH + ",Description", ref)
        (project,) = await self._with_health([row])
        artifact = f"/portfolioitem/feature/{project.id}"
        issue_rows, post_rows = await asyncio.gather(
            self.client.query("/hierarchicalrequirement", await self.params(
                fetch=ISSUE_FETCH, query=f"(Feature = {artifact})", order="DragAndDropRank ASC"), limit=2000),
            self.posts(artifact, order="CreationDate DESC"))
        issues = [issue_from_rally(r) for r in issue_rows]
        updates, comments = split_posts([comment_from_rally(r) for r in post_rows])
        cycles, _, users = await self.refs(issues, have_projects=[project.id])
        teams = await self.teams({i.team_id for i in issues} | {project.team_id})
        people = [*users, project.lead, *(u.author for u in updates), *(c.author for c in comments)]
        return ProjectDetail(project=project, description=row.get("Description") or "", issues=issues, updates=updates,
                             comments=comments, cycles=cycles, teams=teams, users=unique_users(people))

    async def _initiatives(self, ids: list[int]) -> InitiativeList:
        wanted = sorted(set(ids))
        base = await self.params(fetch=PROJECT_FETCH)
        cap_rows, child_batches, states, latest = await asyncio.gather(
            self.by_ids("/portfolioitem/capability", INITIATIVE_FETCH, wanted),
            asyncio.gather(*(self.client.query("/portfolioitem/feature", {**base, "query": or_query(
                [f"Parent = /portfolioitem/capability/{i}" for i in wanted[n:n + CHUNK]])}, limit=1000)
                for n in range(0, len(wanted), CHUNK))),
            self.capability_states(),
            self.latest_updates("portfolioitem/capability", wanted))
        children = await self._with_health([r for batch in child_batches for r in batch])
        out: list[Initiative] = []
        for row in cap_rows:
            i = initiative_from_rally(row, states)
            active = [p.health.value for p in children if p.initiative_id == i.id and p.status_category == "started" and p.health]
            out.append(i.model_copy(update={"latest_update": latest.get(i.id), "project_health": active,
                                            "health": initiative_health(latest.get(i.id), active)}))
        return InitiativeList(initiatives=out, projects=children)

    async def list_initiatives(self, team_ids: list[int]) -> InitiativeList:
        """Capabilities above your teams' projects (spec assumption 12)."""
        listing = await self.list_projects(team_ids)
        ids = [p.initiative_id for p in listing.projects if p.initiative_id]
        return await self._initiatives(ids) if ids else InitiativeList(initiatives=[], projects=[])

    async def initiative_detail(self, ref: str) -> InitiativeDetail:
        row = await self.find_portfolio_row("portfolioitem/capability", INITIATIVE_FETCH + ",Description", ref)
        cap_id = int(row["ObjectID"])
        listing, post_rows = await asyncio.gather(self._initiatives([cap_id]),
                                                  self.posts(f"/portfolioitem/capability/{cap_id}", order="CreationDate DESC"))
        updates, comments = split_posts([comment_from_rally(r) for r in post_rows])
        return InitiativeDetail(initiative=listing.initiatives[0], description=row.get("Description") or "",
                                projects=listing.projects, updates=updates, comments=comments)

    async def releases(self, team_ids: list[int]) -> list[Release]:
        """PIs for timeline bands. Each team has its own copy of a PI; copies with the same name and dates merge."""
        teams = await self.teams(team_ids)
        batches = await asyncio.gather(*(self.client.query("/release", await self.params(
            t, fetch=RELEASE_FETCH, order="ReleaseDate DESC"), limit=12) for t in teams))
        merged: dict[tuple, Release] = {}
        for row in (r for batch in batches for r in batch):
            rel = release_from_rally(row)
            merged.setdefault((rel.name, rel.start, rel.end), rel)
        return sorted(merged.values(), key=lambda r: r.start or "")
```

In `test_initiatives_roll_up_active_project_health`, the child feature F201 is "Current PI" (started) and has the reported At risk update, so the initiative's auto health is `at_risk`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** (append to `PortfolioReads`; add `UpdateCreate` to the domain import, `format_update` to the pm import, and `POST_FETCH, day` to the mapping import)

```python
    async def post_update(self, kind: str, ref: str, data: UpdateCreate) -> Update:
        """Spec §9.3: a discussion post with the health header and a progress/target footer."""
        type_path, fetch = (("portfolioitem/feature", PROJECT_FETCH) if kind == "project"
                            else ("portfolioitem/capability", INITIATIVE_FETCH))
        row = await self.find_portfolio_row(type_path, fetch, ref)
        self.guard.check_portfolio(row["Name"], oid(row["Project"]))
        text = format_update(data.health, data.html, float(row.get("PercentDoneByStoryPlanEstimate") or 0),
                             day(row.get("PlannedEndDate")))
        body = await self.client.post("/conversationpost/create", {"ConversationPost": {
            "Artifact": f"/{type_path}/{row['ObjectID']}", "Text": text}}, {"fetch": POST_FETCH})
        update = update_from_comment(comment_from_rally(body["CreateResult"]["Object"]))
        if update is None:
            raise RallyError(502, "Rally rewrote the update so it can no longer be read back")
        return update
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (`server/tests/test_session.py`)

```python
import stat

import pytest

from app.credentials import read_key, write_key
from app.rally.client import RallyError
from tests.adapter_kit import make_adapter, standard
from tests.fake_client import FakeClient

KEY = "_abcdefSECRETKEY123"


def factory():
    made = []

    def make(key: str) -> FakeClient:
        c = FakeClient(connected=bool(key))
        if key == KEY:
            standard(c)
        else:
            c.on("GET", "/user", RallyError(401, "Rally rejected the API key"))
        made.append(c)
        return c
    return make, made


def test_write_key_is_private(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "mira" / "credentials"
    write_key(KEY + "\n", path)
    assert read_key(path) == KEY
    assert stat.S_IMODE(path.stat().st_mode) == 0o600
    assert stat.S_IMODE(path.parent.stat().st_mode) == 0o700
    assert read_key(tmp_path / "missing") is None


async def test_session_without_a_key_makes_no_rally_call():
    c = FakeClient(connected=False)
    s = await make_adapter(c).session()
    assert (s.connected, s.me, s.write_guard) == (False, None, "demo") and c.calls == []


async def test_connect_validates_saves_and_swaps(tmp_path):
    make, made = factory()
    old = FakeClient(connected=False)
    a = make_adapter(old, factory=make, tmp_credentials=tmp_path / "credentials")
    s = await a.connect(KEY)
    assert s.connected and s.me.name == "Dana Demo" and s.workspace.name == "Demo Workspace"
    assert read_key(tmp_path / "credentials") == KEY and old.closed and a.client is made[0]
    assert KEY not in s.model_dump_json()


async def test_a_rejected_key_changes_nothing(tmp_path):
    make, made = factory()
    old = standard(FakeClient())
    a = make_adapter(old, factory=make, tmp_credentials=tmp_path / "credentials")
    with pytest.raises(RallyError) as e:
        await a.connect("wrong")
    assert e.value.status == 401 and e.value.message == "Rally rejected that key"
    assert a.client is old and made[0].closed and not (tmp_path / "credentials").exists()


async def test_disconnect_forgets_the_key(tmp_path):
    make, _ = factory()
    a = make_adapter(standard(FakeClient()), factory=make, tmp_credentials=tmp_path / "credentials")
    await a.connect(KEY)
    s = await a.disconnect()
    assert not s.connected and not (tmp_path / "credentials").exists()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `server/app/credentials.py`**

```python
"""The Rally API key Mira was connected with (spec D14): one line in ~/.config/mira/credentials, mode 0600."""
import os
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT_PATH = Path.home() / ".config" / "mira" / "credentials"


def read_key(path: Path) -> str | None:
    try:
        key = Path(path).read_text().strip()
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return None
    return key or None


def write_key(key: str, path: Path) -> None:
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    os.chmod(path.parent, 0o700)
    tmp = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    fd = os.open(tmp, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
    with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as f:
        f.write(key.strip() + "\n")
    os.chmod(tmp, 0o600)
    os.replace(tmp, path)


def delete_key(path: Path) -> None:
    Path(path).unlink(missing_ok=True)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add session methods to `RallyBase`** (`server/app/rally/base.py`; import `Session` from domain, and `delete_key, write_key` from `..credentials`)

```python
    async def session(self) -> Session:
        """Spec §6.1. No Rally call when no key is set. The key itself is never part of the answer."""
        guard = self.settings.write_guard
        if not self.client.connected:
            return Session(connected=False, write_guard=guard)
        try:
            me, workspace = await asyncio.gather(self.me(), self.workspace())
        except RallyError as e:
            if e.status == 401:
                return Session(connected=False, write_guard=guard, error=e.message)
            raise
        return Session(connected=True, me=me, workspace=workspace, write_guard=guard)

    async def connect(self, api_key: str) -> Session:
        """Validate the key with Rally, save it (0600), then swap clients. A rejected key changes nothing."""
        if self._client_factory is None:
            raise RuntimeError("this adapter can't make clients")
        candidate = self._client_factory(api_key.strip())
        try:
            await candidate.get("/user", {"fetch": "ObjectID"})
        except RallyError as e:
            await candidate.aclose()
            if e.status == 401:
                raise RallyError(401, "Rally rejected that key") from None
            raise
        write_key(api_key, self.settings.mira_credentials)
        old, self.client = self.client, candidate
        await old.aclose()
        self._reset()
        return await self.session()

    async def disconnect(self) -> Session:
        """Forget the saved key. A key in .env returns when the proxy restarts."""
        delete_key(self.settings.mira_credentials)
        if self._client_factory is not None:
            old, self.client = self.client, self._client_factory("")
            await old.aclose()
        self._reset()
        return await self.session()
```

```python
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.domain import Session, User
from app.guard import GUARD_MESSAGE, GuardError
from app.main import create_app, parse_ids
from app.rally.client import RallyError
from app.rally.mapping import issue_from_rally
from tests.fixtures import rally_story

import pytest


class FakeAdapter:
    """Any method named in `behaviour` exists; its value is returned (or raised, or called with the args)."""

    def __init__(self, **behaviour):
        self.behaviour = behaviour
        self.calls: list[tuple] = []
        self.closed = False

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        if name.startswith("_") or name not in self.behaviour:
            raise AttributeError(name)

        async def call(*args):
            self.calls.append((name, *args))
            b = self.behaviour[name]
            if isinstance(b, Exception):
                raise b
            return b(*args) if callable(b) else b
        return call

    async def aclose(self):
        self.closed = True


def issue():
    return issue_from_rally(rally_story())


def client_for(adapter) -> TestClient:
    return TestClient(create_app(adapter))


def test_parse_ids():
    assert parse_ids("") == [] and parse_ids("1, 2,3") == [1, 2, 3]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        parse_ids("1,x")


def test_session_routes_never_echo_the_key():

    adapter = FakeAdapter(session=session, connect=session, disconnect=Session(connected=False, write_guard="demo"))
    with client_for(adapter) as c:
        assert c.get("/api/session").json()["connected"] is True
        r = c.post("/api/session", json={"api_key": "_SECRET123"})
        assert r.status_code == 200 and "_SECRET123" not in r.text
        assert c.delete("/api/session").json()["connected"] is False
    assert ("connect", "_SECRET123") in adapter.calls


def test_rejected_key_is_401_and_not_echoed():

        r = c.post("/api/session", json={"api_key": "_SECRET123"})
    assert r.status_code == 401 and r.json() == {"error": "Rally rejected that key"} and "_SECRET123" not in r.text


def test_reads_pass_parsed_arguments():

        assert c.get("/api/releases?teams=9001").status_code == 200
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `server/app/main.py`**

```python
"""FastAPI app: the domain API in front of the Rally adapter (spec §6)."""
import logging
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from starlette.middleware.trustedhost import TrustedHostMiddleware

from .config import Settings
from .credentials import read_key
from .domain import (Bootstrap, Changes, Comment, CommentCreate, InitiativeDetail, InitiativeList, Issue, IssueCreate,
                     IssueDetail, IssuePage, IssuePatch, Label, ProjectDetail, ProjectList, RankRequest, Release,
                     SearchResult, Session, SessionCreate, SubIssue, SubIssueCreate, SubIssuePatch, TagCreate, TeamLoad,
                     Update, UpdateCreate, User)
from .guard import GuardError, WriteGuard
from .rally.adapter import RallyAdapter
from .rally.client import RallyClient, RallyError

log = logging.getLogger("mira")
OK = {"ok": True}


def parse_ids(text: str) -> list[int]:
    """'1,2,3' → [1, 2, 3]. Anything else is a 422."""
    try:
        return [int(part) for part in text.split(",") if part.strip()]
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError("teams must be a comma-separated list of IDs") from None


def build_adapter(settings: Settings) -> RallyAdapter:
    def make_client(key: str) -> RallyClient:
        return RallyClient(settings.rally_base_url, key, timeout=settings.rally_timeout_s,
                           max_concurrency=settings.rally_max_concurrency)

    key = read_key(settings.mira_credentials) or settings.rally_api_key  # spec D14: the app's key, else .env
    guard = WriteGuard(settings.write_guard, settings.rally_project, settings.demo_prefix, settings.demo_tag)
    return RallyAdapter(make_client(key), settings, guard, client_factory=make_client)


def create_app(adapter=None) -> FastAPI:
    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
        app.state.adapter = adapter or build_adapter(Settings())
        yield
        await app.state.adapter.aclose()

    app = FastAPI(title="Mira proxy", lifespan=lifespan)
    app.add_middleware(TrustedHostMiddleware, allowed_hosts=["127.0.0.1", "localhost", "testserver"])

    @app.exception_handler(RallyError)
    async def rally_error(_: Request, e: RallyError) -> JSONResponse:
        return JSONResponse({"error": e.message}, status_code=e.status)

    @app.exception_handler(GuardError)
    async def guard_error(request: Request, e: GuardError) -> JSONResponse:
        log.warning("write guard blocked %s %s: %s", request.method, request.url.path, e)
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, status_code=403)

    @app.exception_handler(ValueError)
    async def bad_value(_: Request, e: ValueError) -> JSONResponse:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, status_code=422)

    def rally(request: Request) -> RallyAdapter:
        return request.app.state.adapter

    @app.get("/api/health")
    async def health() -> dict:
        return OK

    # ---------- session (spec §6.1) ----------
    @app.get("/api/session", response_model=Session)
    async def get_session(request: Request):
        return await rally(request).session()

    @app.post("/api/session", response_model=Session)
    async def connect(request: Request, body: SessionCreate):
        return await rally(request).connect(body.api_key)

    @app.delete("/api/session", response_model=Session)
    async def disconnect(request: Request):
        return await rally(request).disconnect()

    # ---------- reads (spec §6.2) ----------
    @app.get("/api/bootstrap", response_model=Bootstrap)
    async def bootstrap(request: Request):
        return await rally(request).bootstrap()

    @app.get("/api/teams/{team_id}", response_model=TeamLoad)
    async def team_load(request: Request, team_id: int):
        return await rally(request).team_load(team_id)

    @app.get("/api/teams/{team_id}/issues", response_model=IssuePage)
    async def team_issues(request: Request, team_id: int, cursor: int = 1):
        return await rally(request).team_issues(team_id, cursor)

    @app.get("/api/changes", response_model=Changes)
    async def changes(request: Request, since: str, teams: str = ""):
        return await rally(request).changes(since, parse_ids(teams))

    @app.get("/api/issues/{ref}", response_model=IssueDetail)
    async def get_issue(request: Request, ref: str):
        return await rally(request).get_issue(ref)

    @app.get("/api/search", response_model=SearchResult)
    async def search(request: Request, q: str):
        return await rally(request).search(q)

    @app.get("/api/users", response_model=list[User])
    async def users(request: Request, q: str):
        return await rally(request).users(q)

    @app.get("/api/labels", response_model=list[str])
    async def labels(request: Request, q: str = ""):
        return await rally(request).labels(q)

    @app.get("/api/projects", response_model=ProjectList)
    async def projects(request: Request, teams: str = "", initiative: int | None = None):
        return await rally(request).list_projects(parse_ids(teams), initiative)

    @app.get("/api/projects/{ref}", response_model=ProjectDetail)
    async def project(request: Request, ref: str):
        return await rally(request).project_detail(ref)

    @app.get("/api/initiatives", response_model=InitiativeList)
    async def initiatives(request: Request, teams: str = ""):
        return await rally(request).list_initiatives(parse_ids(teams))

    @app.get("/api/initiatives/{ref}", response_model=InitiativeDetail)
    async def initiative(request: Request, ref: str):
        return await rally(request).initiative_detail(ref)

    @app.get("/api/releases", response_model=list[Release])
    async def releases(request: Request, teams: str = ""):
        return await rally(request).releases(parse_ids(teams))

    # ---------- writes (spec §6.3) ----------
    @app.post("/api/issues", response_model=Issue, status_code=201)
    async def create_issue(request: Request, data: IssueCreate):
        return await rally(request).create_issue(data)

    @app.patch("/api/issues/{issue_id}", response_model=Issue)
    async def update_issue(request: Request, issue_id: int, patch: IssuePatch):
        return await rally(request).update_issue(issue_id, patch)

    @app.post("/api/issues/{issue_id}/rank", response_model=Issue)
    async def rank_issue(request: Request, issue_id: int, req: RankRequest):
        return await rally(request).rank_issue(issue_id, req)

    @app.post("/api/issues/{issue_id}/sub-issues", response_model=SubIssue, status_code=201)
    async def create_sub_issue(request: Request, issue_id: int, data: SubIssueCreate):
        return await rally(request).create_sub_issue(issue_id, data)

    @app.patch("/api/sub-issues/{sub_id}", response_model=SubIssue)
    async def update_sub_issue(request: Request, sub_id: int, patch: SubIssuePatch):
        return await rally(request).update_sub_issue(sub_id, patch)

    @app.delete("/api/sub-issues/{sub_id}")
    async def delete_sub_issue(request: Request, sub_id: int):
        await rally(request).delete_sub_issue(sub_id)
        return OK

    @app.post("/api/issues/{issue_id}/comments", response_model=Comment, status_code=201)
    async def add_comment(request: Request, issue_id: int, data: CommentCreate):
        return await rally(request).add_comment(issue_id, data)

    @app.delete("/api/comments/{post_id}")
    async def delete_comment(request: Request, post_id: int):
        await rally(request).delete_comment(post_id)
        return OK

    @app.post("/api/tags", response_model=Label, status_code=201)
    async def create_tag(request: Request, data: TagCreate):
        return await rally(request).create_tag(data)

    @app.post("/api/projects/{ref}/updates", response_model=Update, status_code=201)
    async def project_update(request: Request, ref: str, data: UpdateCreate):
        return await rally(request).post_update("project", ref, data)

    @app.post("/api/initiatives/{ref}/updates", response_model=Update, status_code=201)
    async def initiative_update(request: Request, ref: str, data: UpdateCreate):
        return await rally(request).post_update("initiative", ref, data)

    return app


app = create_app()
```

`test_health.py` still calls `create_app()` with no adapter. `build_adapter(Settings())` runs in the lifespan and makes no Rally call until a route needs one, so `/api/health` keeps working with no key.

### Task 14: Verify CLI — the guard is always on for checks; `check api` v2

**Files:**
- Modify: `.claude/skills/verify-rally-poc/rallypoc`
- Modify: `.claude/skills/verify-rally-poc/SKILL.md`
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Task 13 API.
- Produces:
  - `rallypoc stack up [--write-guard demo|off]` (default `demo`), and the same flag on `restart`.
  - `require_demo()`, used by `check api`, `check ui` and `browser run`.

- [ ] **Step 1: The guard rule in `stack_up`**

In `SERVICES`, leave the commands as they are. In `stack_up`, pass the mode as an environment variable. Environment variables beat `.env` in pydantic-settings. Change the Popen call and record the mode:

```python
def stack_up(args) -> None:
    d = state_dir(args)
    mode = getattr(args, "write_guard", "demo") or "demo"
    for name, svc in SERVICES.items():
        if pid_of(args, name):
            print(f"{name}: already up")
            continue
        if port_open(svc["port"]):
            raise Usage(f"port {svc['port']} is busy and not ours; run `rallypoc doctor`")
        log_path = d / "logs" / f"{name}.log"
        env = {**os.environ, "WRITE_GUARD": mode} if name == "server" else None
        echo(f"(cd {svc['cwd'].relative_to(ROOT)} && {'WRITE_GUARD=' + mode + ' ' if env else ''}{' '.join(svc['cmd'])}) >> {log_path}")
        with open(log_path, "ab") as log:
            p = subprocess.Popen(svc["cmd"], cwd=svc["cwd"], stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                                 start_new_session=True, env=env)
        (d / "pids" / f"{name}.pid").write_text(str(p.pid))
    for name, svc in SERVICES.items():
        wait_http(svc["health"], 90, name)
    print(f"stack: up (write guard {mode})")
```

In `main()`, add the flag to the `stack` parser:

```python
    s.add_argument("--write-guard", choices=["demo", "off"], default="demo",
                   help="demo (default) limits writes to [DEMO] items; off is for live use only. Checks refuse to run with off")
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `require_demo` before any check that drives the app**

Add `require_demo` after `api_call`:

```python
def require_demo() -> None:
    """Checks must never touch real work (spec §11): refuse unless the running proxy guards writes."""
    status, s = api_call("GET", "/api/session")
    if status != 200 or not isinstance(s, dict):
        raise Usage(f"/api/session answered HTTP {status}; is the stack up?")
    if s.get("write_guard") != "demo":
        raise Usage("the proxy is running with the write guard off; run `rallypoc stack restart` (demo is the default)")
    if not s.get("connected"):
        raise Usage("the proxy isn't connected to Rally: " + str(s.get("error") or "no API key"))
```

The Step 3 `check_api` calls it first. In `cmd_browser`, call it right after the `install` branch:

```python
    if args.action == "install":
        ...
        return
    require_demo()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Replace `check_api` with the v2 checks**

The v1 helpers `DEMO_STATE_STORY` and `_demo_ids` stay, with `_demo_ids` updated to v2 field names. Replace everything from `DEMO_STATE_STORY = ...` through the end of `check_api` with:

```python
DEMO_STORY = "[DEMO] Story editor with HTML round-trip"     # seeded: Defined, in the current sprint
DEMO_BACKLOG = "[DEMO] Backlog drag-to-rank"                 # seeded: Defined, no sprint, Ready off


def _demo_ids(issues: list[dict], prefix: str, tag: str) -> set[int]:
    return {i["id"] for i in issues if i["title"].startswith(prefix) and tag in i["labels"]}


def check_api(args) -> None:
    """Spec §12 live checks. Writes touch only [DEMO] items, with the guard on, and every one is put back."""
    require_demo()
    failed = 0

    def verdict(name: str, ok: bool, evidence="", reason: str = "") -> None:
        nonlocal failed
        record(args, f"api:{name}", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL", evidence, "" if ok else reason)
        failed += not ok

    status, b = api_call("GET", "/api/bootstrap")
    if status != 200:
        raise Fail(f"bootstrap returned HTTP {status}: {b}")
    saved = state_dir(args) / "evidence" / "check-api-bootstrap.json"
    saved.write_text(json.dumps(b, indent=1))
    prefix, tag, demo_team = b["demo_prefix"], b["demo_tag"], b["demo_team_id"]
    demo = _demo_ids(b["issues"], prefix, tag)
    verdict("bootstrap-demo", len(demo) >= 8, saved, f"{len(demo)} [DEMO] issues assigned to you; expected 8 — run `rallypoc seed`")
    others = [t for t in b["teams"] if t["id"] != demo_team]
    verdict("bootstrap-teams", demo_team in {t["id"] for t in b["teams"]} and len(others) >= 1,
            f"{len(b['teams'])} teams", f"your teams: {[t['name'] for t in b['teams']]}")

    if others:  # read-only: a real team loads (spec §12 "Real AI/ML issues load")
        s, load = api_call("GET", f"/api/teams/{others[0]['id']}")
        verdict("team-load-real", s == 200 and len(load.get("issues", [])) > 0,
                f"{others[0]['name']}: {len(load.get('issues', [])) if s == 200 else s} issues", f"HTTP {s}")

    real = next((i for i in b["issues"] if i["id"] not in demo), None)
    if real is None:
        record(args, "api:guard-blocks-real", "SKIP", "", "no non-demo issue is assigned to you")
    else:
        # A no-op patch (same estimate): even if the guard were broken, Rally would see no change.
        s, body = api_call("PATCH", f"/api/issues/{real['id']}", {"estimate": real["estimate"]})
        verdict("guard-blocks-real", s == 403, f"HTTP {s}", f"expected 403, got {s}: {body}")

    s, team = api_call("GET", f"/api/teams/{demo_team}")
    if s != 200:
        raise Fail(f"demo team load returned HTTP {s}: {team}")
    by_title = {i["title"]: i for i in team["issues"]}
    story, backlog = by_title.get(DEMO_STORY), by_title.get(DEMO_BACKLOG)
    if not story or not backlog:
        raise Fail(f"seeded stories missing ({DEMO_STORY!r}, {DEMO_BACKLOG!r}); run `rallypoc seed`")

    def round_trip(name: str, issue: dict, steps: list[dict], restore: dict, check) -> None:
        path = f"/api/issues/{issue['id']}"
        seen: list[str] = []
        ok = True
        try:
            for patch in steps:
                s, got = api_call("PATCH", path, patch)
                good = s == 200 and check(patch, got)
                seen.append(f"{patch}->{got.get('status') if isinstance(got, dict) else s}")
                ok = ok and good
        finally:
            restored = False
            for _ in range(2):  # Rally is shared: always try twice to put it back
                try:
                    s, got = api_call("PATCH", path, restore)
                    restored = s == 200
                except (Usage, httpx.HTTPError):
                    restored = False
                if restored:
                    break
        if not restored:
            verdict(name, False, "", f"left modified: {issue['title']} — restore {restore} failed")
        else:
            verdict(name, ok, "; ".join(seen), "a step returned the wrong value")

    statuses = ["todo", "in_progress", "in_review", "done", "backlog"]
    round_trip("status-round-trip", backlog, [{"status": st} for st in statuses],
               {"status": backlog["status"]}, lambda p, got: got["status"] == p["status"])
    round_trip("backlog-todo-via-ready", backlog, [{"status": "todo"}, {"status": "backlog"}],
               {"status": "backlog"}, lambda p, got: got["status"] == p["status"] and got["cycle_id"] is None)
    round_trip("priority-round-trip", story, [{"priority": 2}, {"priority": 0}],
               {"priority": story["priority"]}, lambda p, got: got["priority"] == p["priority"])

    label = next((lbl for i in team["issues"] + b["issues"] for lbl in i["labels"] if lbl != tag), None)
    if label is None:
        record(args, "api:label-round-trip", "SKIP", "", "no label other than the demo label is in use")
    else:
        round_trip("label-round-trip", story, [{"labels": story["labels"] + [label]}],
                   {"labels": story["labels"]}, lambda p, got: label in got["labels"])

    s, comment = api_call("POST", f"/api/issues/{story['id']}/comments", {"html": "<p>[DEMO] check api comment</p>"})
    if s == 201:
        d, _ = api_call("DELETE", f"/api/comments/{comment['id']}")
        verdict("comment-round-trip", d == 200, f"posted {comment['id']}, deleted HTTP {d}", f"delete HTTP {d}")
    else:
        verdict("comment-round-trip", False, "", f"post HTTP {s}: {comment}")

    s, sub = api_call("POST", f"/api/issues/{story['id']}/sub-issues", {"title": "[DEMO] check api sub-issue", "hours": 1})
    if s == 201:
        d, _ = api_call("DELETE", f"/api/sub-issues/{sub['id']}")
        verdict("sub-issue-round-trip", d == 200, f"created {sub['key']}, deleted HTTP {d}", f"delete HTTP {d}")
    else:
        verdict("sub-issue-round-trip", False, "", f"create HTTP {s}: {sub}")

    s, projects = api_call("GET", f"/api/projects?teams={demo_team}")
    project = next((p for p in projects.get("projects", []) if p["name"].startswith(prefix)), None) if s == 200 else None
    if project is None:
        verdict("update-round-trip", False, "", f"no [DEMO] project in the demo team (HTTP {s})")
    else:
        s, update = api_call("POST", f"/api/projects/{project['key']}/updates", {"health": "at_risk", "html": "<p>[DEMO] check api</p>"})
        shown = None
        try:
            if s == 201:
                _, after = api_call("GET", f"/api/projects?teams={demo_team}")
                shown = next((p["health"] for p in after["projects"] if p["id"] == project["id"]), None)
        finally:
            if s == 201:
                api_call("DELETE", f"/api/comments/{update['id']}")
        ok = s == 201 and isinstance(shown, dict) and shown.get("value") == "at_risk" and shown.get("source") == "update"
        verdict("update-round-trip", ok, f"health after posting: {shown}", f"post HTTP {s}: {update}")

    team_backlog = sorted((i for i in team["issues"] if i["status"] == "backlog"), key=lambda i: i["rank"])
    demo_backlog = [i for i in team_backlog if i["id"] in _demo_ids(team["issues"], prefix, tag)]
    if len(demo_backlog) < 2:
        verdict("rank-round-trip", False, "", "need two [DEMO] backlog stories; run `rallypoc seed`")
    else:
        first, last = demo_backlog[0], demo_backlog[-1]
        everything = sorted(team["issues"], key=lambda i: i["rank"])
        neighbor = everything[everything.index(last) - 1]
        s1 = s2 = None
        moved = restored = False
        try:
            s1, got = api_call("POST", f"/api/issues/{last['id']}/rank", {"above": first["id"]})
            moved = s1 == 200 and got["rank"] < first["rank"]
        finally:
            try:
                s2, back = api_call("POST", f"/api/issues/{last['id']}/rank", {"below": neighbor["id"]})
                restored = s2 == 200 and back["rank"] > neighbor["rank"]
            except (Usage, httpx.HTTPError):
                restored = False
        if not restored:
            verdict("rank-round-trip", False, "", f"left reordered: {last['key']} may not be back below {neighbor['key']}")
        else:
            verdict("rank-round-trip", moved, f"moved={moved} restored={restored}", f"HTTP {s1}/{s2}")
    if failed:
        raise Fail(f"{failed} API check(s) failed")
```

### Task 15: Rank midpoint

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/store/rank.ts`, `web/src/store/rank.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `rankBetween(lo: string | null, hi: string | null): string | null` and `byRank(a: {rank: string; id: number}, b): number`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (`web/src/store/rank.test.ts`)

```ts
import { expect, test } from "vitest";
import { byRank, rankBetween } from "./rank";

const between = (lo: string | null, hi: string | null, r: string | null) =>
  r !== null && (lo === null || lo < r) && (hi === null || r < hi) && [...r].every((c) => c >= " " && c <= "~");

test("a midpoint lands strictly between its neighbours", () => {
  expect(rankBetween("a", "c")).toBe("b");
  expect(between("a", "b", rankBetween("a", "b"))).toBe(true);
  expect(between("a", "a!", rankBetween("a", "a!"))).toBe(true);   // adjacent strings
  expect(between("O~~~", "O~~~~", rankBetween("O~~~", "O~~~~"))).toBe(true);
  expect(rankBetween(null, null)).toBe("O");
  expect(between("O~~", null, rankBetween("O~~", null))).toBe(true); // last place
  expect(between(null, "!", rankBetween(null, "!"))).toBe(true);     // first place
})

test("impossible requests return null", () => {
  expect(rankBetween("b", "a")).toBeNull();
  expect(rankBetween("a", "a")).toBeNull();
  expect(rankBetween(null, " ")).toBeNull(); // nothing printable sorts before a lone space
});

test("random neighbours always get a rank between them", () => {
  let seed = 7;
  const rand = (n: number) => ((seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) % 2 ** 31) % n);
  const word = () => Array.from({ length: 1 + rand(12) }, () => String.fromCharCode(33 + rand(94))).join("");
  for (let n = 0; n < 3000; n++) {
    const [lo, hi] = [word(), word()].sort();
    if (lo === hi) continue;
    expect(between(lo, hi, rankBetween(lo, hi)), `${lo} | ${hi}`).toBe(true);
    expect(between(lo, null, rankBetween(lo, null))).toBe(true);
    expect(between(null, hi, rankBetween(null, hi))).toBe(true);
  }
});

test("byRank sorts by code point, then id", () => {
  const xs = [{ id: 3, rank: "b" }, { id: 2, rank: "a" }, { id: 1, rank: "b" }, { id: 4, rank: "B" }];
  expect(xs.sort(byRank).map((x) => x.id)).toEqual([4, 2, 1, 3]);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to verify they fail**

Run: `cd web && npx vitest --run src/store/rank.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `web/src/store/rank.ts`**

```ts
/** Rally's DragAndDropRank strings: printable ASCII, ordered by code point (spec D11, verified on real data). */
const MIN = 32; // " "
const MAX = 126; // "~"

/**
 * A rank strictly between `lo` and `hi` (null means no bound), for placing a dropped issue before the server
 * answers. Returns null when no printable string fits; the caller then waits for the server's rank.
 */
export function rankBetween(lo: string | null, hi: string | null): string | null {
  if (lo !== null && hi !== null && lo >= hi) return null;
  let out = "";
  let onLo = lo !== null; // out is still equal to lo's prefix
  let onHi = hi !== null; // out is still equal to hi's prefix
  for (let i = 0; i < 512; i++) {
    if (onHi && i >= hi!.length) return null; // out would equal hi: no room below it on this path
    const a = onLo && i < lo!.length ? lo!.charCodeAt(i) : MIN - 1;
    const b = onHi ? hi!.charCodeAt(i) : MAX + 1;
    if (b - a >= 2) return out + String.fromCharCode(Math.floor((a + b) / 2));
    if (a >= MIN) {
      out += String.fromCharCode(a); // keep lo's character and look one place deeper
      onHi = onHi && a === b;
    } else {
      out += String.fromCharCode(b); // lo has ended and hi's character is the lowest printable one
      onLo = false;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function byRank(a: { rank: string; id: number }, b: { rank: string; id: number }): number {
  if (a.rank < b.rank) return -1;
  if (a.rank > b.rank) return 1;
  return a.id - b.id;
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from "vitest";
import { formatRoute, HOME, parsePath, sameRoute, type Route } from "./route";

test("every route round-trips through its URL", () => {
  const routes: Route[] = [
    { name: "my", tab: "assigned" }, { name: "my", tab: "created" },
    { name: "team", teamId: 9001, tab: "active" }, { name: "team", teamId: 9001, tab: "backlog" },
    { name: "team", teamId: 9001, tab: "all" }, { name: "team", teamId: 9001, tab: "cycles" },
    { name: "team", teamId: 9001, tab: "projects" }, { name: "issue", key: "US1001" },
    { name: "projects" }, { name: "initiatives" }, { name: "settings" },
  ];

});

test("the root, a bare team and lowercase keys normalise", () => {

  expect(parsePath("/team/9001")).toEqual({ name: "team", teamId: 9001, tab: "active" });
  expect(parsePath("/issue/us1001")).toEqual({ name: "issue", key: "US1001" });
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `web/src/route.ts`**

```ts
/** Every screen has a URL (spec D18). History routing; the dev server serves index.html for any path. */
export type MyTab = "assigned" | "created";
export type IssueTab = "active" | "backlog" | "all";
export type TeamTab = IssueTab | "cycles" | "projects";
export type Route =
  | { name: "my"; tab: MyTab }
  | { name: "team"; teamId: number; tab: TeamTab }
  | { name: "issue"; key: string }
  | { name: "projects" }
  | { name: "initiatives" }
  | { name: "settings" }
  | { name: "not-found"; path: string };

export const HOME: Route = { name: "my", tab: "assigned" };
const TEAM_TABS: TeamTab[] = ["active", "backlog", "all", "cycles", "projects"];
const KEY = /^[A-Za-z]{1,4}\d+$/;

export function parsePath(path: string): Route {
  const parts = path.split("?")[0].split("/").filter(Boolean);
  if (parts.length === 0) return HOME;
  const [head, a, b] = parts;
  if (head === "my" && parts.length === 2 && (a === "assigned" || a === "created")) return { name: "my", tab: a };
  if (head === "team" && /^\d+$/.test(a ?? "") && parts.length <= 3) {
    const tab = (b ?? "active") as TeamTab;
    if (TEAM_TABS.includes(tab)) return { name: "team", teamId: Number(a), tab };
  }
  if (head === "issue" && parts.length === 2 && KEY.test(a)) return { name: "issue", key: a.toUpperCase() };
  if (parts.length === 1 && (head === "projects" || head === "initiatives" || head === "settings")) return { name: head };
  return { name: "not-found", path };
}

export function formatRoute(r: Route): string {
  switch (r.name) {
    case "my": return `/my/${r.tab}`;
    case "team": return `/team/${r.teamId}/${r.tab}`;
    case "issue": return `/issue/${r.key}`;
    case "not-found": return r.path;
    default: return `/${r.name}`;
  }
}

export const sameRoute = (a: Route, b: Route): boolean => formatRoute(a) === formatRoute(b);
```

```ts
// Mirrors server/app/domain.py. Rally shapes never reach the client (spec D2); every word here is Linear's.
export type Status = "backlog" | "todo" | "in_progress" | "in_review" | "done";
export const STATUSES: Status[] = ["backlog", "todo", "in_progress", "in_review", "done"];
export const STATUS_LABEL: Record<Status, string> = {
  backlog: "Backlog", todo: "Todo", in_progress: "In Progress", in_review: "In Review", done: "Done",
};
export type SubStatus = "todo" | "in_progress" | "done";
export const SUB_STATUS_LABEL: Record<SubStatus, string> = { todo: "Todo", in_progress: "In Progress", done: "Done" };
/** Linear's numbers: 0 no priority, 1 urgent … 4 low. */
export type Priority = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
export const PRIORITIES: Priority[] = [1, 2, 3, 4, 0];
export const PRIORITY_LABEL: Record<Priority, string> = { 0: "No priority", 1: "Urgent", 2: "High", 3: "Medium", 4: "Low" };
export type HealthValue = "on_track" | "at_risk" | "off_track" | "no_target";
export type GuardMode = "demo" | "off";

export interface User { id: number; name: string }
export interface Workspace { id: number; name: string }
export interface Team { id: number; name: string; level: "team" | "art"; parent_id: number | null }
export interface Cycle { id: number; team_id: number; name: string; start: string; end: string }
export interface Health { value: HealthValue; source: "update" | "auto"; reason: string }
export interface Update {
  id: number; author: User | null; health: Exclude<HealthValue, "no_target">; html: string; created_at: string;
  progress: number | null; target: string | null;
}
export interface Project {
  id: number; key: string; name: string; status_name: string; status_category: "backlog" | "planned" | "started" | "completed";
  lead: User | null; start: string | null; target: string | null; progress: number; issue_count: number; done_count: number;
  points: number; done_points: number; pi_id: number | null; initiative_id: number | null; team_id: number; risk: boolean;
  updated_at: string; health: Health | null; latest_update: Update | null;
}

export interface Issue {
  id: number;
  key: string;
  kind: "story" | "defect";
  title: string;
  status: Status;
  priority: Priority;
  assignee: User | null;
  creator: User | null;
  team_id: number;
  cycle_id: number | null;
  project_id: number | null;
  estimate: number | null;
  labels: string[];
  blocked: boolean;
  blocked_reason: string;
  description: string;
  rank: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  completed_at: string | null;
  sub_issue_count: number;
}

export interface SubIssue {
  id: number; key: string; title: string; status: SubStatus; assignee: User | null; issue_id: number; team_id: number;
  hours: number | null; index: number; updated_at: string;
}
export interface Comment { id: number; author: User | null; html: string; created_at: string }
export interface Activity { id: number; author: User | null; text: string; created_at: string }

export interface Session { connected: boolean; me: User | null; workspace: Workspace | null; write_guard: GuardMode; error: string | null }

export interface Bootstrap {
  me: User; workspace: Workspace; write_guard: GuardMode; demo_prefix: string; demo_tag: string; demo_team_id: number | null;
  teams: Team[]; issues: Issue[]; sub_issues: SubIssue[]; cycles: Cycle[]; projects: Project[]; users: User[]; server_time: string;
}
export interface TeamLoad {
  team: Team; issues: Issue[]; cycles: Cycle[]; projects: Project[]; users: User[]; member_ids: number[]; server_time: string;
}
export interface IssuePage { issues: Issue[]; next_cursor: number | null; cycles: Cycle[]; projects: Project[]; users: User[] }
export interface Changes {
  issues: Issue[]; sub_issues: SubIssue[]; cycles: Cycle[]; projects: Project[]; users: User[]; server_time: string;
}
export interface IssueDetail {
  issue: Issue; sub_issues: SubIssue[]; comments: Comment[]; activity: Activity[]; cycles: Cycle[]; projects: Project[]; users: User[];
}
export interface SearchResult { issues: Issue[]; teams: Team[]; projects: Project[] }

export interface IssuePatch {
  title?: string;
  status?: Status;
  priority?: Priority;
  assignee_id?: number | null;
  cycle_id?: number | null;
  project_id?: number;
  estimate?: number | null;
  labels?: string[];
  blocked?: boolean;
  blocked_reason?: string;
  description?: string;
}
export interface IssueCreate {
  team_id: number; title: string; project_id: number; status?: Status; priority?: Priority; assignee_id?: number | null;
  labels?: string[]; cycle_id?: number | null; estimate?: number | null; description?: string; position?: "top" | "bottom";
}
export interface SubIssuePatch { title?: string; status?: SubStatus; assignee_id?: number | null; hours?: number | null }
export type RankRequest = { above: number } | { below: number };
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `web/src/api/client.ts`**

```ts
import type {
  Bootstrap, Changes, Issue, IssueCreate, IssueDetail, IssuePage, IssuePatch, RankRequest, SearchResult, Session, SubIssue,
  SubIssuePatch, TeamLoad, User,
} from "./types";

export const OFFLINE = "Local server isn't running";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(public status: number, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}

export interface Api {
  session(): Promise<Session>;
  bootstrap(): Promise<Bootstrap>;
  team(id: number): Promise<TeamLoad>;
  teamIssues(id: number, cursor: number): Promise<IssuePage>;
  changes(since: string, teams: number[]): Promise<Changes>;
  issue(ref: string | number): Promise<IssueDetail>;
  patchIssue(id: number, patch: IssuePatch): Promise<Issue>;
  createIssue(data: IssueCreate): Promise<Issue>;
  rankIssue(id: number, req: RankRequest): Promise<Issue>;
  patchSubIssue(id: number, patch: SubIssuePatch): Promise<SubIssue>;
  search(q: string): Promise<SearchResult>;
  users(q: string): Promise<User[]>;
  labels(q: string): Promise<string[]>;
}

export function errorFrom(status: number, data: unknown): ApiError {
  const body = (data ?? {}) as { error?: unknown; detail?: unknown };
  if (typeof body.error === "string") return new ApiError(status, body.error);
  if (Array.isArray(body.detail)) {
    return new ApiError(status, body.detail.map((d: { msg?: string }) => d.msg ?? "invalid").join("; "));
  }
  if (typeof body.detail === "string") return new ApiError(status, body.detail);
  // Vite's dev proxy answers 5xx with no JSON body when the FastAPI process is down.
  if (status >= 500) return new ApiError(0, OFFLINE);
  return new ApiError(status, `Request failed (${status})`);
}

async function request<T>(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<T> {
  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await fetch(`/api${path}`, {
      method,
      headers: body === undefined ? undefined : { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError(0, OFFLINE);
  }
  const data: unknown = await res.json().catch(() => null);
  if (res.ok) return data as T;
  throw errorFrom(res.status, data);
}

const q = encodeURIComponent;

export const api: Api = {
  session: () => request("GET", "/session"),
  bootstrap: () => request("GET", "/bootstrap"),
  team: (id) => request("GET", `/teams/${id}`),
  teamIssues: (id, cursor) => request("GET", `/teams/${id}/issues?cursor=${cursor}`),
  changes: (since, teams) => request("GET", `/changes?since=${q(since)}&teams=${teams.join(",")}`),
  issue: (ref) => request("GET", `/issues/${q(String(ref))}`),
  patchIssue: (id, patch) => request("PATCH", `/issues/${id}`, patch),
  createIssue: (data) => request("POST", "/issues", data),
  rankIssue: (id, req) => request("POST", `/issues/${id}/rank`, req),
  patchSubIssue: (id, patch) => request("PATCH", `/sub-issues/${id}`, patch),
  search: (text) => request("GET", `/search?q=${q(text)}`),
  users: (text) => request("GET", `/users?q=${q(text)}`),
  labels: (text) => request("GET", `/labels?q=${q(text)}`),
};
```

```ts
                  status: "todo", priority: 1, rank: "N~~~" }),

    ],

    cycles: [SPRINT3, SPRINT4, SPRINT3_AIML],
    projects: [makeProject(), makeProject({ id: 202, key: "F202", name: "Real project", team_id: 9002 })],

export function makeTeamLoad(over: Partial<TeamLoad> = {}): TeamLoad {

  return {
    team: DEMO_TEAM,
    issues: [
      ...b.issues.filter((i) => i.team_id === 9001),
      makeIssue({ id: 1401, key: "US1401", title: "[DEMO] Teammate's issue", assignee: SAM, creator: SAM, status: "todo", rank: "R~~~" }),
    ],

    server_time: "2026-10-16T11:59:30.000Z", ...over,
  };
}

export const EMPTY_CHANGES: Changes = {
  issues: [], sub_issues: [], cycles: [], projects: [], users: [], server_time: "2026-10-16T12:00:30.000Z",
};

export function fakeApi(): Mocked<Api> {
  const notStubbed = (name: string) => vi.fn(() => Promise.reject(new Error(`${name} not stubbed`)));
  const names: (keyof Api)[] = ["session", "bootstrap", "team", "teamIssues", "changes", "issue", "patchIssue", "createIssue",
    "rankIssue", "patchSubIssue", "search", "users", "labels"];
  return Object.fromEntries(names.map((n) => [n, notStubbed(n)])) as unknown as Mocked<Api>;
}

export function deferred<T>() {
  let resolve!: (value: T) => void;
  let reject!: (reason: unknown) => void;
  const promise = new Promise<T>((res, rej) => {
    resolve = res;
    reject = rej;
  });
  return { promise, resolve, reject };
}

export async function readyStore(api: Mocked<Api> = fakeApi(), bootstrap: Bootstrap = makeBootstrap()) {
  api.bootstrap.mockResolvedValueOnce(bootstrap);
  const store = createAppStore(api);
  await store.getState().load();
  return { store, api };
}

type Route = unknown | [number, unknown];

/** Stub global fetch: keys are "METHOD /api/path" (query string ignored); values are a body or [status, body]. */
export function serve(routes: Record<string, Route>) {
  const fetchMock = vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const key = `${init?.method ?? "GET"} ${String(input).split("?")[0]}`;
    if (!(key in routes)) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: `unrouted ${key}` }), { status: 404 });
    const route = routes[key];
    const [status, body] = Array.isArray(route) && typeof route[0] === "number" ? route : [200, route];
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status: status as number });
  });
  vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetchMock);
  return fetchMock;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `web/src/store/patch.ts`**

```ts
import type { Issue, IssuePatch, SubIssue, SubIssuePatch, User } from "../api/types";

/** An edit as the store applies it: the API patch, or a rank the store computed for a drop. */
export type LocalIssuePatch = IssuePatch & { rank?: string };

const userFor = (id: number | null | undefined, users: Record<number, User>): User | null =>
  id == null ? null : (users[id] ?? { id, name: "" });

/** The optimistic copy of an edit. It follows the server's status/cycle rules (spec §5.2). */
export function applyIssuePatch(issue: Issue, patch: LocalIssuePatch, users: Record<number, User>): Issue {
  const next: Issue = { ...issue };
  if (patch.title !== undefined) next.title = patch.title;
  if (patch.priority !== undefined) next.priority = patch.priority;
  if ("assignee_id" in patch) next.assignee = userFor(patch.assignee_id, users);
  if (patch.project_id !== undefined) next.project_id = patch.project_id;
  if ("estimate" in patch) next.estimate = patch.estimate ?? null;
  if (patch.labels !== undefined) next.labels = patch.labels;
  if (patch.blocked !== undefined) next.blocked = patch.blocked;
  if (patch.blocked_reason !== undefined) next.blocked_reason = patch.blocked_reason;
  if (patch.description !== undefined) next.description = patch.description;
  if (patch.rank !== undefined) next.rank = patch.rank;
  if ("cycle_id" in patch) {
    next.cycle_id = patch.cycle_id ?? null;
    if (next.cycle_id !== null && next.status === "backlog") next.status = "todo"; // in a cycle, Defined reads as Todo
  }
  if (patch.status !== undefined) {
    next.status = patch.status;
    if (patch.status === "backlog") next.cycle_id = null;
    next.completed_at = patch.status === "done" ? (issue.completed_at ?? new Date().toISOString()) : null;
  }
  return next;
}

/** The issue fields each patch key can change, side effects included. */
const ISSUE_FIELDS: Record<keyof LocalIssuePatch, (keyof Issue)[]> = {
  title: ["title"], status: ["status", "cycle_id", "completed_at"], priority: ["priority"], assignee_id: ["assignee"],
  cycle_id: ["cycle_id", "status"], project_id: ["project_id"], estimate: ["estimate"], labels: ["labels"],
  blocked: ["blocked"], blocked_reason: ["blocked_reason"], description: ["description"], rank: ["rank"],
};

/** Put back only the fields this patch touched, so a later in-flight edit keeps its optimistic value. */
export function revertIssueFields(current: Issue, before: Issue, patch: LocalIssuePatch): Issue {
  const next = { ...current } as Record<keyof Issue, unknown>;
  for (const key of Object.keys(patch) as (keyof LocalIssuePatch)[]) {
    for (const field of ISSUE_FIELDS[key]) next[field] = before[field];
  }
  return next as unknown as Issue;
}

export function applySubIssuePatch(sub: SubIssue, patch: SubIssuePatch, users: Record<number, User>): SubIssue {
  const next: SubIssue = { ...sub };
  if (patch.title !== undefined) next.title = patch.title;
  if (patch.status !== undefined) next.status = patch.status;
  if ("assignee_id" in patch) next.assignee = userFor(patch.assignee_id, users);
  if ("hours" in patch) next.hours = patch.hours ?? null;
  return next;
}

const SUB_FIELDS: Record<keyof SubIssuePatch, keyof SubIssue> = { title: "title", status: "status", assignee_id: "assignee", hours: "hours" };

export function revertSubIssueFields(current: SubIssue, before: SubIssue, patch: SubIssuePatch): SubIssue {
  const next = { ...current } as Record<keyof SubIssue, unknown>;
  for (const key of Object.keys(patch) as (keyof SubIssuePatch)[]) next[SUB_FIELDS[key]] = before[SUB_FIELDS[key]];
  return next as unknown as SubIssue;
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/client";
import type { Issue } from "../api/types";
import { DANA, deferred, EMPTY_CHANGES, fakeApi, makeBootstrap, makeIssue, makeTeamLoad, readyStore } from "../test/fixtures";
import { createAppStore } from "./store";

const tick = () => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));

test("load maps the bootstrap", async () => {
  const { store } = await readyStore();
  const s = store.getState();
  expect(s.status).toBe("ready");
  expect(s.meta).toMatchObject({ me: DANA, writeGuard: "demo", demoTeamId: 9001, workspace: { name: "Demo Workspace" } });
  expect(s.yourTeamIds).toEqual([9001, 9002]);
  expect(Object.keys(s.issues).map(Number).sort()).toEqual([1001, 1002, 1201, 1300]);
  expect(s.subIssues[4002].issue_id).toBe(1300);
  expect(s.cycles[401].team_id).toBe(9002);
  expect(s.lastSync).toBe("2026-10-16T11:59:30.000Z");
});

test.each([[401, "needs-key"], [0, "offline"], [500, "error"]] as const)("a %i on load means %s", async (status, expected) => {
  const api = fakeApi();
  api.bootstrap.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(status, "nope"));
  const store = createAppStore(api);
  await store.getState().load();
  expect(store.getState().status).toBe(expected);
});

test("an edit shows at once and the server copy then replaces it", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  const reply = deferred<Issue>();
  api.patchIssue.mockReturnValueOnce(reply.promise);
  const done = store.getState().updateIssue(1001, { priority: 1 });
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001].priority).toBe(1);
  reply.resolve(makeIssue({ priority: 1, updated_at: "2026-10-16T12:01:00.000Z" }));
  await done;
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001].updated_at).toBe("2026-10-16T12:01:00.000Z");
});

test("a failed edit rolls back only its fields and offers retry only when Rally was unreachable", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.patchIssue.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(502, "Rally returned HTTP 503"));
  await store.getState().updateIssue(1001, { priority: 4, title: "[DEMO] Renamed" });
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001]).toMatchObject({ priority: 2, title: "[DEMO] Example story" });
  expect(store.getState().toasts[0].message).toBe("Rally returned HTTP 503");
  expect(store.getState().toasts[0].retry).toBeTypeOf("function");
  api.patchIssue.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(403, "Demo mode: only [DEMO] items can be edited"));
  await store.getState().updateIssue(1201, { priority: 4 });
  expect(store.getState().toasts[1].retry).toBeUndefined();
});

test("two edits of one field: failures restore the value from before the first", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  const first = deferred<Issue>();
  const second = deferred<Issue>();
  api.patchIssue.mockReturnValueOnce(first.promise).mockReturnValueOnce(second.promise);
  const a = store.getState().updateIssue(1001, { priority: 1 });
  const b = store.getState().updateIssue(1001, { priority: 3 });
  first.reject(new ApiError(422, "no"));
  await a;
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001].priority).toBe(3); // the later edit still shows
  second.reject(new ApiError(422, "no"));
  await b;
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001].priority).toBe(2); // the true original
});

test("Backlog clears the cycle, a cycle makes a Backlog issue Todo, and a failure restores both", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  const reply = deferred<Issue>();
  api.patchIssue.mockReturnValueOnce(reply.promise).mockReturnValue(new Promise(() => {}));
  const backlog = store.getState().updateIssue(1001, { status: "backlog" });
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001]).toMatchObject({ status: "backlog", cycle_id: null });
  void store.getState().updateIssue(1002, { cycle_id: 301 });
  expect(store.getState().issues[1002]).toMatchObject({ status: "todo", cycle_id: 301 });
  reply.reject(new ApiError(422, "no"));
  await backlog;
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001]).toMatchObject({ status: "in_progress", cycle_id: 301 });
});

test("bulk edits show at once and reach Rally one at a time", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  const first = deferred<Issue>();
  api.patchIssue.mockReturnValueOnce(first.promise).mockResolvedValueOnce(makeIssue({ id: 1002, key: "US1002", priority: 1 }));
  const all = store.getState().updateIssues([1001, 1002], { priority: 1 });
  expect(store.getState().issues[1002].priority).toBe(1);
  await tick();
  expect(api.patchIssue).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  first.resolve(makeIssue({ priority: 1 }));
  await all;
  expect(api.patchIssue).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
});

test("a rank drop places the issue between its new neighbours at once; the server rank replaces it", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore(); // ranks: 1201 N~~~, 1001 O~~~, 1002 P~~~, 1300 Q~~~
  const reply = deferred<Issue>();
  api.rankIssue.mockReturnValueOnce(reply.promise);
  const done = store.getState().rankIssue(1300, { above: 1001 });
  const r = store.getState().issues[1300].rank;
  expect(r > "N~~~" && r < "O~~~").toBe(true);
  reply.resolve(makeIssue({ id: 1300, key: "US1300", rank: "Nz" }));
  await done;
  expect(store.getState().issues[1300].rank).toBe("Nz");
});

test("two quick drags where the first fails: the issue stays where the last drop put it", async () => {
  // Review focus 3.
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  const first = deferred<Issue>();
  const second = deferred<Issue>();
  api.rankIssue.mockReturnValueOnce(first.promise).mockReturnValueOnce(second.promise);
  const a = store.getState().rankIssue(1300, { above: 1001 });
  const b = store.getState().rankIssue(1300, { below: 1002 });
  const latest = store.getState().issues[1300].rank;
  expect(latest > "P~~~").toBe(true);
  first.reject(new ApiError(502, "Rally returned HTTP 503"));
  await a;
  expect(store.getState().issues[1300].rank).toBe(latest);
  second.resolve(makeIssue({ id: 1300, key: "US1300", rank: "Pz" }));
  await b;
  expect(store.getState().issues[1300].rank).toBe("Pz");
});

test("loading a team merges its issues, cycles, projects and people", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.team.mockResolvedValueOnce(makeTeamLoad());
  const p = store.getState().loadTeam(9001);
  expect(store.getState().teamState[9001].status).toBe("loading");
  await p;
  expect(store.getState().teamState[9001]).toMatchObject({ status: "ready", memberIds: [501, 502] });
  expect(store.getState().issues[1401].title).toBe("[DEMO] Teammate's issue");
  expect(store.getState().loadedTeamIds()).toEqual([9001]);
});

test("a team refresh keeps an issue that is mid-edit, even when the server no longer lists it", async () => {
  // Review focus 4.
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.patchIssue.mockReturnValue(new Promise(() => {}));
  void store.getState().updateIssue(1001, { priority: 1 });
  api.team.mockResolvedValueOnce(makeTeamLoad({ issues: [] }));
  await store.getState().loadTeam(9001);
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001].priority).toBe(1);
  expect(store.getState().issues[1002]).toBeUndefined(); // open, not mid-edit, and gone from its team: dropped
  expect(store.getState().issues[1201]).toBeDefined();   // another team's issue is untouched
});

test("a poll that began before an edit settled doesn't overwrite it", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  const stamp = store.getState().stamp();
  api.patchIssue.mockResolvedValueOnce(makeIssue({ priority: 1 }));
  await store.getState().updateIssue(1001, { priority: 1 });
  store.getState().applyChanges({ ...EMPTY_CHANGES, issues: [makeIssue({ priority: 2 })] }, stamp);
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001].priority).toBe(1);
  store.getState().applyChanges({ ...EMPTY_CHANGES, issues: [makeIssue({ priority: 3 })] }, store.getState().stamp());
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001].priority).toBe(3);
  expect(store.getState().lastSync).toBe("2026-10-16T12:00:30.000Z");
});

test("the All tab pages until the server says there are no more", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  const page = { cycles: [], projects: [], users: [] };
  api.teamIssues
    .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ...page, issues: [makeIssue({ id: 1500, key: "US1500", status: "done" })], next_cursor: 101 })
    .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ...page, issues: [], next_cursor: null });
  await store.getState().loadTeamPage(9001);
  await store.getState().loadTeamPage(9001);
  await store.getState().loadTeamPage(9001);
  expect(api.teamIssues.mock.calls).toEqual([[9001, 1], [9001, 101]]);
  expect(store.getState().issues[1500]).toBeDefined();
});

test("opening an unknown issue reports not-found without a toast", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.issue.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(404, "Issue US999999 was not found"));
  expect(await store.getState().loadIssueDetail("US999999")).toBe("not-found");
  expect(store.getState().toasts).toEqual([]);
});

test("the 5-minute refresh drops issues that are no longer yours", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.bootstrap.mockResolvedValueOnce(makeBootstrap({ issues: [makeIssue()] }));
  await store.getState().refresh();
  expect(store.getState().issues[1002]).toBeUndefined();
  expect(store.getState().issues[1201]).toBeUndefined();
  expect(store.getState().issues[1300]).toBeDefined(); // Sam's: a sub-issue's parent, not "mine", so kept
});

test("sub-issue edits are optimistic and roll back", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.patchSubIssue.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(422, "no"));
  const p = store.getState().updateSubIssue(4002, { status: "done" });
  expect(store.getState().subIssues[4002].status).toBe("done");
  await p;
  expect(store.getState().subIssues[4002].status).toBe("todo");
});

test("remote search keeps local copies and caches teams; people search caches users", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.search.mockResolvedValueOnce({ issues: [makeIssue({ title: "stale" })], teams: [{ id: 9050, name: "DataLine", level: "team", parent_id: null }], projects: [] });
  await store.getState().searchRemote("example");
  expect(store.getState().issues[1001].title).toBe("[DEMO] Example story");
  expect(store.getState().teams[9050].name).toBe("DataLine");
  api.users.mockResolvedValueOnce([{ id: 777, name: "Pat Person" }]);
  await store.getState().searchUsers("pat");
  expect(store.getState().users[777].name).toBe("Pat Person");
});
```
