- [ ] **Step 8: Write `web/src/store/store.ts`**

```ts
import { createStore, type StoreApi } from "zustand/vanilla";
import { ApiError, type Api } from "../api/client";
import type {
  Bootstrap, Changes, Cycle, Issue, IssueCreate, IssuePatch, Project, RankRequest, SearchResult, SubIssue, SubIssuePatch,
  Team, User, Workspace,
} from "../api/types";
import { applyIssuePatch, applySubIssuePatch, revertIssueFields, revertSubIssueFields, type LocalIssuePatch } from "./patch";
import { byRank, rankBetween } from "./rank";

export type Status = "loading" | "ready" | "needs-key" | "offline" | "error";
export interface Toast { id: number; message: string; retry?: () => void }
export interface Meta {
  me: User;
  workspace: Workspace;
  writeGuard: "demo" | "off";
  demoPrefix: string;
  demoTag: string;
  demoTeamId: number | null;
}
/** Load state per team (spec §7). `nextCursor` is undefined until the All tab asks, and null once every page is in. */
export interface TeamState { status: "loading" | "ready" | "error"; memberIds: number[]; nextCursor?: number | null }
export type DetailResult = number | "not-found" | "error";

export interface AppState {
  status: Status;
  error: string | null;
  meta: Meta | null;
  /** Teams Rally says are yours (spec D6). The sidebar adds joined and removes hidden ones. */
  yourTeamIds: number[];
  users: Record<number, User>;
  teams: Record<number, Team>;
  cycles: Record<number, Cycle>;
  projects: Record<number, Project>;
  issues: Record<number, Issue>;
  subIssues: Record<number, SubIssue>;
  teamState: Record<number, TeamState>;
  lastSync: string | null;
  stale: boolean;
  toasts: Toast[];
  /** In-flight write counts per "issue:<id>" and "sub:<id>". */
  pending: Record<string, number>;
  load(): Promise<void>;
  /** The 5-minute full refresh (spec §6.4): re-bootstrap, then reload every loaded team. Throws if Rally fails. */
  refresh(): Promise<void>;
  loadTeam(id: number): Promise<void>;
  loadTeamPage(id: number): Promise<void>;
  loadedTeamIds(): number[];
  /** `readStamp` is `stamp()` taken when the poll began; items written since then are left alone. */
  applyChanges(changes: Changes, readStamp?: number): void;
  stamp(): number;
  updateIssue(id: number, patch: IssuePatch): Promise<void>;
  updateIssues(ids: number[], patch: IssuePatch): Promise<void>;
  /** Several issues, each with its own patch: all show at once, and the writes reach Rally one at a time. */
  updateMany(changes: { id: number; patch: IssuePatch }[]): Promise<void>;
  rankIssue(id: number, req: RankRequest): Promise<void>;
  createIssue(data: IssueCreate): Promise<Issue | null>;
  loadIssueDetail(ref: string | number): Promise<DetailResult>;
  searchRemote(q: string): Promise<SearchResult>;
  searchUsers(q: string): Promise<User[]>;
  searchLabels(q: string): Promise<string[]>;
  updateSubIssue(id: number, patch: SubIssuePatch): Promise<void>;
  pushToast(message: string, retry?: () => void): void;
  dismissToast(id: number): void;
  setStale(stale: boolean): void;
}

export type AppStore = StoreApi<AppState>;

export function asApiError(e: unknown): ApiError {
  return e instanceof ApiError ? e : new ApiError(-1, e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
}

const byId = <T extends { id: number }>(xs: T[]): Record<number, T> => Object.fromEntries(xs.map((x) => [x.id, x]));
const merge = <T extends { id: number }>(into: Record<number, T>, xs: T[] = []): Record<number, T> =>
  xs.length ? { ...into, ...byId(xs) } : into;
const issueKey = (id: number) => `issue:${id}`;
const subKey = (id: number) => `sub:${id}`;

const metaFrom = (b: Bootstrap): Meta => ({
  me: b.me, workspace: b.workspace, writeGuard: b.write_guard, demoPrefix: b.demo_prefix, demoTag: b.demo_tag,
  demoTeamId: b.demo_team_id,
});

export function createAppStore(api: Api): AppStore {
  const queues = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
  interface Entry<T, P> { patch: P; before: T }
  const entries = new Map<string, Entry<never, never>[]>();
  let toastSeq = 0;
  /** A logical clock ticked whenever a write starts or finishes, so a read can tell it predates a write. */
  let clock = 0;
  const touched = new Map<string, number>();

  return createStore<AppState>()((set, get) => {
    const bump = (key: string, delta: number) =>
      set((s) => {
        touched.set(key, ++clock);
        const n = (s.pending[key] ?? 0) + delta;
        const pending = { ...s.pending };
        if (n > 0) pending[key] = n;
        else delete pending[key];
        return { pending };
      });

    /** One queue per item: its writes reach Rally in the order they were made. */
    const enqueue = (key: string, job: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> => {
      const next = (queues.get(key) ?? Promise.resolve()).then(job);
      queues.set(key, next);
      void next.then(() => {
        if (queues.get(key) === next) queues.delete(key);
      });
      return next;
    };

    /** Keys with a write in flight, or one that started or finished after the read began (`since`). */
    const hotKeys = (s: AppState, since: number) => {
      const hot = new Set(Object.keys(s.pending));
      for (const [key, at] of touched) if (at > since) hot.add(key);
      return hot;
    };

    /**
     * Merge server issues and sub-issues without clobbering local writes: anything hot keeps its local copy.
     * `drop` first removes cached issues a full refresh no longer returns (deletions, moves out of scope), unless hot.
     */
    const mergeItems = (s: AppState, issues: Issue[], subs: SubIssue[], since: number, drop?: (i: Issue) => boolean) => {
      const hot = hotKeys(s, since);
      const nextIssues = { ...s.issues };
      if (drop) for (const i of Object.values(s.issues)) if (drop(i) && !hot.has(issueKey(i.id))) delete nextIssues[i.id];
      for (const i of issues) if (!hot.has(issueKey(i.id))) nextIssues[i.id] = i;
      const nextSubs = { ...s.subIssues };
      for (const t of subs) if (!hot.has(subKey(t.id))) nextSubs[t.id] = t;
      return { issues: nextIssues, subIssues: nextSubs };
    };

    const mergeRefs = (s: AppState, r: { cycles?: Cycle[]; projects?: Project[]; users?: User[]; teams?: Team[] }) => ({
      cycles: merge(s.cycles, r.cycles), projects: merge(s.projects, r.projects), users: merge(s.users, r.users),
      teams: merge(s.teams, r.teams),
    });

    const putIssue = (issue: Issue) => set((s) => ({ issues: { ...s.issues, [issue.id]: issue } }));
    const putSub = (sub: SubIssue) => set((s) => ({ subIssues: { ...s.subIssues, [sub.id]: sub } }));
    /** True when no later write for this key is still queued, so the server copy is the latest truth. */
    const lastInQueue = (key: string) => (get().pending[key] ?? 0) <= 1;
    /**
     * A failed entry reverts only the fields no later pending entry also sets. For those, each later entry
     * inherits this entry's original value, so it restores the true original if it fails too.
     */
    function rollback<T, P extends object>(
      key: string, entry: Entry<T, P>, current: T | undefined, revert: (c: T, b: T, p: P) => T,
    ): T | undefined {
      const list = (entries.get(key) ?? []) as unknown as Entry<T, P>[];
      const later = list.slice(list.indexOf(entry) + 1);
      const own: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      for (const [field, value] of Object.entries(entry.patch)) {
        const hit = later.filter((l) => field in l.patch);
        if (hit.length === 0) own[field] = value;
        else for (const l of hit) l.before = revert(l.before, entry.before, { [field]: value } as P);
      }
      return current === undefined ? undefined : revert(current, entry.before, own as P);
    }
    const track = <T, P>(key: string, entry: Entry<T, P>) =>
      entries.set(key, [...(entries.get(key) ?? []), entry as unknown as Entry<never, never>]);
    const untrack = (key: string, entry: object) => {
      const rest = (entries.get(key) ?? []).filter((x) => x !== entry);
      if (rest.length) entries.set(key, rest);
      else entries.delete(key);
    };
    /** Only a slow or unreachable Rally can succeed on retry; guard and validation refusals won't. */
    const fail = (e: unknown, retry?: () => void) => {
      const err = asApiError(e);
      get().pushToast(err.message, [0, 502, 504].includes(err.status) ? retry : undefined);
    };
    const setTeam = (id: number, patch: Partial<TeamState>) =>
      set((s) => ({ teamState: { ...s.teamState, [id]: { status: "loading", memberIds: [], ...s.teamState[id], ...patch } } }));

    /** Applies the edit at once; `after` holds the send back until that promise settles (used to serialise bulk edits). */
    const writeIssue = (id: number, patch: LocalIssuePatch, send: () => Promise<Issue>, retry: () => void,
                        after: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve()): Promise<void> => {
      const before = get().issues[id];
      if (!before) return Promise.resolve();
      putIssue(applyIssuePatch(before, patch, get().users));
      const key = issueKey(id);
      const entry = { patch, before };
      track(key, entry);
      bump(key, 1);
      return enqueue(key, async () => {
        await after;
        try {
          const fresh = await send();
          if (lastInQueue(key)) putIssue(fresh);
        } catch (e) {
          const reverted = rollback(key, entry, get().issues[id], revertIssueFields);
          if (reverted) putIssue(reverted);
          fail(e, retry);
        } finally {
          untrack(key, entry);
          bump(key, -1);
        }
      });
    };

    return {
      status: "loading",
      error: null,
      meta: null,
      yourTeamIds: [],
      users: {},
      teams: {},
      cycles: {},
      projects: {},
      issues: {},
      subIssues: {},
      teamState: {},
      lastSync: null,
      stale: false,
      toasts: [],
      pending: {},

      async load() {
        // keep the offline banner up while a retry is in flight
        set(get().status === "offline" ? { error: null } : { status: "loading", error: null });
        const started = clock;
        try {
          const b = await api.bootstrap();
          set((s) => ({
            status: "ready", error: null, meta: metaFrom(b), yourTeamIds: b.teams.map((t) => t.id), lastSync: b.server_time,
            stale: false, ...mergeRefs(s, b), ...mergeItems(s, b.issues, b.sub_issues, started),
          }));
        } catch (e) {
          const err = asApiError(e);
          set({ status: err.status === 401 ? "needs-key" : err.status === 0 ? "offline" : "error", error: err.message });
        }
      },

      async refresh() {
        const started = clock;
        const b = await api.bootstrap();
        const loaded = new Set(get().loadedTeamIds());
        const me = b.me.id;
        // Yours before, absent now, and not covered by a loaded team's refresh: reassigned away, or deleted.
        const gone = (i: Issue) => !loaded.has(i.team_id) && (i.assignee?.id === me || i.creator?.id === me);
        set((s) => ({
          meta: metaFrom(b), yourTeamIds: b.teams.map((t) => t.id), lastSync: b.server_time, stale: false,
          ...mergeRefs(s, b), ...mergeItems(s, b.issues, b.sub_issues, started, gone),
        }));
        await Promise.all([...loaded].map((id) => get().loadTeam(id)));
      },

      async loadTeam(id) {
        const known = get().teamState[id]?.status === "ready";
        if (!known) setTeam(id, { status: "loading" });
        const started = clock;
        try {
          const t = await api.team(id);
          const listed = new Set(t.issues.map((i) => i.id));
          // A full team load catches deletions and moves out of the team (spec §6.4): open issues it no longer lists go.
          const gone = (i: Issue) => i.team_id === id && i.status !== "done" && !listed.has(i.id);
          set((s) => ({
            ...mergeRefs(s, { ...t, teams: [t.team] }), ...mergeItems(s, t.issues, [], started, gone),
            teamState: { ...s.teamState, [id]: { ...s.teamState[id], status: "ready", memberIds: t.member_ids } },
          }));
        } catch (e) {
          setTeam(id, { status: known ? "ready" : "error" });
          fail(e, () => void get().loadTeam(id));
        }
      },

      async loadTeamPage(id) {
        const cursor = get().teamState[id]?.nextCursor;
        if (cursor === null) return;
        const started = clock;
        try {
          const page = await api.teamIssues(id, cursor ?? 1);
          set((s) => ({
            ...mergeRefs(s, page), ...mergeItems(s, page.issues, [], started),
            teamState: { ...s.teamState, [id]: { status: "loading", memberIds: [], ...s.teamState[id], nextCursor: page.next_cursor } },
          }));
        } catch (e) {
          fail(e, () => void get().loadTeamPage(id));
        }
      },

      loadedTeamIds: () =>
        Object.entries(get().teamState).filter(([, st]) => st.status === "ready").map(([id]) => Number(id)),

      stamp: () => clock,

      applyChanges(changes, readStamp = Infinity) {
        set((s) => ({
          ...mergeRefs(s, changes), ...mergeItems(s, changes.issues, changes.sub_issues, readStamp),
          lastSync: changes.server_time, stale: false,
        }));
      },

      updateIssue(id, patch) {
        return writeIssue(id, patch, () => api.patchIssue(id, patch), () => void get().updateIssue(id, patch));
      },

      updateIssues(ids, patch) {
        return get().updateMany(ids.map((id) => ({ id, patch })));
      },

      async updateMany(changes) {
        // Every item changes on screen now; the writes then reach Rally one after another, because Rally
        // answers parallel writes with ConcurrencyConflict. Each send waits for the previous item's write to settle.
        let previous: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
        const all = changes.map(({ id, patch }) =>
          (previous = writeIssue(id, patch, () => api.patchIssue(id, patch), () => void get().updateIssue(id, patch), previous)));
        await Promise.all(all);
      },

      rankIssue(id, req) {
        const s = get();
        const targetId = "above" in req ? req.above : req.below;
        const target = s.issues[targetId];
        if (targetId === id || !s.issues[id] || !target) return Promise.resolve();
        // Place it between the target and the target's cached neighbour on that side (spec §5.5).
        const others = Object.values(s.issues).filter((i) => i.id !== id).sort(byRank);
        const at = others.indexOf(target);
        const rank = "above" in req
          ? rankBetween(others[at - 1]?.rank ?? null, target.rank)
          : rankBetween(target.rank, others[at + 1]?.rank ?? null);
        return writeIssue(id, rank === null ? {} : { rank }, () => api.rankIssue(id, req), () => void get().rankIssue(id, req));
      },

      async createIssue(data) {
        try {
          const issue = await api.createIssue(data);
          putIssue(issue);
          return issue;
        } catch (e) {
          fail(e);
          return null;
        }
      },

      async loadIssueDetail(ref) {
        const started = clock;
        try {
          const d = await api.issue(ref);
          set((s) => ({ ...mergeRefs(s, d), ...mergeItems(s, [d.issue], d.sub_issues, started) }));
          return d.issue.id;
        } catch (e) {
          if (asApiError(e).status === 404) return "not-found";
          fail(e);
          return "error";
        }
      },

      async searchRemote(q) {
        try {
          const found = await api.search(q);
          set((s) => ({
            issues: { ...byId(found.issues), ...s.issues }, // local copies (maybe mid-edit) win
            ...mergeRefs(s, { teams: found.teams, projects: found.projects }),
          }));
          return found;
        } catch {
          return { issues: [], teams: [], projects: [] };
        }
      },

      async searchUsers(q) {
        try {
          const users = await api.users(q);
          set((s) => ({ users: merge(s.users, users) }));
          return users;
        } catch {
          return [];
        }
      },

      async searchLabels(q) {
        try {
          return await api.labels(q);
        } catch {
          return [];
        }
      },

      updateSubIssue(id, patch) {
        const before = get().subIssues[id];
        if (!before) return Promise.resolve();
        putSub(applySubIssuePatch(before, patch, get().users));
        const key = subKey(id);
        const entry = { patch, before };
        track(key, entry);
        bump(key, 1);
        return enqueue(key, async () => {
          try {
            const fresh = await api.patchSubIssue(id, patch);
            if (lastInQueue(key)) putSub(fresh);
          } catch (e) {
            const reverted = rollback(key, entry, get().subIssues[id], revertSubIssueFields);
            if (reverted) putSub(reverted);
            fail(e, () => void get().updateSubIssue(id, patch));
          } finally {
            untrack(key, entry);
            bump(key, -1);
          }
        });
      },

      pushToast(message, retry) {
        set((s) => ({ toasts: [...s.toasts, { id: ++toastSeq, message, retry }] }));
      },

      dismissToast(id) {
        set((s) => ({ toasts: s.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== id) }));
      },

      setStale(stale) {
        set({ stale });
      },
    };
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the poller and its tests**

`web/src/store/poller.ts`:

```ts
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import type { AppStore } from "./store";

export interface PollerOptions { intervalMs?: number; maxBackoffMs?: number; staleAfterFailures?: number; refreshMs?: number }

/**
 * Spec §6.4: poll your issues and every loaded team every 20s; back off 20s → 40s → 80s and mark data stale after
 * 3 failures; pause while hidden; every 5 minutes run a full refresh instead, which catches deletions and moves.
 */
export function startPoller(store: AppStore, api: Pick<Api, "changes">, opts: PollerOptions = {}): () => void {
  const interval = opts.intervalMs ?? 20_000;
  const maxBackoff = opts.maxBackoffMs ?? 80_000;
  const staleAfter = opts.staleAfterFailures ?? 3;
  const refreshEvery = opts.refreshMs ?? 300_000;
  let failures = 0;
  let lastRefresh = Date.now();
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  let stopped = false;

  const schedule = (ms: number) => {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if (!stopped) timer = setTimeout(() => void tick(), ms);
  };

  async function tick() {
    if (document.visibilityState === "hidden") return; // resumed by the visibilitychange listener
    const s = store.getState();
    if (s.status !== "ready" || !s.lastSync) {
      schedule(interval);
      return;
    }
    try {
      if (Date.now() - lastRefresh >= refreshEvery) {
        lastRefresh = Date.now();
        await s.refresh();
      } else {
        const readStamp = s.stamp();
        s.applyChanges(await api.changes(s.lastSync, s.loadedTeamIds()), readStamp);
      }
      failures = 0;
      schedule(interval);
    } catch {
      failures += 1;
      if (failures >= staleAfter) store.getState().setStale(true);
      schedule(Math.min(interval * 2 ** failures, maxBackoff));
    }
  }

  const onVisibility = () => {
    if (document.visibilityState === "visible") schedule(0);
  };
  document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
  schedule(interval);

  return () => {
    stopped = true;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
  };
}
```

`web/src/store/poller.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, expect, test, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/client";
import { EMPTY_CHANGES, makeBootstrap, makeTeamLoad, readyStore } from "../test/fixtures";
import { startPoller } from "./poller";

let visibility: DocumentVisibilityState = "visible";

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
  visibility = "visible";
  Object.defineProperty(document, "visibilityState", { configurable: true, get: () => visibility });
});
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

test("polls every 20s with the last sync time and the loaded teams", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.team.mockResolvedValueOnce(makeTeamLoad());
  await store.getState().loadTeam(9001);
  api.changes.mockResolvedValue(EMPTY_CHANGES);
  const stop = startPoller(store, api);
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(19_999);
  expect(api.changes).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1);
  expect(api.changes).toHaveBeenCalledWith("2026-10-16T11:59:30.000Z", [9001]);
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(20_000);
  expect(api.changes).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("2026-10-16T12:00:30.000Z", [9001]);
  stop();
});

test("backs off 40s then 80s and marks data stale after three failures", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.changes.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError(504, "Rally didn't respond in time"));
  const stop = startPoller(store, api);
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(20_000); // failure 1
  expect(api.changes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(40_000); // failure 2
  expect(api.changes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  expect(store.getState().stale).toBe(false);
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(80_000); // failure 3
  expect(api.changes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
  expect(store.getState().stale).toBe(true);
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(80_000); // capped at 80s
  expect(api.changes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(4);
  api.changes.mockResolvedValue(EMPTY_CHANGES);
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(80_000);
  expect(store.getState().stale).toBe(false);
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(20_000); // back to 20s after a success
  expect(api.changes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(6);
  stop();
});

test("a full refresh replaces a poll every 5 minutes", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.team.mockResolvedValue(makeTeamLoad());
  await store.getState().loadTeam(9001);
  api.changes.mockResolvedValue(EMPTY_CHANGES);
  api.bootstrap.mockResolvedValue(makeBootstrap());
  const stop = startPoller(store, api, { refreshMs: 60_000 });
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(60_000); // ticks at 20s and 40s poll; 60s refreshes
  expect(api.changes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  expect(api.bootstrap).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2); // the first load, then the refresh
  expect(api.team).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  stop();
});

test("pauses while the tab is hidden and polls on return", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.changes.mockResolvedValue(EMPTY_CHANGES);
  const stop = startPoller(store, api);
  visibility = "hidden";
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(60_000);
  expect(api.changes).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  visibility = "visible";
  document.dispatchEvent(new Event("visibilitychange"));
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
  expect(api.changes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  stop();
});

test("stop cancels future polls", async () => {
  const { store, api } = await readyStore();
  api.changes.mockResolvedValue(EMPTY_CHANGES);
  startPoller(store, api)();
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100_000);
  expect(api.changes).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
});
```

`web/src/App.tsx` (temporary until Task 23):

```tsx
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { api } from "./api/client";
import { SetupScreen } from "./components/SetupScreen";
import { StatusBanner } from "./components/StatusBanner";
import { Empty } from "./components/ViewFrame";
import { Overlays } from "./Overlays";
import { appStore, useApp, useUi } from "./store";
import { startPoller } from "./store/poller";

export function App() {
  const status = useApp((s) => s.status);
  const error = useApp((s) => s.error);
  const theme = useUi((u) => u.theme);

  useEffect(() => {
    void appStore.getState().load();
    return startPoller(appStore, api);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== "offline") return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => void appStore.getState().load(), 5000); // retry every 5s
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [status]);
  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
  }, [theme]);

  if (status === "needs-key") return <SetupScreen />;
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full bg-app text-small text-ink-1">
      <main className="m-2 flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line-2 bg-panel">
        <StatusBanner />
        {status === "loading" && <Empty>Loading Rally…</Empty>}
        {status === "offline" && <Empty>Waiting for the local server…</Empty>}
        {status === "error" && <Empty>{error}</Empty>}
        {status === "ready" && <Empty>Connected. The v2 views arrive in Task 23.</Empty>}
      </main>
      <Overlays />
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 18: UI store v2: route, selection, overlays, filters, teams

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Produces (views/types.ts): `Layout`, `GroupBy`, `OrderBy`, `Property`, `DisplayOptions`, `FilterField`, `FilterValue`, `Filters`, `Scope`.
- Produces (ui.ts):
  - `Picker = "status" | "priority" | "assignee" | "labels" | "project" | "cycle" | "estimate"`, `Menu = "display" | "filter" | null`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `web/src/views/types.ts`**

```ts
/** A view is a scope, plus filters, plus display options (spec §7). */
export type Layout = "list" | "board";
export type GroupBy = "status" | "assignee" | "priority" | "project" | "cycle" | "none";
export type OrderBy = "manual" | "priority" | "updated" | "created" | "estimate";
export type Property = "priority" | "id" | "status" | "labels" | "project" | "cycle" | "estimate" | "assignee" | "updated";
export interface DisplayOptions { layout: Layout; groupBy: GroupBy; orderBy: OrderBy; showEmpty: boolean; properties: Property[] }

export type FilterField = "status" | "assignee" | "creator" | "priority" | "label" | "project" | "cycle";
/** A status, a priority, an id, a label name, or null for "none" (no assignee, no project …). */
export type FilterValue = string | number | null;
/** Values within a field are ORed; fields are ANDed. */
export type Filters = Partial<Record<FilterField, FilterValue[]>>;

export type Scope =
  | { kind: "my"; tab: "assigned" | "created" }
  | { kind: "team"; teamId: number; tab: "active" | "backlog" | "all" };
```

```ts
  for (let n = 0; n < 7; n++) {

  expect(order).toEqual(["palette", "composer", "cheatsheet", "menu", "peek", "page", "selection"]);

});

});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `web/src/store/ui.ts`**

```ts
import { createStore } from "zustand/vanilla";
import { formatRoute, HOME, parsePath, sameRoute, type Route } from "../route";
import type { Filters } from "../views/types";

export type Picker = "status" | "priority" | "assignee" | "labels" | "project" | "cycle" | "estimate";
export type Menu = "display" | "filter" | null;
export interface PaletteState { open: boolean; picker: Picker | null; targetIds: number[] }
type HistoryLike = Pick<History, "pushState" | "replaceState">;

export interface UiState {
  route: Route;
  /** Where Esc and the breadcrumb go from an issue page. */
  backRoute: Route;
  selectedId: number | null;
  selectedIds: number[];
  peekId: number | null;
  /** The current view's rows in display order, for J/K. */
  visibleIds: number[];
  palette: PaletteState;
  composerOpen: boolean;
  cheatsheetOpen: boolean;
  menu: Menu;
  /** Filters per view key, for this session. */
  filters: Record<string, Filters>;
  joinedTeamIds: number[];
  hiddenTeamIds: number[];
  theme: "light" | "dark";
  navigate(route: Route, opts?: { replace?: boolean }): void;
  syncFromLocation(path: string): void;
  openIssue(key: string): void;
  select(id: number | null): void;
  toggleSelected(id: number): void;
  clearSelection(): void;
  openPeek(id: number | null): void;
  setVisible(ids: number[]): void;
  openPalette(picker?: Picker | null, targetIds?: number[]): void;
  closePalette(): void;
  setComposer(open: boolean): void;
  setCheatsheet(open: boolean): void;
  setMenu(menu: Menu): void;
  setFilters(view: string, filters: Filters): void;
  joinTeam(id: number): void;
  hideTeam(id: number): void;
  toggleTheme(): void;
  closeTopOverlay(): void;
}

const THEME_KEY = "rally-poc-theme";
const JOINED_KEY = "mira-teams-joined";
const HIDDEN_KEY = "mira-teams-hidden";
const storage = (): Storage | undefined => (typeof localStorage === "undefined" ? undefined : localStorage);

function readIds(key: string): number[] {
  try {
    const value: unknown = JSON.parse(storage()?.getItem(key) ?? "[]");
    return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((x): x is number => Number.isInteger(x)) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function writeIds(key: string, ids: number[]): void {
  try {
    storage()?.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(ids));
  } catch {
    /* storage full or blocked: keep it for this session */
  }
}

/** Your teams in the sidebar (spec D6): Rally's plus joined ones, minus hidden ones. */
export function sidebarTeamIds(yours: number[], joined: number[], hidden: number[]): number[] {
  return [...new Set([...yours, ...joined])].filter((id) => !hidden.includes(id));
}

const cleared = { selectedId: null, selectedIds: [] as number[], peekId: null, menu: null };

export function createUiStore(history: HistoryLike = window.history, path: string = window.location.pathname) {
  const start = parsePath(path);
  if (start.name !== "not-found" && formatRoute(start) !== path) history.replaceState(null, "", formatRoute(start));

  return createStore<UiState>()((set, get) => {
    const moveTo = (route: Route) => {
      const current = get().route;
      const backRoute = current.name === "issue" ? get().backRoute : current;
      set({ route, backRoute, ...(route.name === "issue" ? { peekId: null, menu: null } : cleared) });
    };
    return {
      route: start,
      backRoute: start.name === "issue" ? HOME : start,
      selectedId: null,
      selectedIds: [],
      peekId: null,
      visibleIds: [],
      palette: { open: false, picker: null, targetIds: [] },
      composerOpen: false,
      cheatsheetOpen: false,
      menu: null,
      filters: {},
      joinedTeamIds: readIds(JOINED_KEY),
      hiddenTeamIds: readIds(HIDDEN_KEY),
      theme: storage()?.getItem(THEME_KEY) === "dark" ? "dark" : "light",
      navigate(route, opts = {}) {
        if (sameRoute(route, get().route)) return;
        const url = formatRoute(route);
        if (opts.replace) history.replaceState(null, "", url);
        else history.pushState(null, "", url);
        moveTo(route);
      },
      syncFromLocation(path) {
        const route = parsePath(path);
        if (!sameRoute(route, get().route)) moveTo(route);
      },
      openIssue: (key) => get().navigate({ name: "issue", key: key.toUpperCase() }),
      select: (id) => set({ selectedId: id }),
      toggleSelected: (id) =>
        set((s) => ({ selectedIds: s.selectedIds.includes(id) ? s.selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...s.selectedIds, id] })),
      clearSelection: () => set({ selectedId: null, selectedIds: [] }),
      openPeek: (id) => set({ peekId: id }),
      setVisible: (ids) => set({ visibleIds: ids }),
      openPalette: (picker = null, targetIds = []) => set({ palette: { open: true, picker, targetIds }, menu: null }),
      closePalette: () => set({ palette: { open: false, picker: null, targetIds: [] } }),
      setComposer: (open) => set({ composerOpen: open }),
      setCheatsheet: (open) => set({ cheatsheetOpen: open }),
      setMenu: (menu) => set({ menu }),
      setFilters: (view, filters) => set((s) => ({ filters: { ...s.filters, [view]: filters } })),
      joinTeam(id) {
        const joined = [...new Set([...get().joinedTeamIds, id])];
        const hidden = get().hiddenTeamIds.filter((x) => x !== id);
        writeIds(JOINED_KEY, joined);
        writeIds(HIDDEN_KEY, hidden);
        set({ joinedTeamIds: joined, hiddenTeamIds: hidden });
      },
      hideTeam(id) {
        const hidden = [...new Set([...get().hiddenTeamIds, id])];
        writeIds(HIDDEN_KEY, hidden);
        set({ hiddenTeamIds: hidden });
      },
      toggleTheme() {
        const theme = get().theme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark";
        storage()?.setItem(THEME_KEY, theme);
        set({ theme });
      },
      closeTopOverlay() {
        const s = get();
        if (s.palette.open) s.closePalette();
        else if (s.composerOpen) set({ composerOpen: false });
        else if (s.cheatsheetOpen) set({ cheatsheetOpen: false });
        else if (s.menu) set({ menu: null });
        else if (s.peekId !== null) set({ peekId: null });
        else if (s.route.name === "issue") s.navigate(s.backRoute);
        else set({ selectedId: null, selectedIds: [] });
      },
    };
  });
}
```

### Task 19: The view model and display options

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/views/model.ts`, `web/src/views/model.test.ts`, `web/src/views/options.ts`, `web/src/views/options.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 18's `views/types.ts`; Task 15's `byRank`.
- Produces (model):
  - `RECENT_DONE_MS`, `Context`, `Group`, `viewKey(scope)`, `inScope(issue, scope, ctx)`, `fieldValues(issue, field)`, `matches(issue, filters)`
  - `comparator(orderBy)`, `cycleKey(cycle)`, `buildGroups(issues, opts, ctx) -> Group[]`
  - `filterOptions(field, issues, ctx)`, `valueLabel(field, value, ctx)`, `toggleFilter(filters, field, value)`
  - `FILTER_FIELDS: [FilterField, string][]`
- Produces (options): `ALL_PROPERTIES`, `PROPERTY_LABEL`, `defaultOptions(scope)`, `loadOptions(view, scope, storage?)`, `saveOptions(view, options, storage?)`, `useViewOptions(view, scope)`.

```ts
import { expect, test } from "vitest";
import type { Issue } from "../api/types";
import { DANA, makeIssue, makeProject, SAM, SPRINT3, SPRINT3_AIML, SPRINT4 } from "../test/fixtures";
import { buildGroups, comparator, filterOptions, inScope, matches, toggleFilter, type Context } from "./model";
import type { DisplayOptions } from "./types";

const NOW = Date.parse("2026-10-16T12:00:00.000Z");
const ctx: Context = {
  meId: 501, now: NOW, users: { 501: DANA, 502: SAM },
  projects: { 201: makeProject(), 202: makeProject({ id: 202, key: "F202", name: "Real project", team_id: 9002 }) },
  cycles: { 301: SPRINT3, 302: SPRINT4, 401: SPRINT3_AIML }, memberIds: [501, 502], teamId: 9001,
};
const opts = (over: Partial<DisplayOptions> = {}): DisplayOptions =>
  ({ layout: "list", groupBy: "status", orderBy: "priority", showEmpty: false, properties: [], ...over });
const ids = (xs: Issue[]) => xs.map((i) => i.id);

const open = makeIssue({ id: 1, status: "todo" });
const doneRecently = makeIssue({ id: 2, status: "done", completed_at: "2026-10-10T00:00:00.000Z" });
const doneLongAgo = makeIssue({ id: 3, status: "done", completed_at: "2026-09-01T00:00:00.000Z" });
const theirs = makeIssue({ id: 4, assignee: SAM, creator: DANA });
const backlog = makeIssue({ id: 5, status: "backlog", cycle_id: null });
const otherTeam = makeIssue({ id: 6, team_id: 9002 });

test("My issues: assigned or created by you, open or done in the last 14 days, across teams", () => {
  const all = [open, doneRecently, doneLongAgo, theirs, backlog, otherTeam];
  expect(ids(all.filter((i) => inScope(i, { kind: "my", tab: "assigned" }, ctx)))).toEqual([1, 2, 5, 6]);
  expect(ids(all.filter((i) => inScope(i, { kind: "my", tab: "created" }, ctx)))).toEqual([1, 2, 4, 5, 6]);
});

test("Team tabs: Active excludes Backlog and old Done; Backlog is Backlog; All is everything in the team", () => {
  const all = [open, doneRecently, doneLongAgo, theirs, backlog, otherTeam];
  const team = (tab: "active" | "backlog" | "all") => ids(all.filter((i) => inScope(i, { kind: "team", teamId: 9001, tab }, ctx)));
  expect(team("active")).toEqual([1, 2, 4]);
  expect(team("backlog")).toEqual([5]);
  expect(team("all")).toEqual([1, 2, 3, 4, 5]);
});

test("filters OR within a field and AND across fields; null means none", () => {
  const unassigned = makeIssue({ id: 7, assignee: null, priority: 1 });
  expect(matches(unassigned, { assignee: [null] })).toBe(true);
  expect(matches(unassigned, { assignee: [null], priority: [2, 3] })).toBe(false);
  expect(matches(makeIssue({ labels: ["ux", "PI4"] }), { label: ["PI4"] })).toBe(true);
  expect(matches(makeIssue({ labels: [] }), { label: [null] })).toBe(true);
  expect(matches(open, {})).toBe(true);
  expect(toggleFilter(toggleFilter({}, "status", "todo"), "status", "todo")).toEqual({});
});

test("ordering: priority puts Urgent first and No priority last; updated is newest first; manual is rank", () => {
  const xs = [makeIssue({ id: 1, priority: 0, rank: "a" }), makeIssue({ id: 2, priority: 3, rank: "b" }), makeIssue({ id: 3, priority: 1, rank: "c" })];
  expect(ids([...xs].sort(comparator("priority")))).toEqual([3, 2, 1]);
  expect(ids([...xs].sort(comparator("manual")))).toEqual([1, 2, 3]);
  const newer = makeIssue({ id: 9, updated_at: "2026-10-16T11:00:00.000Z" });
  expect(ids([makeIssue({ id: 8 }), newer].sort(comparator("updated")))).toEqual([9, 8]);
});

test("status groups: list leads with work in flight, board follows the workflow, empty groups on request", () => {
  const xs = [open, makeIssue({ id: 10, status: "in_progress" }), backlog];
  expect(buildGroups(xs, opts(), ctx).map((g) => g.label)).toEqual(["In Progress", "Todo", "Backlog"]);
  expect(buildGroups(xs, opts({ layout: "board", showEmpty: true }), ctx).map((g) => g.label))
    .toEqual(["Backlog", "Todo", "In Progress", "In Review", "Done"]);
});

test("assignee groups: you first, then people by name, No assignee last; empty ones are the team's people", () => {
  const xs = [makeIssue({ id: 1, assignee: SAM }), makeIssue({ id: 2, assignee: null }), makeIssue({ id: 3 })];
  expect(buildGroups(xs, opts({ groupBy: "assignee" }), ctx).map((g) => g.label)).toEqual(["Dana Demo", "Sam Sample", "No assignee"]);
  const empty = buildGroups([], opts({ groupBy: "assignee", showEmpty: true }), ctx);
  expect(empty.map((g) => [g.label, g.value])).toEqual([["Dana Demo", 501], ["Sam Sample", 502], ["No assignee", null]]);
});

test("cycle groups merge same-named sprints with the same dates across teams", () => {
  const xs = [makeIssue({ id: 1, cycle_id: 301 }), makeIssue({ id: 2, team_id: 9002, cycle_id: 401 }), makeIssue({ id: 3, cycle_id: 302 }),
              makeIssue({ id: 4, cycle_id: null, status: "todo" })];
  const groups = buildGroups(xs, opts({ groupBy: "cycle" }), { ...ctx, teamId: undefined });
  expect(groups.map((g) => [g.label, ids(g.issues)])).toEqual([
    ["2026 PI4 Sprint 3", [1, 2]], ["2026 PI4 Sprint 4", [3]], ["No cycle", [4]]]);
  expect(groups[0].cycleIds.sort()).toEqual([301, 401]);
  const shifted = { ...SPRINT3_AIML, start: "2026-10-09T04:00:00.000Z" };
  expect(buildGroups(xs, opts({ groupBy: "cycle" }), { ...ctx, cycles: { ...ctx.cycles, 401: shifted } }).length).toBe(4);
});

test("project and priority groups; filter options name their values", () => {
  const xs = [makeIssue({ id: 1, project_id: 202 }), makeIssue({ id: 2 }), makeIssue({ id: 3, kind: "defect", project_id: null })];
  expect(buildGroups(xs, opts({ groupBy: "project" }), ctx).map((g) => g.label)).toEqual(["[DEMO] Feature A", "Real project", "No project"]);
  expect(buildGroups(xs, opts({ groupBy: "priority", showEmpty: true }), ctx).map((g) => g.label))
    .toEqual(["Urgent", "High", "Medium", "Low", "No priority"]);
  expect(filterOptions("assignee", [makeIssue({ assignee: null }), makeIssue()], ctx).map((o) => o.label)).toEqual(["Dana Demo", "No assignee"]);
  expect(buildGroups(xs, opts({ groupBy: "none" }), ctx).map((g) => [g.label, g.issues.length])).toEqual([["All issues", 3]]);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `web/src/views/model.ts`**

```ts
import { PRIORITIES, PRIORITY_LABEL, STATUSES, STATUS_LABEL, type Cycle, type Issue, type Priority, type Project, type Status, type User } from "../api/types";
import { byRank } from "../store/rank";
import type { DisplayOptions, FilterField, FilterValue, Filters, GroupBy, OrderBy, Scope } from "./types";

export const RECENT_DONE_MS = 14 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // spec assumption 2
const LIST_STATUS_ORDER: Status[] = ["in_review", "in_progress", "todo", "backlog", "done"]; // work in flight first
export const FILTER_FIELDS: [FilterField, string][] = [
  ["status", "Status"], ["assignee", "Assignee"], ["creator", "Creator"], ["priority", "Priority"], ["label", "Labels"],
  ["project", "Project"], ["cycle", "Cycle"],
];

export interface Context {
  meId: number | null;
  now: number;
  users: Record<number, User>;
  projects: Record<number, Project>;
  cycles: Record<number, Cycle>;
  /** A team's people, for empty assignee groups. */
  memberIds?: number[];
  /** Limits empty project and cycle groups to that team's own. */
  teamId?: number;
}

export interface Group {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  field: GroupBy;
  /** What dropping an issue here sets: a status, priority, user id, project id, or null for "none". */
  value: FilterValue;
  /** Every cycle merged into this group (cycle grouping only). */
  cycleIds: number[];
  issues: Issue[];
}

export const viewKey = (scope: Scope): string =>
  scope.kind === "my" ? `my:${scope.tab}` : `team:${scope.teamId}:${scope.tab}`;

const recentlyDone = (i: Issue, now: number) =>
  i.status === "done" && i.completed_at !== null && now - Date.parse(i.completed_at) <= RECENT_DONE_MS;
const openOrRecent = (i: Issue, now: number) => i.status !== "done" || recentlyDone(i, now);

/** Spec §4: what each issue tab contains. */
export function inScope(i: Issue, scope: Scope, ctx: Pick<Context, "meId" | "now">): boolean {
  if (scope.kind === "my") {
    const who = scope.tab === "assigned" ? i.assignee : i.creator;
    return who !== null && who.id === ctx.meId && openOrRecent(i, ctx.now);
  }
  if (i.team_id !== scope.teamId) return false;
  if (scope.tab === "active") return i.status !== "backlog" && openOrRecent(i, ctx.now);
  if (scope.tab === "backlog") return i.status === "backlog";
  return true;
}

export function fieldValues(i: Issue, field: FilterField): FilterValue[] {
  switch (field) {
    case "status": return [i.status];
    case "assignee": return [i.assignee?.id ?? null];
    case "creator": return [i.creator?.id ?? null];
    case "priority": return [i.priority];
    case "label": return i.labels.length ? i.labels : [null];
    case "project": return [i.project_id];
    case "cycle": return [i.cycle_id];
  }
}

export function matches(i: Issue, filters: Filters): boolean {
  return (Object.entries(filters) as [FilterField, FilterValue[] | undefined][]).every(
    ([field, wanted]) => !wanted || wanted.length === 0 || fieldValues(i, field).some((v) => wanted.includes(v)));
}

export function toggleFilter(filters: Filters, field: FilterField, value: FilterValue): Filters {
  const current = filters[field] ?? [];
  const next = current.includes(value) ? current.filter((v) => v !== value) : [...current, value];
  const out = { ...filters };
  if (next.length) out[field] = next;
  else delete out[field];
  return out;
}

const time = (s: string | null) => (s ? Date.parse(s) : 0);

export function comparator(order: OrderBy): (a: Issue, b: Issue) => number {
  switch (order) {
    case "manual": return byRank;
    case "priority": return (a, b) => (a.priority || 5) - (b.priority || 5) || byRank(a, b);
    case "updated": return (a, b) => time(b.updated_at) - time(a.updated_at) || byRank(a, b);
    case "created": return (a, b) => time(b.created_at) - time(a.created_at) || byRank(a, b);
    case "estimate": return (a, b) => (b.estimate ?? -1) - (a.estimate ?? -1) || byRank(a, b);
  }
}

/** Same-named cycles with the same dates are one cycle in cross-team views (spec D8, assumption 8). */
export const cycleKey = (c: Cycle): string => `${c.name}|${c.start.slice(0, 10)}|${c.end.slice(0, 10)}`;

export function valueLabel(field: FilterField | GroupBy, v: FilterValue, ctx: Context): string {
  switch (field) {
    case "status": return STATUS_LABEL[v as Status];
    case "priority": return PRIORITY_LABEL[v as Priority];
    case "assignee": return v === null ? "No assignee" : ctx.users[v as number]?.name ?? `Person ${v}`;
    case "creator": return v === null ? "No creator" : ctx.users[v as number]?.name ?? `Person ${v}`;
    case "label": return v === null ? "No labels" : String(v);
    case "project": return v === null ? "No project" : ctx.projects[v as number]?.name ?? `Project ${v}`;
    case "cycle": return v === null ? "No cycle" : ctx.cycles[v as number]?.name ?? `Cycle ${v}`;
    case "none": return "All issues";
  }
}

export function filterOptions(field: FilterField, issues: Issue[], ctx: Context): { value: FilterValue; label: string }[] {
  if (field === "status") return STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: STATUS_LABEL[s] }));
  if (field === "priority") return PRIORITIES.map((p) => ({ value: p, label: PRIORITY_LABEL[p] }));
  const seen = new Map<string, { value: FilterValue; label: string }>();
  for (const i of issues) {
    for (const v of fieldValues(i, field)) if (!seen.has(String(v))) seen.set(String(v), { value: v, label: valueLabel(field, v, ctx) });
  }
  return [...seen.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.value === null ? 1 : b.value === null ? -1 : a.label.localeCompare(b.label)));
}

type Bucket = Omit<Group, "issues" | "field"> & { sort: string | number };
const LAST = "￿";

function bucketFor(i: Issue, opts: Pick<DisplayOptions, "groupBy" | "layout">, ctx: Context): Bucket {
  switch (opts.groupBy) {
    case "status": return statusBucket(i.status, opts.layout);
    case "priority": return priorityBucket(i.priority);
    case "assignee": return userBucket(i.assignee?.id ?? null, ctx);
    case "project": return projectBucket(i.project_id, ctx);
    case "cycle": return i.cycle_id !== null && ctx.cycles[i.cycle_id] ? cycleBucket(ctx.cycles[i.cycle_id], ctx)
      : i.cycle_id === null ? { key: "none", label: "No cycle", value: null, cycleIds: [], sort: LAST }
        : { key: `c${i.cycle_id}`, label: `Cycle ${i.cycle_id}`, value: i.cycle_id, cycleIds: [i.cycle_id], sort: "~" };
    case "none": return { key: "all", label: "All issues", value: null, cycleIds: [], sort: 0 };
  }
}

const statusBucket = (s: Status, layout: DisplayOptions["layout"]): Bucket =>
  ({ key: s, label: STATUS_LABEL[s], value: s, cycleIds: [], sort: (layout === "board" ? STATUSES : LIST_STATUS_ORDER).indexOf(s) });
const priorityBucket = (p: Priority): Bucket =>
  ({ key: `p${p}`, label: PRIORITY_LABEL[p], value: p, cycleIds: [], sort: PRIORITIES.indexOf(p) });
const userBucket = (id: number | null, ctx: Context): Bucket => id === null
  ? { key: "none", label: "No assignee", value: null, cycleIds: [], sort: LAST }
  : { key: `u${id}`, label: ctx.users[id]?.name ?? `Person ${id}`, value: id, cycleIds: [],
      sort: id === ctx.meId ? "" : (ctx.users[id]?.name ?? "").toLowerCase() };
const projectBucket = (id: number | null, ctx: Context): Bucket => id === null
  ? { key: "none", label: "No project", value: null, cycleIds: [], sort: LAST }
  : { key: `pr${id}`, label: ctx.projects[id]?.name ?? `Project ${id}`, value: id, cycleIds: [], sort: (ctx.projects[id]?.name ?? "~").toLowerCase() };
function cycleBucket(c: Cycle, ctx: Context): Bucket {
  const key = cycleKey(c);
  const merged = Object.values(ctx.cycles).filter((x) => cycleKey(x) === key).map((x) => x.id);
  return { key, label: c.name, value: c.id, cycleIds: merged, sort: c.start };
}

function emptyBuckets(opts: Pick<DisplayOptions, "groupBy" | "layout">, ctx: Context): Bucket[] {
  switch (opts.groupBy) {
    case "status": return STATUSES.map((s) => statusBucket(s, opts.layout));
    case "priority": return PRIORITIES.map(priorityBucket);
    case "assignee": return [...(ctx.memberIds ?? []).map((id) => userBucket(id, ctx)), userBucket(null, ctx)];
    case "project": return [
      ...Object.values(ctx.projects).filter((p) => p.team_id === ctx.teamId && p.status_category !== "completed").map((p) => projectBucket(p.id, ctx)),
      projectBucket(null, ctx)];
    case "cycle": return [
      ...Object.values(ctx.cycles).filter((c) => c.team_id === ctx.teamId && Date.parse(c.end) >= ctx.now).map((c) => cycleBucket(c, ctx)),
      { key: "none", label: "No cycle", value: null, cycleIds: [], sort: LAST }];
    case "none": return [];
  }
}

/** Groups in display order, each sorted by the view's ordering (spec §7). */
export function buildGroups(issues: Issue[], opts: Pick<DisplayOptions, "groupBy" | "orderBy" | "showEmpty" | "layout">, ctx: Context): Group[] {
  const buckets = new Map<string, Bucket & { issues: Issue[] }>();
  const add = (b: Bucket) => {
    if (!buckets.has(b.key)) buckets.set(b.key, { ...b, issues: [] });
    return buckets.get(b.key)!;
  };
  if (opts.showEmpty) emptyBuckets(opts, ctx).forEach(add);
  for (const i of [...issues].sort(comparator(opts.orderBy))) add(bucketFor(i, opts, ctx)).issues.push(i);
  return [...buckets.values()]
    .sort((a, b) => (a.sort < b.sort ? -1 : a.sort > b.sort ? 1 : 0))
    .map((b) => ({ key: b.key, label: b.label, field: opts.groupBy, value: b.value, cycleIds: b.cycleIds, issues: b.issues }));
}
```

The "me first" assignee sort uses `""`, which sorts before every name. `LAST` (`￿`) puts "none" after everything. Numeric sorts (status, priority) never mix with string sorts, because each grouping produces only one kind.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `web/src/views/options.ts`**

```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { DisplayOptions, GroupBy, Layout, OrderBy, Property, Scope } from "./types";

export const ALL_PROPERTIES: Property[] = ["priority", "id", "status", "labels", "project", "cycle", "estimate", "assignee", "updated"];
export const PROPERTY_LABEL: Record<Property, string> = {
  priority: "Priority", id: "ID", status: "Status", labels: "Labels", project: "Project", cycle: "Cycle",
  estimate: "Estimate", assignee: "Assignee", updated: "Updated",
};
const LAYOUTS: Layout[] = ["list", "board"];
const GROUPS: GroupBy[] = ["status", "assignee", "priority", "project", "cycle", "none"];
const ORDERS: OrderBy[] = ["manual", "priority", "updated", "created", "estimate"];
const KEY = (view: string) => `mira-view:${view}`;

/** Linear's defaults: group by status, order by priority. Backlog orders manually (spec §7). */
export function defaultOptions(scope: Scope): DisplayOptions {
  const backlog = scope.kind === "team" && scope.tab === "backlog";
  return { layout: "list", groupBy: "status", orderBy: backlog ? "manual" : "priority", showEmpty: false, properties: [...ALL_PROPERTIES] };
}

const pick = <T,>(allowed: T[], value: unknown, fallback: T): T => (allowed.includes(value as T) ? (value as T) : fallback);

/** Saved options, field by field; anything missing, unknown or unreadable falls back to the default. */
export function loadOptions(view: string, scope: Scope, storage: Storage | undefined = globalThis.localStorage): DisplayOptions {
  const base = defaultOptions(scope);
  try {
    const raw = storage?.getItem(KEY(view));
    if (!raw) return base;
    const saved = JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, unknown>;
    return {
      layout: pick(LAYOUTS, saved.layout, base.layout),
      groupBy: pick(GROUPS, saved.groupBy, base.groupBy),
      orderBy: pick(ORDERS, saved.orderBy, base.orderBy),
      showEmpty: typeof saved.showEmpty === "boolean" ? saved.showEmpty : base.showEmpty,
      properties: Array.isArray(saved.properties)
        ? saved.properties.filter((p): p is Property => ALL_PROPERTIES.includes(p as Property))
        : base.properties,
    };
  } catch {
    return base;
  }
}

export function saveOptions(view: string, options: DisplayOptions, storage: Storage | undefined = globalThis.localStorage): void {
  try {
    storage?.setItem(KEY(view), JSON.stringify(options));
  } catch {
    /* storage blocked: the options last for this session */
  }
}

export function useViewOptions(view: string, scope: Scope): [DisplayOptions, (next: Partial<DisplayOptions>) => void] {
  const [options, setOptions] = useState(() => loadOptions(view, scope));
  useEffect(() => setOptions(loadOptions(view, scope)), [view]); // reload only when the view changes
  const update = useCallback((next: Partial<DisplayOptions>) => setOptions((current) => {
    const merged = { ...current, ...next };
    saveOptions(view, merged);
    return merged;
  }), [view]);
  return [options, update];
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from "vitest";
import type { Issue } from "../api/types";
import { makeIssue, SPRINT3, SPRINT3_AIML, SPRINT4 } from "../test/fixtures";
import { REORDER_HINT, resolveDrop, type Drop, type DropResult } from "./drag";
import type { Group } from "./model";
import type { FilterValue, GroupBy } from "./types";

const group = (field: GroupBy, key: string, value: FilterValue, issues: Issue[] = [], cycleIds: number[] = []): Group =>
  ({ key, label: key, field, value, cycleIds, issues });
const a = makeIssue({ id: 1 });
const b = makeIssue({ id: 2 });
const c = makeIssue({ id: 3 });
const progress = group("status", "in_progress", "in_progress", [a, b, c]);
const cycles = { 301: SPRINT3, 302: SPRINT4, 401: SPRINT3_AIML };
const drop = (over: Partial<Drop>): DropResult =>
  resolveDrop({ issue: a, from: progress, to: progress, overId: null, orderBy: "manual", cycles, ...over });

test("dropping into another group sets that group's property", () => {
  expect(drop({ to: group("status", "done", "done") }).patch).toEqual({ status: "done" });
  expect(drop({ from: group("assignee", "u501", 501), to: group("assignee", "none", null) }).patch).toEqual({ assignee_id: null });
  expect(drop({ from: group("priority", "p2", 2), to: group("priority", "p1", 1) }).patch).toEqual({ priority: 1 });
  expect(drop({ from: group("project", "pr201", 201), to: group("project", "pr202", 202) }).patch).toEqual({ project_id: 202 });
});

test("drops that can't be written say why instead", () => {
  expect(drop({ from: group("project", "pr201", 201), to: group("project", "none", null) }).hint).toBe("Issues need a project");
  const defect = makeIssue({ id: 9, kind: "defect", project_id: null });
  expect(drop({ issue: defect, from: group("project", "none", null), to: group("project", "pr201", 201) }).hint)
    .toBe("Only stories belong to projects");
});

test("a merged cycle group picks the cycle of the issue's own team", () => {
  const merged = group("cycle", "sprint3", 301, [], [301, 401]);
  const aiml = makeIssue({ id: 7, team_id: 9002 });
  expect(drop({ issue: aiml, from: group("cycle", "none", null), to: merged }).patch).toEqual({ cycle_id: 401 });
  expect(drop({ issue: aiml, from: group("cycle", "none", null), to: group("cycle", "sprint4", 302, [], [302]) }).hint)
    .toBe("That cycle isn't in this issue's team");
  expect(drop({ from: merged, to: group("cycle", "none", null) }).patch).toEqual({ cycle_id: null });
});

test("between rows of one group: ranks when ordered manually, explains otherwise", () => {
  expect(drop({ overId: 3 }).rank).toEqual({ below: 3 });           // moving down lands below the row
  expect(drop({ issue: c, overId: 1 }).rank).toEqual({ above: 1 }); // moving up lands above it
  expect(drop({ overId: 3, orderBy: "priority" })).toEqual({ patch: null, rank: null, hint: REORDER_HINT });
  expect(drop({ overId: 1 })).toEqual({ patch: null, rank: null, hint: null }); // dropped on itself
});

test("onto a row of another group: sets the property, and lands above that row when manual", () => {
  const todo = group("status", "todo", "todo", [makeIssue({ id: 5 })]);
  expect(drop({ to: todo, overId: 5 })).toEqual({ patch: { status: "todo" }, rank: { above: 5 }, hint: null });
  expect(drop({ to: todo, overId: 5, orderBy: "updated" })).toEqual({ patch: { status: "todo" }, rank: null, hint: null });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `web/src/views/drag.ts`**

```ts
import type { Cycle, Issue, IssuePatch, Priority, RankRequest, Status } from "../api/types";
import type { Group } from "./model";
import type { OrderBy } from "./types";

export const REORDER_HINT = "Set Ordering to Manual to reorder issues";

export interface Drop {
  issue: Issue;
  from: Group;
  to: Group;
  /** The row it was dropped on, or null for the group itself. */
  overId: number | null;
  orderBy: OrderBy;
  cycles: Record<number, Cycle>;
}
export interface DropResult { patch: IssuePatch | null; rank: RankRequest | null; hint: string | null }

const none: DropResult = { patch: null, rank: null, hint: null };
const explain = (hint: string): DropResult => ({ ...none, hint });

/** What dropping into `to` writes, or the reason it can't. */
function patchFor(issue: Issue, to: Group, cycles: Record<number, Cycle>): IssuePatch | string | null {
  switch (to.field) {
    case "status": return { status: to.value as Status };
    case "priority": return { priority: to.value as Priority };
    case "assignee": return { assignee_id: to.value as number | null };
    case "project":
      if (to.value === null) return "Issues need a project";
      if (issue.kind !== "story") return "Only stories belong to projects";
      return { project_id: to.value as number };
    case "cycle": {
      if (to.value === null) return { cycle_id: null };
      const own = to.cycleIds.find((id) => cycles[id]?.team_id === issue.team_id);
      return own === undefined ? "That cycle isn't in this issue's team" : { cycle_id: own };
    }
    case "none": return null;
  }
}

/** Spec §7 drag rules: into a group sets its property; between rows ranks, but only when ordered manually. */
export function resolveDrop(d: Drop): DropResult {
  const crossing = d.from.key !== d.to.key;
  let patch: IssuePatch | null = null;
  if (crossing) {
    const p = patchFor(d.issue, d.to, d.cycles);
    if (typeof p === "string") return explain(p);
    patch = p;
  }
  const over = d.overId;
  if (over === null || over === d.issue.id) return { ...none, patch };
  if (d.orderBy !== "manual") return crossing ? { ...none, patch } : explain(REORDER_HINT);
  if (crossing) return { patch, rank: { above: over }, hint: null };
  const from = d.from.issues.findIndex((i) => i.id === d.issue.id);
  const to = d.to.issues.findIndex((i) => i.id === over);
  return { ...none, rank: from < to ? { below: over } : { above: over } };
}
```

`web/src/components/StatusIcon.tsx`:

```tsx
import { STATUS_LABEL, type Status } from "../api/types";

const COLOR: Record<Status, string> = {
  backlog: "var(--color-status-backlog)", todo: "var(--color-status-todo)", in_progress: "var(--color-status-progress)",
  in_review: "var(--color-status-review)", done: "var(--color-status-done)",
};

/** Spec §8: dashed circle Backlog, open circle Todo, half yellow In Progress, three-quarter green In Review, filled check Done. */
export function StatusIcon({ status, size = 14 }: { status: Status; size?: number }) {
  const c = COLOR[status];
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 14 14" role="img" aria-label={STATUS_LABEL[status]} className="shrink-0">
      <circle cx="7" cy="7" r="5.5" fill={status === "done" ? c : "none"} stroke={c} strokeWidth="1.5"
        strokeDasharray={status === "backlog" ? "2 1.6" : undefined} />
      {status === "in_progress" && <path d="M7 3.5 A3.5 3.5 0 0 1 7 10.5 Z" fill={c} />}
      {status === "in_review" && <path d="M7 3.5 A3.5 3.5 0 1 1 3.5 7 L7 7 Z" fill={c} />}
      {status === "done" && (
        <path d="M4.6 7.2 6.3 8.8 9.4 5.6" fill="none" stroke="white" strokeWidth="1.4" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />
      )}
    </svg>
  );
}
```

`web/src/components/PriorityIcon.tsx`:

```tsx
import { PRIORITY_LABEL, type Priority } from "../api/types";

const BARS: Record<2 | 3 | 4, number> = { 2: 3, 3: 2, 4: 1 }; // High, Medium, Low

/** Spec §8: three dashes for none, 1–3 bars for Low to High, an orange square with "!" for Urgent. */
export function PriorityIcon({ priority, size = 14 }: { priority: Priority; size?: number }) {
  const common = { width: size, height: size, viewBox: "0 0 14 14", role: "img", "aria-label": PRIORITY_LABEL[priority], className: "shrink-0" };
  if (priority === 1) {
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        <rect x="1" y="1" width="12" height="12" rx="3" fill="var(--color-urgent)" />
        <path d="M7 3.8v4" stroke="white" strokeWidth="1.6" strokeLinecap="round" />
        <circle cx="7" cy="10.2" r="0.9" fill="white" />
      </svg>
    );
  }
  if (priority === 0) {
    return <svg {...common}><path d="M2 7h2M6 7h2M10 7h2" stroke="var(--color-ink-4)" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeLinecap="round" /></svg>;
  }
  const filled = BARS[priority];
  return (
    <svg {...common}>
      {[0, 1, 2].map((n) => (
        <rect key={n} x={2 + n * 4} y={9 - n * 3} width="2.5" height={3 + n * 3} rx="0.75"
          fill={n < filled ? "var(--color-ink-2)" : "var(--color-line-3)"} />
      ))}
    </svg>
  );
}
```

`web/src/components/IssueRow.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import type { Issue } from "../api/types";
import { uiStore, useApp, useUi } from "../store";
import type { Property } from "../views/types";
import { Avatar } from "./Avatar";
import { PriorityIcon } from "./PriorityIcon";
import { StatusIcon } from "./StatusIcon";

const chip = "max-w-40 truncate rounded-full border border-line-2 px-2 text-mini text-ink-3";

export const shortDate = (iso: string) => new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "short", day: "numeric" });

/** Spec §8 row: priority, ID, status, title, then labels, project, cycle, estimate, updated, assignee. */
export function IssueRow({ issue, properties }: { issue: Issue; properties: Property[] }) {
  const selected = useUi((u) => u.selectedId === issue.id);
  const checked = useUi((u) => u.selectedIds.includes(issue.id));
  const selecting = useUi((u) => u.selectedIds.length > 0);
  const project = useApp((s) => (issue.project_id ? s.projects[issue.project_id] : undefined));
  const cycle = useApp((s) => (issue.cycle_id ? s.cycles[issue.cycle_id] : undefined));
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selected) ref.current?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: "nearest" });
  }, [selected]);
  const show = (p: Property) => properties.includes(p);
  const ui = uiStore.getState();
  return (
    <div ref={ref} role="option" aria-selected={selected} data-issue-id={issue.id} data-team-id={issue.team_id}
      onClick={() => ui.select(issue.id)} onDoubleClick={() => ui.openIssue(issue.key)}
      className={`group flex h-(--size-row) cursor-default items-center gap-2 rounded-control px-3 transition-colors duration-150 ${selected ? "bg-selected" : "hover:bg-hover"}`}>
      <input type="checkbox" aria-label={`Select ${issue.key}`} checked={checked}
        onChange={() => ui.toggleSelected(issue.id)} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className={checked || selecting ? "" : "opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100"} />
      {show("priority") && <PriorityIcon priority={issue.priority} />}
      {show("id") && <span className="w-20 shrink-0 text-ink-3">{issue.key}</span>}
      {show("status") && <StatusIcon status={issue.status} />}
      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-medium text-ink-1">{issue.title}</span>
      {issue.blocked && <span className="rounded-full bg-blocked/15 px-2 text-mini text-blocked">Blocked</span>}
      {show("labels") && issue.labels.slice(0, 2).map((l) => <span key={l} className={chip}>{l}</span>)}
      {show("project") && project && <span className={chip}>{project.name}</span>}
      {show("cycle") && cycle && <span className="text-mini text-ink-3">{cycle.name}</span>}
      {show("estimate") && issue.estimate !== null && <span className="w-6 text-right text-mini text-ink-3">{issue.estimate}</span>}
      {show("updated") && <span className="w-14 text-right text-mini text-ink-3">{shortDate(issue.updated_at)}</span>}
      {show("assignee") && <Avatar user={issue.assignee} />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/components/GroupHeader.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Priority, Status } from "../api/types";
import type { Group } from "../views/model";
import { Avatar } from "./Avatar";
import { PriorityIcon } from "./PriorityIcon";
import { StatusIcon } from "./StatusIcon";

export function GroupIcon({ group }: { group: Group }) {
  if (group.field === "status") return <StatusIcon status={group.value as Status} />;
  if (group.field === "priority") return <PriorityIcon priority={group.value as Priority} />;
  if (group.field === "assignee") return <Avatar user={group.value === null ? null : { id: group.value as number, name: group.label }} />;
  return null;
}

export function GroupHeader({ group }: { group: Group }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex h-(--size-group) items-center gap-2 rounded-control bg-hover/70 px-3">
      <GroupIcon group={group} />
      <span className="font-medium text-ink-2">{group.label}</span>
      <span className="text-ink-3">{group.issues.length}</span>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/components/SubIssueRow.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { SubIssue } from "../api/types";
import { appStore, uiStore, useApp } from "../store";
import { Avatar } from "./Avatar";

export function SubIssueRow({ sub, showParent = false }: { sub: SubIssue; showParent?: boolean }) {
  const parent = useApp((s) => s.issues[sub.issue_id]);
  const done = sub.status === "done";
  return (
    <div data-sub-issue-id={sub.id} className="flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-control px-3 hover:bg-hover">
      <input type="checkbox" aria-label={`Complete ${sub.key}`} checked={done}
        onChange={() => void appStore.getState().updateSubIssue(sub.id, { status: done ? "todo" : "done" })} />
      <span className="w-20 shrink-0 text-ink-3">{sub.key}</span>
      <span className={`min-w-0 flex-1 truncate ${done ? "text-ink-4 line-through" : "text-ink-1"}`}>{sub.title}</span>
      {showParent && parent && (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => uiStore.getState().openIssue(parent.key)} className="text-mini text-ink-3 hover:text-ink-1">
          {parent.key}
        </button>
      )}
      {sub.hours !== null && <span className="w-10 text-right text-mini text-ink-3">{sub.hours}h</span>}
      <Avatar user={sub.assignee} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`web/src/components/Popover.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";

/** A button with a panel under it. A click outside both closes it; Esc closes it through the UI store's overlay order. */
export function Popover({ open, onClose, button, children }: { open: boolean; onClose(): void; button: ReactNode; children: ReactNode }) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!ref.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);
  return <div ref={ref} className="relative">{button}{open && children}</div>;
}
```
