```tsx
import { closestCenter, DndContext, PointerSensor, useDroppable, useSensor, useSensors, type DragEndEvent } from "@dnd-kit/core";
import { SortableContext, useSortable, verticalListSortingStrategy } from "@dnd-kit/sortable";
import { CSS } from "@dnd-kit/utilities";
import type { Issue } from "../api/types";
import { GroupHeader } from "../components/GroupHeader";
import { IssueRow } from "../components/IssueRow";
import type { Group } from "./model";
import type { DisplayOptions, Property } from "./types";

export type DropHandler = (issueId: number, fromKey: string, toKey: string, overId: number | null) => void;

/** Rows and groups both carry their group's key; a drop on a row also names that row. */
export function handleDragEnd(e: DragEndEvent, onDrop: DropHandler): void {
  const from = e.active.data.current?.groupKey as string | undefined;
  const to = e.over?.data.current?.groupKey as string | undefined;
  if (!e.over || !from || !to) return;
  onDrop(Number(e.active.id), from, to, typeof e.over.id === "number" ? e.over.id : null);
}

export function useDragSensors() {
  return useSensors(useSensor(PointerSensor, { activationConstraint: { distance: 4 } }));
}

function SortableRow({ issue, groupKey, properties }: { issue: Issue; groupKey: string; properties: Property[] }) {
  // Only pointer listeners: dnd-kit's `attributes` add role="button", which makes the keyboard handler ignore Space/Enter.
  const { listeners, setNodeRef, transform, transition, isDragging } = useSortable({ id: issue.id, data: { groupKey } });
  return (
    <div ref={setNodeRef} {...listeners} style={{ transform: CSS.Transform.toString(transform), transition }}
      className={isDragging ? "relative z-10 opacity-80" : ""}>
      <IssueRow issue={issue} properties={properties} />
    </div>
  );
}

function ListGroup({ group, options }: { group: Group; options: DisplayOptions }) {
  const { setNodeRef, isOver } = useDroppable({ id: `group:${group.key}`, data: { groupKey: group.key } });
  return (
    <section ref={setNodeRef} aria-label={group.label} className={`rounded-control ${isOver ? "bg-hover/40" : ""}`}>
      {options.groupBy !== "none" && <GroupHeader group={group} />}
      <SortableContext items={group.issues.map((i) => i.id)} strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}>
        {group.issues.map((issue) => <SortableRow key={issue.id} issue={issue} groupKey={group.key} properties={options.properties} />)}
      </SortableContext>
    </section>
  );
}

export function ListLayout({ label, groups, options, onDrop }: { label: string; groups: Group[]; options: DisplayOptions; onDrop: DropHandler }) {
  const sensors = useDragSensors();
  return (
    <DndContext sensors={sensors} collisionDetection={closestCenter} onDragEnd={(e) => handleDragEnd(e, onDrop)}>
      <div role="listbox" aria-label={label} aria-multiselectable="true" className="flex flex-col gap-1 p-2">
        {groups.map((g) => <ListGroup key={g.key} group={g} options={options} />)}
      </div>
    </DndContext>
  );
}
```

`web/src/views/BoardLayout.tsx`:

```tsx
import { closestCenter, DndContext, useDroppable } from "@dnd-kit/core";
import { SortableContext, useSortable, verticalListSortingStrategy } from "@dnd-kit/sortable";
import { CSS } from "@dnd-kit/utilities";
import type { Issue } from "../api/types";
import { Avatar } from "../components/Avatar";
import { GroupIcon } from "../components/GroupHeader";
import { PriorityIcon } from "../components/PriorityIcon";
import { StatusIcon } from "../components/StatusIcon";
import { uiStore, useApp, useUi } from "../store";
import { handleDragEnd, useDragSensors, type DropHandler } from "./ListLayout";
import type { Group } from "./model";
import type { DisplayOptions, Property } from "./types";

function Card({ issue, groupKey, properties }: { issue: Issue; groupKey: string; properties: Property[] }) {
  const { listeners, setNodeRef, transform, transition, isDragging } = useSortable({ id: issue.id, data: { groupKey } });
  const selected = useUi((u) => u.selectedId === issue.id);
  const project = useApp((s) => (issue.project_id ? s.projects[issue.project_id] : undefined));
  const show = (p: Property) => properties.includes(p);
  const ui = uiStore.getState();
  return (
    <div ref={setNodeRef} {...listeners} role="option" aria-selected={selected} data-issue-id={issue.id} data-team-id={issue.team_id}
      style={{ transform: CSS.Transform.toString(transform), transition }}
      onClick={() => ui.select(issue.id)} onDoubleClick={() => ui.openIssue(issue.key)}
      className={`rounded-control border bg-surface p-2.5 transition-shadow duration-150 ${selected ? "border-focus" : "border-line-2"} ${isDragging ? "relative z-10 shadow-palette" : ""}`}>
      <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-mini text-ink-3">
        {show("id") && <span>{issue.key}</span>}
        {issue.blocked && <span className="text-blocked">Blocked</span>}
        <span className="ml-auto">{show("estimate") && issue.estimate !== null ? issue.estimate : ""}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="mb-2 line-clamp-2 font-medium text-ink-1">{issue.title}</div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        {show("priority") && <PriorityIcon priority={issue.priority} />}
        {show("status") && <StatusIcon status={issue.status} />}
        {show("project") && project && <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-mini text-ink-3">{project.name}</span>}
        <span className="ml-auto">{show("assignee") && <Avatar user={issue.assignee} />}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Column({ group, options }: { group: Group; options: DisplayOptions }) {
  const { setNodeRef, isOver } = useDroppable({ id: `group:${group.key}`, data: { groupKey: group.key } });
  return (
    <section ref={setNodeRef} aria-label={`${group.label} column`}
      className={`flex w-72 shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 rounded-control p-2 transition-colors duration-150 ${isOver ? "bg-hover" : "bg-hover/40"}`}>
      <header className="flex h-8 items-center gap-2 px-1">
        <GroupIcon group={group} />
        <span className="font-medium text-ink-2">{group.label}</span>
        <span className="text-ink-3">{group.issues.length}</span>
      </header>
      <SortableContext items={group.issues.map((i) => i.id)} strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}>
        {group.issues.map((issue) => <Card key={issue.id} issue={issue} groupKey={group.key} properties={options.properties} />)}
      </SortableContext>
    </section>
  );
}

export function BoardLayout({ label, groups, options, onDrop }: { label: string; groups: Group[]; options: DisplayOptions; onDrop: DropHandler }) {
  const sensors = useDragSensors();
  return (
    <DndContext sensors={sensors} collisionDetection={closestCenter} onDragEnd={(e) => handleDragEnd(e, onDrop)}>
      <div role="listbox" aria-label={label} className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 items-start gap-2 overflow-x-auto p-2">
        {groups.map((g) => <Column key={g.key} group={g} options={options} />)}
      </div>
    </DndContext>
  );
}
```

`web/src/views/DisplayMenu.tsx`:

```tsx
import { ALL_PROPERTIES, PROPERTY_LABEL } from "./options";
import type { DisplayOptions, GroupBy, OrderBy } from "./types";

const GROUPS: [GroupBy, string][] = [
  ["status", "Status"], ["assignee", "Assignee"], ["priority", "Priority"], ["project", "Project"], ["cycle", "Cycle"], ["none", "No grouping"],
];
const ORDERS: [OrderBy, string][] = [
  ["manual", "Manual"], ["priority", "Priority"], ["updated", "Last updated"], ["created", "Created"], ["estimate", "Estimate"],
];
const pill = (on: boolean) =>
  `rounded-full px-3 py-0.5 text-mini transition-colors duration-150 ${on ? "bg-selected font-medium text-ink-1" : "text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover"}`;
const select = "h-7 rounded-control border border-line-2 bg-transparent px-2 text-small text-ink-1";

/** Linear's display options (spec §7): layout, grouping, ordering, empty groups, row properties. */
export function DisplayMenu({ options, onChange }: { options: DisplayOptions; onChange(next: Partial<DisplayOptions>): void }) {
  return (
    <div role="dialog" aria-label="Display options"
      className="absolute right-0 top-9 z-30 w-80 rounded-palette border-[0.5px] border-line-2 bg-surface p-3 shadow-palette">
      <div role="group" aria-label="Layout" className="mb-3 flex gap-1">
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={options.layout === "list"} onClick={() => onChange({ layout: "list" })} className={pill(options.layout === "list")}>List</button>
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={options.layout === "board"} onClick={() => onChange({ layout: "board" })} className={pill(options.layout === "board")}>Board</button>
      </div>
      <label className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-ink-2">
        Grouping
        <select aria-label="Grouping" value={options.groupBy} onChange={(e) => onChange({ groupBy: e.target.value as GroupBy })} className={select}>
          {GROUPS.map(([v, l]) => <option key={v} value={v}>{l}</option>)}
        </select>
      </label>
      <label className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-ink-2">
        Ordering
        <select aria-label="Ordering" value={options.orderBy} onChange={(e) => onChange({ orderBy: e.target.value as OrderBy })} className={select}>
          {ORDERS.map(([v, l]) => <option key={v} value={v}>{l}</option>)}
        </select>
      </label>
      <label className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-ink-2">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={options.showEmpty} onChange={(e) => onChange({ showEmpty: e.target.checked })} />
        Show empty groups
      </label>
      <div role="group" aria-label="Display properties" className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
        {ALL_PROPERTIES.map((p) => {
          const on = options.properties.includes(p);
          return (
            <button key={p} type="button" aria-pressed={on} className={pill(on)}
              onClick={() => onChange({ properties: on ? options.properties.filter((x) => x !== p) : [...options.properties, p] })}>
              {PROPERTY_LABEL[p]}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/views/FilterMenu.tsx`:

```tsx
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { Issue } from "../api/types";
import { FILTER_FIELDS, filterOptions, toggleFilter, valueLabel, type Context } from "./model";
import type { FilterField, Filters } from "./types";

/** F opens this (spec §10): pick a field, then tick its values. Values come from the issues in the view. */
export function FilterMenu({ issues, filters, ctx, onChange }: { issues: Issue[]; filters: Filters; ctx: Context; onChange(f: Filters): void }) {
  const [field, setField] = useState<FilterField>("status");
  const values = useMemo(() => filterOptions(field, issues, ctx), [field, issues, ctx]);
  const label = FILTER_FIELDS.find(([f]) => f === field)?.[1] ?? field;
  return (
    <div role="dialog" aria-label="Filter"
      className="absolute left-0 top-9 z-30 flex w-96 overflow-hidden rounded-palette border-[0.5px] border-line-2 bg-surface shadow-palette">
      <ul className="w-32 shrink-0 border-r border-line-1 p-1">
        {FILTER_FIELDS.map(([f, l]) => (
          <li key={f}>
            <button type="button" aria-pressed={field === f} onClick={() => setField(f)}
              className={`flex h-8 w-full items-center rounded-control px-2 text-left ${field === f ? "bg-hover text-ink-1" : "text-ink-2 hover:bg-hover"}`}>
              {l}
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <ul aria-label={`${label} values`} className="max-h-72 flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-1">
        {values.map((o) => (
          <li key={String(o.value)}>
            <label className="flex h-8 items-center gap-2 rounded-control px-2 text-ink-1 hover:bg-hover">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={(filters[field] ?? []).includes(o.value)} onChange={() => onChange(toggleFilter(filters, field, o.value))} />
              {o.label}
            </label>
          </li>
        ))}
        {values.length === 0 && <li className="px-2 py-3 text-ink-3">Nothing to filter by</li>}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

export function FilterChips({ filters, ctx, onChange }: { filters: Filters; ctx: Context; onChange(f: Filters): void }) {
  const active = FILTER_FIELDS.filter(([f]) => filters[f]?.length);
  return (
    <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
      {active.map(([field, label]) => (
        <span key={field} className="flex h-6 items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-line-2 pl-2 pr-1 text-mini text-ink-2">
          {label} is {filters[field]!.map((v) => valueLabel(field, v, ctx)).join(", ")}
          <button type="button" aria-label={`Clear ${label} filter`} className="rounded-full p-0.5 text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover"
            onClick={() => {
              const next = { ...filters };
              delete next[field];
              onChange(next);
            }}>
            <X size={12} />
          </button>
        </span>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { uiStore } from "../store";
import type { Picker } from "../store/ui";

const button = "rounded-control px-2 py-1 text-ink-2 hover:bg-hover";
const ACTIONS: [Picker, string][] = [
  ["status", "Status"], ["priority", "Priority"], ["assignee", "Assignee"], ["labels", "Labels"], ["project", "Project"],
  ["cycle", "Cycle"], ["estimate", "Estimate"],
];

/** X selects in any view and brings up this bar (spec §7). */
export function BulkBar({ ids }: { ids: number[] }) {
  const ui = uiStore.getState();
  return (
    <div role="toolbar" aria-label="Bulk actions"
      className="mx-2 mt-2 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-control border border-line-2 bg-surface px-3 py-1.5 shadow-palette">
      <span className="mr-2 font-medium text-ink-1">{ids.length} selected</span>
      {ACTIONS.map(([picker, label]) => (
        <button key={picker} type="button" className={button} onClick={() => ui.openPalette(picker, ids)}>{label}…</button>
      ))}
      <button type="button" className={`${button} ml-auto text-ink-3`} onClick={() => ui.clearSelection()}>Clear</button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/views/IssueView.tsx`:

```tsx
import { ListFilter, SlidersHorizontal } from "lucide-react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Popover } from "../components/Popover";
import { appStore, uiStore, useAppState, useUi } from "../store";
import { BoardLayout } from "./BoardLayout";
import { BulkBar } from "./BulkBar";
import { DisplayMenu } from "./DisplayMenu";
import { resolveDrop } from "./drag";
import { FilterChips, FilterMenu } from "./FilterMenu";
import { ListLayout, type DropHandler } from "./ListLayout";
import { buildGroups, inScope, matches, viewKey, type Context } from "./model";
import { useViewOptions } from "./options";
import type { Filters, Scope } from "./types";

const NO_FILTERS: Filters = {};
const tool = "flex h-7 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-control px-2 text-ink-2 hover:bg-hover";

/** The one issue view (spec D12): a scope, plus filters, plus display options, as a list or a board. */
export function IssueView({ scope, label, empty }: { scope: Scope; label: string; empty: ReactNode }) {
  const app = useAppState();
  const view = viewKey(scope);
  const [options, setOptions] = useViewOptions(view, scope);
  const filters = useUi((u) => u.filters[view] ?? NO_FILTERS);
  const menu = useUi((u) => u.menu);
  const selectedIds = useUi((u) => u.selectedIds);

  const ctx = useMemo<Context>(() => ({
    meId: app.meta?.me.id ?? null, now: Date.now(), users: app.users, projects: app.projects, cycles: app.cycles,
    memberIds: scope.kind === "team" ? app.teamState[scope.teamId]?.memberIds : undefined,
    teamId: scope.kind === "team" ? scope.teamId : undefined,
  }), [app.meta, app.users, app.projects, app.cycles, app.teamState, scope]);
  const inView = useMemo(() => Object.values(app.issues).filter((i) => inScope(i, scope, ctx)), [app.issues, scope, ctx]);
  const shown = useMemo(() => inView.filter((i) => matches(i, filters)), [inView, filters]);
  const groups = useMemo(() => buildGroups(shown, options, ctx), [shown, options, ctx]);
  const ids = useMemo(() => groups.flatMap((g) => g.issues.map((i) => i.id)), [groups]);
  useEffect(() => uiStore.getState().setVisible(ids), [ids]);

  const onDrop = useCallback<DropHandler>((issueId, fromKey, toKey, overId) => {
    const s = appStore.getState();
    const issue = s.issues[issueId];
    const from = groups.find((g) => g.key === fromKey);
    const to = groups.find((g) => g.key === toKey);
    if (!issue || !from || !to) return;
    const result = resolveDrop({ issue, from, to, overId, orderBy: options.orderBy, cycles: s.cycles });
    if (result.hint) return s.pushToast(result.hint);
    if (result.patch) void s.updateIssue(issueId, result.patch);
    if (result.rank) void s.rankIssue(issueId, result.rank);
  }, [groups, options.orderBy]);

  const closeMenu = useCallback(() => uiStore.getState().setMenu(null), []);
  const toggle = (m: "filter" | "display") => () => uiStore.getState().setMenu(menu === m ? null : m);
  const setFilters = (f: Filters) => uiStore.getState().setFilters(view, f);

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
      <div className="flex h-10 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-line-1 px-3">
        <Popover open={menu === "filter"} onClose={closeMenu}
          button={<button type="button" aria-expanded={menu === "filter"} onClick={toggle("filter")} className={tool}><ListFilter size={14} />Filter</button>}>
          <FilterMenu issues={inView} filters={filters} ctx={ctx} onChange={setFilters} />
        </Popover>
        <FilterChips filters={filters} ctx={ctx} onChange={setFilters} />
        <div className="ml-auto">
          <Popover open={menu === "display"} onClose={closeMenu}
            button={<button type="button" aria-expanded={menu === "display"} onClick={toggle("display")} className={tool}><SlidersHorizontal size={14} />Display</button>}>
            <DisplayMenu options={options} onChange={setOptions} />
          </Popover>
        </div>
      </div>
      {selectedIds.length > 0 && <BulkBar ids={selectedIds} />}
      {shown.length === 0 && !options.showEmpty
        ? empty
        : options.layout === "board"
          ? <BoardLayout label={label} groups={groups} options={options} onDrop={onDrop} />
          : <ListLayout label={label} groups={groups} options={options} onDrop={onDrop} />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Run: `cd web && npx vitest --run src/views && npm run typecheck`

`useUi((u) => u.filters[view] ?? NO_FILTERS)` returns a stable object, which zustand v5 needs. An inline `{}` there would re-render forever.

```tsx
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";

import { App } from "./App";
import { appStore, uiStore } from "./store";

  localStorage.clear();
  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/my/assigned");
  appStore.setState(appStore.getInitialState(), true);
  uiStore.setState(uiStore.getInitialState(), true);
});
afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());

  render(<App />);
  expect(await screen.findByRole("option", { name: /Example story/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
```

`web/src/components/Tabs.tsx`:

```tsx
export function Tabs<T extends string>({ label, tabs, value, onChange }: { label: string; tabs: [T, string][]; value: T; onChange(tab: T): void }) {
  return (
    <div role="tablist" aria-label={label} className="flex items-center gap-1">
      {tabs.map(([v, l]) => (
        <button key={v} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={value === v} onClick={() => onChange(v)}
          className={`rounded-full px-3 py-0.5 text-mini transition-colors duration-150 ${value === v ? "bg-selected font-medium text-ink-1" : "text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover"}`}>
          {l}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Box, ChevronRight, Inbox, Keyboard, Moon, Search, Settings, Sun, Target } from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { Team } from "../api/types";
import type { Route, TeamTab } from "../route";
import { uiStore, useApp, useUi } from "../store";
import { sidebarTeamIds } from "../store/ui";

const item = "flex h-(--size-nav) w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-control px-2 text-left font-medium transition-colors duration-150";
const cls = (on: boolean) => `${item} ${on ? "bg-selected text-ink-1" : "text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover hover:text-ink-1"}`;
const ISSUE_TABS: TeamTab[] = ["active", "backlog", "all"];

function TeamItem({ id, team, route }: { id: number; team: Team | undefined; route: Route }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(true);
  const name = team?.name ?? `Team ${id}`;
  const here = route.name === "team" && route.teamId === id ? route.tab : null;
  const go = (tab: TeamTab) => () => uiStore.getState().navigate({ name: "team", teamId: id, tab });
  const link = (tab: TeamTab, label: string, on: boolean) => (
    <li><button type="button" aria-current={on ? "page" : undefined} onClick={go(tab)} className={`${cls(on)} pl-7`}>{label}</button></li>
  );
  return (
    <li>
      <button type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} className={cls(false)}>
        <ChevronRight size={12} className={`transition-transform duration-150 ${open ? "rotate-90" : ""}`} />
        <span className="truncate">{name}</span>
      </button>
      {open && (
        <ul aria-label={`${name} views`} className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
          {link("active", "Issues", here !== null && ISSUE_TABS.includes(here))}
          {link("cycles", "Cycles", here === "cycles")}
          {link("projects", "Projects", here === "projects")}
        </ul>
      )}
    </li>
  );
}

/** Spec §4: workspace, My issues, Workspace › Initiatives / Projects, Your teams › Issues · Cycles · Projects. */
export function Sidebar() {
  const meta = useApp((s) => s.meta);
  const yours = useApp((s) => s.yourTeamIds);
  const teams = useApp((s) => s.teams);
  const route = useUi((u) => u.route);
  const joined = useUi((u) => u.joinedTeamIds);
  const hidden = useUi((u) => u.hiddenTeamIds);
  const theme = useUi((u) => u.theme);
  const ids = useMemo(() => sidebarTeamIds(yours, joined, hidden), [yours, joined, hidden]);
  const ui = uiStore.getState();
  const go = (r: Route) => () => ui.navigate(r);
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Main" className="flex w-(--size-sidebar) shrink-0 flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-3">
      <div className="flex h-(--size-nav) items-center gap-2 px-2">
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-medium text-ink-1">{meta?.workspace.name ?? "Mira"}</span>
        <button type="button" aria-label="Search" onClick={() => ui.openPalette()} className="rounded-control p-1.5 text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover">
          <Search size={14} />
        </button>
      </div>
      <button type="button" aria-current={route.name === "my" ? "page" : undefined} onClick={go({ name: "my", tab: "assigned" })} className={cls(route.name === "my")}>
        <Inbox size={14} /><span className="flex-1">My issues</span><kbd className="text-mini font-normal text-ink-4">G M</kbd>
      </button>
      <section aria-label="Workspace">
        <h2 className="mb-1 px-2 text-mini font-medium text-ink-3">Workspace</h2>
        <button type="button" aria-current={route.name === "initiatives" ? "page" : undefined} onClick={go({ name: "initiatives" })} className={cls(route.name === "initiatives")}>
          <Target size={14} /><span className="flex-1">Initiatives</span><kbd className="text-mini font-normal text-ink-4">G N</kbd>
        </button>
        <button type="button" aria-current={route.name === "projects" ? "page" : undefined} onClick={go({ name: "projects" })} className={cls(route.name === "projects")}>
          <Box size={14} /><span className="flex-1">Projects</span><kbd className="text-mini font-normal text-ink-4">G P</kbd>
        </button>
      </section>
      <section aria-label="Your teams">
        <h2 className="mb-1 px-2 text-mini font-medium text-ink-3">Your teams</h2>
        {ids.length === 0
          ? <p className="px-2 text-mini text-ink-3">No teams yet. Open one from ⌘K.</p>
          : <ul className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">{ids.map((id) => <TeamItem key={id} id={id} team={teams[id]} route={route} />)}</ul>}
      </section>
      <div className="mt-auto flex items-center gap-1">
        {meta?.writeGuard === "demo" && (
          <span title="Only [DEMO] items can be changed" className="mr-auto rounded-full bg-hover px-2 py-0.5 text-mini text-ink-2">Demo mode</span>
        )}
        <button type="button" aria-label="Settings" onClick={go({ name: "settings" })} className="rounded-control p-2 text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover"><Settings size={14} /></button>
        <button type="button" aria-label="Toggle theme" onClick={() => ui.toggleTheme()} className="rounded-control p-2 text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover">
          {theme === "dark" ? <Sun size={14} /> : <Moon size={14} />}
        </button>
        <button type="button" aria-label="Keyboard shortcuts" onClick={() => ui.setCheatsheet(true)} className="rounded-control p-2 text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover">
          <Keyboard size={14} />
        </button>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { SubIssueRow } from "../components/SubIssueRow";
import { Tabs } from "../components/Tabs";
import { Empty, ViewFrame } from "../components/ViewFrame";
import type { MyTab } from "../route";
import { uiStore, useApp } from "../store";
import { IssueView } from "../views/IssueView";
import type { Scope } from "../views/types";

const EMPTY: Record<MyTab, string> = { assigned: "Nothing is assigned to you.", created: "You haven't created any open issues." };

/** Spec §4: your issues across every team, as Assigned or Created. */
export function MyIssues({ tab }: { tab: MyTab }) {
  const scope = useMemo<Scope>(() => ({ kind: "my", tab }), [tab]);
  const subIssues = useApp((s) => s.subIssues);
  const issues = useApp((s) => s.issues);
  const me = useApp((s) => s.meta?.me.id);
  // Planning ruling 10: your sub-issues on someone else's issue, after your own issues.
  const others = useMemo(() => (tab !== "assigned" ? [] : Object.values(subIssues).filter(
    (t) => t.assignee?.id === me && t.status !== "done" && issues[t.issue_id]?.assignee?.id !== me)), [tab, subIssues, issues, me]);
  return (
    <ViewFrame title="My issues"
      actions={<Tabs label="My issues" tabs={[["assigned", "Assigned"], ["created", "Created"]]} value={tab}
        onChange={(t) => uiStore.getState().navigate({ name: "my", tab: t })} />}>
      <IssueView scope={scope} label="My issues" empty={<Empty>{EMPTY[tab]}</Empty>} />
      {others.length > 0 && (
        <section aria-label="Sub-issues" className="px-2 pb-2">
          <h3 className="flex h-(--size-group) items-center px-3 font-medium text-ink-2">Sub-issues on others' issues</h3>
          {others.map((t) => <SubIssueRow key={t.id} sub={t} showParent />)}
        </section>
      )}
    </ViewFrame>
  );
}
```

`web/src/screens/TeamIssues.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { Tabs } from "../components/Tabs";
import { Empty, ViewFrame } from "../components/ViewFrame";
import type { IssueTab } from "../route";
import { appStore, uiStore, useApp, useUi } from "../store";
import { sidebarTeamIds } from "../store/ui";
import { IssueView } from "../views/IssueView";
import type { Scope } from "../views/types";

const EMPTY: Record<IssueTab, string> = {
  active: "No active issues: nothing in Todo, In Progress or In Review.", backlog: "The backlog is empty.", all: "This team has no issues.",
};

/** Spec §4: a team's Active, Backlog and All issues. Loads the team on first open (spec D10). */
export function TeamIssues({ teamId, tab }: { teamId: number; tab: IssueTab }) {
  const team = useApp((s) => s.teams[teamId]);
  const state = useApp((s) => s.teamState[teamId]);
  const yours = useApp((s) => s.yourTeamIds);
  const joined = useUi((u) => u.joinedTeamIds);
  const hidden = useUi((u) => u.hiddenTeamIds);
  const inSidebar = useMemo(() => sidebarTeamIds(yours, joined, hidden).includes(teamId), [yours, joined, hidden, teamId]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!appStore.getState().teamState[teamId]) void appStore.getState().loadTeam(teamId);
  }, [teamId]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (tab === "all" && appStore.getState().teamState[teamId]?.nextCursor === undefined) void appStore.getState().loadTeamPage(teamId);
  }, [tab, teamId]);
  const scope = useMemo<Scope>(() => ({ kind: "team", teamId, tab }), [teamId, tab]);
  const name = team?.name ?? "Team";
  const ui = uiStore.getState();
  return (
    <ViewFrame title={name} subtitle="Issues"
      actions={<>
        {!inSidebar && <button type="button" onClick={() => ui.joinTeam(teamId)} className="rounded-control px-2 py-1 text-ink-2 hover:bg-hover">Join team</button>}
        <Tabs label="Issues" tabs={[["active", "Active"], ["backlog", "Backlog"], ["all", "All issues"]]} value={tab}
          onChange={(t) => ui.navigate({ name: "team", teamId, tab: t })} />
      </>}>
      {state?.status === "error" && !team ? <Empty>Couldn't load this team.</Empty>
        : state?.status !== "ready" ? <Empty>Loading {name}…</Empty>
        : <IssueView scope={scope} label={`${name} ${tab}`} empty={<Empty>{EMPTY[tab]}</Empty>} />}
      {tab === "all" && typeof state?.nextCursor === "number" && (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => void appStore.getState().loadTeamPage(teamId)}
          className="mx-auto my-3 rounded-control border border-line-2 px-3 py-1 text-ink-2 hover:bg-hover">Load more</button>
      )}
    </ViewFrame>
  );
}
```

`web/src/App.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { api } from "./api/client";
import { SetupScreen } from "./components/SetupScreen";
import { Sidebar } from "./components/Sidebar";
import { StatusBanner } from "./components/StatusBanner";
import { Empty, ViewFrame } from "./components/ViewFrame";
import { Overlays } from "./Overlays";
import type { Route } from "./route";
import { MyIssues } from "./screens/MyIssues";
import { Placeholder } from "./screens/Placeholder";
import { TeamIssues } from "./screens/TeamIssues";
import { appStore, uiStore, useApp, useUi } from "./store";
import { startPoller } from "./store/poller";

function Screen({ route }: { route: Route }) {
  switch (route.name) {
    case "my": return <MyIssues tab={route.tab} />;
    case "team":
      if (route.tab === "cycles") return <Placeholder title="Cycles" />;
      if (route.tab === "projects") return <Placeholder title="Projects" />;
      return <TeamIssues teamId={route.teamId} tab={route.tab} />;
    case "issue": return <Placeholder title="Issue pages" />;
    case "projects": return <Placeholder title="Projects" />;
    case "initiatives": return <Placeholder title="Initiatives" />;
    case "settings": return <Placeholder title="Settings" />;
    case "not-found": return <ViewFrame title="Not found"><Empty>Nothing lives at {route.path}.</Empty></ViewFrame>;
  }
}

export function App() {
  const status = useApp((s) => s.status);
  const error = useApp((s) => s.error);
  const route = useUi((u) => u.route);
  const theme = useUi((u) => u.theme);
  const joined = useUi((u) => u.joinedTeamIds);

  useEffect(() => {
    void appStore.getState().load();
    return startPoller(appStore, api);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== "offline") return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => void appStore.getState().load(), 5000); // retry every 5s
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [status]);
  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
  }, [theme]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const onPop = () => uiStore.getState().syncFromLocation(window.location.pathname);
    window.addEventListener("popstate", onPop);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("popstate", onPop);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => { // joined teams need their names, so load them once
    if (status !== "ready") return;
    const s = appStore.getState();
    for (const id of joined) if (!s.teams[id] && !s.teamState[id]) void s.loadTeam(id);
  }, [status, joined]);

  if (status === "needs-key") return <SetupScreen />;
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full bg-app text-small text-ink-1">
      <Sidebar />
      <main className="m-2 ml-0 flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line-2 bg-panel">
        <StatusBanner />
        {status === "loading" && <Empty>Loading Rally…</Empty>}
        {status === "offline" && <Empty>Waiting for the local server…</Empty>}
        {status === "error" && <Empty>{error}</Empty>}
        {status === "ready" && <Screen route={route} />}
      </main>
      <Overlays />
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Interfaces:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing shortcut tests** (`web/src/keyboard/shortcuts.test.ts`, replacing the v1 file)

```ts
import { expect, test } from "vitest";
import { resolveKey, type KeyLike } from "./shortcuts";

const key = (k: string, over: Partial<KeyLike> = {}): KeyLike =>
  ({ key: k, metaKey: false, ctrlKey: false, altKey: false, shiftKey: false, target: document.body, ...over });

test("Linear's single keys (spec §10)", () => {
  const map: [string, string][] = [
    ["c", "create"], ["s", "pick-status"], ["p", "pick-priority"], ["a", "pick-assignee"], ["i", "assign-me"],
    ["l", "pick-labels"], ["x", "toggle-select"], ["j", "down"], ["k", "up"], [" ", "open-peek"], ["Enter", "open-page"],
    ["f", "filter"], ["?", "cheatsheet"],
  ];
  for (const [k, action] of map) expect(resolveKey(key(k), null).action, k).toBe(action);
});

test("Shift picks project, cycle and estimate; Caps Lock alone keeps the plain meaning", () => {
  expect(resolveKey(key("P", { shiftKey: true }), null).action).toBe("pick-project");
  expect(resolveKey(key("C", { shiftKey: true }), null).action).toBe("pick-cycle");
  expect(resolveKey(key("E", { shiftKey: true }), null).action).toBe("pick-estimate");
  expect(resolveKey(key("S"), null).action).toBe("pick-status");
  expect(resolveKey(key("J", { shiftKey: true }), null).action).toBeUndefined();
});

test("G then M, P or N", () => {
  expect(resolveKey(key("g"), null)).toEqual({ prefix: "g" });
  expect(resolveKey(key("m"), "g").action).toBe("go-my-issues");
  expect(resolveKey(key("p"), "g").action).toBe("go-projects");
  expect(resolveKey(key("n"), "g").action).toBe("go-initiatives");
  expect(resolveKey(key("x"), "g")).toEqual({ action: undefined, prefix: null });
});

test("⌘K, ⌘. and Escape", () => {
  expect(resolveKey(key("k", { metaKey: true }), null).action).toBe("palette");
  expect(resolveKey(key("k", { ctrlKey: true }), null).action).toBe("palette");
  expect(resolveKey(key(".", { metaKey: true }), null).action).toBe("copy-id");
  expect(resolveKey(key("Escape"), null).action).toBe("escape");
  expect(resolveKey(key("c", { metaKey: true }), null).action).toBeUndefined();
});

test("typing never triggers a shortcut, except ⌘K and Escape", () => {
  const input = document.createElement("input");
  const editor = document.createElement("div");
  editor.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
  for (const target of [input, editor]) {
    expect(resolveKey(key("s", { target }), null).action).toBeUndefined();
    expect(resolveKey(key(".", { target, metaKey: true }), null).action).toBeUndefined();
    expect(resolveKey(key("k", { target, metaKey: true }), null).action).toBe("palette");
    expect(resolveKey(key("Escape", { target }), null).action).toBe("escape");
  }
});

test("buttons and checkboxes keep Space and Enter", () => {
  const button = document.createElement("button");
  const box = Object.assign(document.createElement("input"), { type: "checkbox" });
  expect(resolveKey(key(" ", { target: button }), null).action).toBeUndefined();
  expect(resolveKey(key("Enter", { target: box }), null).action).toBeUndefined();
  expect(resolveKey(key("s", { target: button }), null).action).toBe("pick-status");
});
```

The checkbox counts as a typing target, because `isTypingTarget` returns true for every `INPUT`. So `Enter` on it is `undefined` either way, which is what the test expects.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `web/src/keyboard/shortcuts.ts`**

```ts
export type Action =
  | "down" | "up" | "open-peek" | "open-page" | "create" | "toggle-select" | "palette" | "cheatsheet" | "escape"
  | "filter" | "copy-id" | "assign-me"
  | "pick-status" | "pick-priority" | "pick-assignee" | "pick-labels" | "pick-project" | "pick-cycle" | "pick-estimate"
  | "go-my-issues" | "go-projects" | "go-initiatives";

export interface KeyLike { key: string; metaKey: boolean; ctrlKey: boolean; altKey: boolean; shiftKey: boolean; target: EventTarget | null }

const SINGLE: Record<string, Action> = {
  j: "down", k: "up", " ": "open-peek", Enter: "open-page", c: "create", x: "toggle-select", "?": "cheatsheet", f: "filter",
  s: "pick-status", p: "pick-priority", a: "pick-assignee", i: "assign-me", l: "pick-labels",
};
const SHIFTED: Record<string, Action> = { p: "pick-project", c: "pick-cycle", e: "pick-estimate" };
const AFTER_G: Record<string, Action> = { m: "go-my-issues", p: "go-projects", n: "go-initiatives" };

export function isTypingTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
  if (["INPUT", "TEXTAREA", "SELECT"].includes(target.tagName)) return true;
  return target.closest('[contenteditable=""], [contenteditable="true"]') !== null;
}

/** Buttons, links and checkboxes use Space/Enter themselves. */
function isActivatable(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
  return target.closest('button, a[href], [role="button"], input[type="checkbox"]') !== null;
}

/** Linear's keys (spec §10). Typing in a field never triggers a shortcut, except ⌘K and Escape. */
export function resolveKey(e: KeyLike, prefix: "g" | null): { action?: Action; prefix: "g" | null } {
  if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") return { action: "palette", prefix: null };
  if (e.key === "Escape") return { action: "escape", prefix: null };
  if (isTypingTarget(e.target)) return { prefix: null };
  if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === ".") return { action: "copy-id", prefix: null };
  if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return { prefix: null };
  if ((e.key === " " || e.key === "Enter") && isActivatable(e.target)) return { prefix: null }; // native activation wins
  const key = e.key.length === 1 ? e.key.toLowerCase() : e.key; // Caps Lock sends capitals without Shift
  if (prefix === "g") return { action: AFTER_G[key], prefix: null };
  if (e.shiftKey && /^[a-z]$/.test(key)) return { action: SHIFTED[key], prefix: null }; // ⇧P, ⇧C, ⇧E
  if (key === "g") return { prefix: "g" };
  return { action: SINGLE[key], prefix: null };
}
```

`useShortcuts.ts` passes the browser `KeyboardEvent`, which already has `shiftKey`, so it needs no change.

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, expect, test, vi } from "vitest";
import { appStore, uiStore } from "../store";
import { makeBootstrap, serve } from "../test/fixtures";
import { runAction } from "./actions";

const ui = () => uiStore.getState();

beforeEach(async () => {
  appStore.setState(appStore.getInitialState(), true);
  uiStore.setState(uiStore.getInitialState(), true);
  serve({ "GET /api/bootstrap": makeBootstrap() });
  await appStore.getState().load();
  ui().setVisible([1001, 1201, 1002]);
});
afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());

test("J and K move through the visible rows, and an open peek follows", () => {
  runAction("down");
  expect(ui().selectedId).toBe(1001);
  runAction("down");
  ui().openPeek(1201);
  runAction("down");
  expect([ui().selectedId, ui().peekId]).toEqual([1002, 1002]);
  runAction("down");
  expect(ui().selectedId).toBe(1002); // stays on the last row
  runAction("up");
  expect(ui().selectedId).toBe(1201);
});

test("X adds the selected row to the multi-selection", () => {
  ui().select(1201);
  runAction("toggle-select");
  expect(ui().selectedIds).toEqual([1201]);
});

test("pickers target the multi-selection, else the selected row", () => {
  ui().select(1001);
  runAction("pick-status");
  expect(ui().palette).toEqual({ open: true, picker: "status", targetIds: [1001] });
  ui().closePalette();
  ui().toggleSelected(1002);
  ui().toggleSelected(1201);
  runAction("pick-project");
  expect(ui().palette).toEqual({ open: true, picker: "project", targetIds: [1002, 1201] });
});

test("I assigns the targets to you", () => {
  const spy = vi.spyOn(appStore.getState(), "updateIssues").mockResolvedValue();
  ui().select(1300);
  runAction("assign-me");
  expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith([1300], { assignee_id: 501 });
});

test("Enter opens the issue page and Esc goes back", () => {
  ui().navigate({ name: "team", teamId: 9001, tab: "backlog" });
  ui().select(1002);
  runAction("open-page");
  expect(ui().route).toEqual({ name: "issue", key: "US1002" });
  runAction("escape");
  expect(ui().route).toEqual({ name: "team", teamId: 9001, tab: "backlog" });
});

test("G M, G P, G N navigate; F toggles the filter menu", () => {
  runAction("go-projects");
  expect(ui().route).toEqual({ name: "projects" });
  runAction("go-initiatives");
  expect(ui().route).toEqual({ name: "initiatives" });
  runAction("go-my-issues");
  expect(ui().route).toEqual({ name: "my", tab: "assigned" });
  runAction("filter");
  expect(ui().menu).toBe("filter");
  runAction("filter");
  expect(ui().menu).toBeNull();
});

test("while the palette is open only ⌘K acts", () => {
  runAction("palette");
  runAction("down");
  expect(ui().selectedId).toBeNull();
  runAction("palette");
  expect(ui().palette.open).toBe(false);
});

test("⌘. copies the selected IDs", () => {
  const writeText = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
  Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", { configurable: true, value: { writeText } });
  ui().toggleSelected(1001);
  ui().toggleSelected(1201);
  runAction("copy-id");
  expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith("US1001, US1201");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `web/src/keyboard/actions.ts`**

```ts
import { appStore, uiStore } from "../store";
import type { Picker, UiState } from "../store/ui";
import type { Action } from "./shortcuts";

const PICKERS: Partial<Record<Action, Picker>> = {
  "pick-status": "status", "pick-priority": "priority", "pick-assignee": "assignee", "pick-labels": "labels",
  "pick-project": "project", "pick-cycle": "cycle", "pick-estimate": "estimate",
};

/** What a command acts on: the multi-selection, else the open page, the peeked issue, or the selected row. */
function targets(ui: UiState): number[] {
  if (ui.selectedIds.length) return ui.selectedIds;
  const route = ui.route;
  const page = route.name === "issue" ? Object.values(appStore.getState().issues).find((i) => i.key === route.key)?.id ?? null : null;
  const one = page ?? ui.peekId ?? ui.selectedId;
  return one === null ? [] : [one];
}

function move(ui: UiState, delta: number): void {
  const ids = ui.visibleIds;
  if (!ids.length) return;
  const at = ui.selectedId === null ? -1 : ids.indexOf(ui.selectedId);
  const next = at < 0 ? (delta > 0 ? 0 : ids.length - 1) : Math.min(ids.length - 1, Math.max(0, at + delta));
  ui.select(ids[next]);
  if (ui.peekId !== null) ui.openPeek(ids[next]); // the side panel follows the selection
}

export function runAction(action: Action): void {
  const ui = uiStore.getState();
  if (action === "palette") {
    if (ui.palette.open) ui.closePalette();
    else ui.openPalette(null, targets(ui));
    return;
  }
  if (ui.palette.open || ui.composerOpen) return; // dialogs own the keyboard
  switch (action) {
    case "escape": return ui.closeTopOverlay();
    case "cheatsheet": return ui.setCheatsheet(!ui.cheatsheetOpen);
    case "create": return ui.setComposer(true);
    case "filter": return ui.setMenu(ui.menu === "filter" ? null : "filter");
    case "go-my-issues": return ui.navigate({ name: "my", tab: "assigned" });
    case "go-projects": return ui.navigate({ name: "projects" });
    case "go-initiatives": return ui.navigate({ name: "initiatives" });
    case "down": return move(ui, 1);
    case "up": return move(ui, -1);
    case "toggle-select":
      if (ui.selectedId !== null) ui.toggleSelected(ui.selectedId);
      return;
  }
  const ids = targets(ui);
  if (!ids.length) return;
  const app = appStore.getState();
  if (action === "open-peek") return ui.openPeek(ui.peekId === ids[0] ? null : ids[0]);
  if (action === "open-page") {
    const key = app.issues[ids[0]]?.key;
    if (key) ui.openIssue(key);
    return;
  }
  if (action === "copy-id") {
    const keys = ids.map((id) => app.issues[id]?.key).filter(Boolean).join(", ");
    void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(keys);
    return;
  }
  if (action === "assign-me") {
    if (app.meta) void app.updateIssues(ids, { assignee_id: app.meta.me.id });
    return;
  }
  const picker = PICKERS[action];
  if (picker) ui.openPalette(picker, ids);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the palette modules**

`web/src/palette/search.ts`:

```ts
import type { Issue } from "../api/types";

/** Local ⌘K matches: exact key, key prefix, title prefix, then anywhere. */
export function searchIssues(issues: Record<number, Issue>, query: string, limit = 8): Issue[] {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!q) return [];
  const score = (i: Issue) => {
    const key = i.key.toLowerCase();
    const title = i.title.toLowerCase();
    if (key === q) return 0;
    if (key.startsWith(q)) return 1;
    if (title.startsWith(q)) return 2;
    return title.includes(q) || key.includes(q) ? 3 : -1;
  };
  return Object.values(issues)
    .map((i) => [score(i), i] as const)
    .filter(([s]) => s >= 0)
    .sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0] || a[1].key.localeCompare(b[1].key))
    .slice(0, limit)
    .map(([, i]) => i);
}
```

`web/src/palette/pickers.ts`:

```ts
import { PRIORITIES, PRIORITY_LABEL, STATUSES, STATUS_LABEL, type Issue, type IssuePatch, type Priority, type Status, type User } from "../api/types";
import type { AppState } from "../store/store";
import type { Picker } from "../store/ui";

export interface PickerOption {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  hint?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  status?: Status;
  priority?: Priority;
  user?: User | null;
  /** The change for one issue, or null when that issue needs none. */
  apply(issue: Issue): IssuePatch | null;
}

export const PICKER_PLACEHOLDER: Record<Picker, string> = {
  status: "Change status…", priority: "Set priority…", assignee: "Assign to…", labels: "Add or remove labels…",
  project: "Move to project…", cycle: "Move to cycle…", estimate: "Set estimate…",
};
export const ESTIMATES = [1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13]; // spec assumption 7

const byName = (a: { name: string }, b: { name: string }) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name);

export function pickerOptions(picker: Picker, app: AppState, targets: Issue[], remoteLabels: string[] = []): PickerOption[] {
  const teams = new Set(targets.map((t) => t.team_id));
  switch (picker) {
    case "status":
      return STATUSES.map((s): PickerOption => ({ id: s, label: STATUS_LABEL[s], status: s, apply: (i) => (i.status === s ? null : { status: s }) }));
    case "priority":
      return PRIORITIES.map((p): PickerOption => ({
        id: `p${p}`, label: PRIORITY_LABEL[p], priority: p, apply: (i) => (i.priority === p ? null : { priority: p }) }));
    case "assignee": {
      const me = app.meta?.me;
      const assign = (u: User | null, hint?: string): PickerOption => ({
        id: u ? `u${u.id}` : "none", label: u?.name ?? "No assignee", hint, user: u,
        apply: (i) => ((i.assignee?.id ?? null) === (u?.id ?? null) ? null : { assignee_id: u?.id ?? null }) });
      return [assign(null), ...(me ? [assign(me, "You")] : []),
        ...Object.values(app.users).filter((u) => u.id !== me?.id).sort(byName).map((u) => assign(u))];
    }
    case "project": {
      const prefix = app.meta?.writeGuard === "demo" ? app.meta.demoPrefix : null;
      return Object.values(app.projects)
        .filter((p) => teams.has(p.team_id) && p.status_category !== "completed")
        .sort(byName)
        .map((p): PickerOption => {
          const locked = prefix !== null && !p.name.startsWith(prefix);
          return { id: `pr${p.id}`, label: p.name, hint: locked ? "Demo mode" : p.key, disabled: locked,
                   apply: (i) => (i.kind !== "story" || i.project_id === p.id ? null : { project_id: p.id }) };
        });
    }
    case "cycle": {
      const now = Date.now();
      const cycles = Object.values(app.cycles)
        .filter((c) => teams.has(c.team_id) && Date.parse(c.end) >= now)
        .sort((a, b) => a.start.localeCompare(b.start));
      return [
        { id: "none", label: "No cycle", apply: (i) => (i.cycle_id === null ? null : { cycle_id: null }) },
        ...cycles.map((c): PickerOption => ({
          id: `c${c.id}`, label: c.name,
          hint: teams.size > 1 ? app.teams[c.team_id]?.name : Date.parse(c.start) <= now ? "Current" : undefined,
          apply: (i) => (i.team_id !== c.team_id || i.cycle_id === c.id ? null : { cycle_id: c.id }),
        })),
      ];
    }
    case "estimate": {
      const inUse = targets.map((t) => t.estimate).filter((e): e is number => e !== null && !ESTIMATES.includes(e));
      const values = [...new Set([...ESTIMATES, ...inUse])].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      return [
        ...values.map((n): PickerOption => ({
          id: `e${n}`, label: n === 1 ? "1 point" : `${n} points`, apply: (i) => (i.estimate === n ? null : { estimate: n }) })),
        { id: "none", label: "No estimate", apply: (i) => (i.estimate === null ? null : { estimate: null }) },
      ];
    }
    case "labels": {
      const demoTag = app.meta?.writeGuard === "demo" ? app.meta.demoTag : null;
      const names = new Set<string>([...Object.values(app.issues).flatMap((i) => i.labels), ...remoteLabels]);
      if (app.meta) names.add(app.meta.demoTag);
      return [...names].sort().map((label): PickerOption => {
        const all = targets.length > 0 && targets.every((i) => i.labels.includes(label));
        return {
          id: `l:${label}`, label: `${all ? "Remove" : "Add"} ${label}`,
          hint: label === demoTag ? "Required in demo mode" : undefined, disabled: label === demoTag,
          apply: (i) => {
            const has = i.labels.includes(label);
            if (all) return has ? { labels: i.labels.filter((l) => l !== label) } : null;
            return has ? null : { labels: [...i.labels, label] };
          },
        };
      });
    }
  }
}
```

The labels test's names are the store's labels (`collabathon-demo`) plus the remote `PI4` and `ux`. Sorted by code point, uppercase first, they read `["PI4", "collabathon-demo", "ux"]`.

`web/src/palette/commands.ts`:

```ts
import type { Issue, Team } from "../api/types";
import { appStore, uiStore } from "../store";
import type { Picker } from "../store/ui";

export interface Command { id: string; group: string; label: string; shortcut?: string; run(): void }

const PICKS: [Picker, string, string][] = [
  ["status", "Change status…", "S"], ["priority", "Set priority…", "P"], ["assignee", "Assign to…", "A"],
  ["labels", "Labels…", "L"], ["project", "Move to project…", "⇧P"], ["cycle", "Move to cycle…", "⇧C"], ["estimate", "Set estimate…", "⇧E"],
];

/** Context first (spec §10): the selected issues' actions, then navigation, then Mira's own commands. */
export function buildCommands({ targets, teams }: { targets: Issue[]; teams: Team[] }): Command[] {
  const ui = uiStore.getState();
  const app = appStore.getState();
  const ids = targets.map((t) => t.id);
  const then = (fn: () => void) => () => {
    fn();
    uiStore.getState().closePalette();
  };
  const out: Command[] = [];
  if (targets.length) {
    const group = targets.length === 1 ? targets[0].key : `${targets.length} issues`;
    const blocked = targets.every((t) => t.blocked);
    for (const [picker, label, shortcut] of PICKS) out.push({ id: `pick-${picker}`, group, label, shortcut, run: () => ui.openPalette(picker, ids) });
    out.push(
      { id: "assign-me", group, label: "Assign to me", shortcut: "I",
        run: then(() => { if (app.meta) void app.updateIssues(ids, { assignee_id: app.meta.me.id }); }) },
      { id: "toggle-blocked", group, label: blocked ? "Unblock" : "Mark blocked", run: then(() => void app.updateIssues(ids, { blocked: !blocked })) },
      { id: "copy-id", group, label: "Copy ID", shortcut: "⌘.", run: then(() => void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(targets.map((t) => t.key).join(", "))) },
    );
    if (targets.length === 1) out.push({ id: "open", group, label: "Open", shortcut: "Enter", run: then(() => ui.openIssue(targets[0].key)) });
  }
  out.push(
    { id: "go-my", group: "Navigation", label: "Go to My issues", shortcut: "G M", run: then(() => ui.navigate({ name: "my", tab: "assigned" })) },
    { id: "go-projects", group: "Navigation", label: "Go to Projects", shortcut: "G P", run: then(() => ui.navigate({ name: "projects" })) },
    { id: "go-initiatives", group: "Navigation", label: "Go to Initiatives", shortcut: "G N", run: then(() => ui.navigate({ name: "initiatives" })) },
    ...teams.map((t): Command => ({ id: `go-team-${t.id}`, group: "Navigation", label: `Go to team ${t.name}`,
                                   run: then(() => ui.navigate({ name: "team", teamId: t.id, tab: "active" })) })),
    { id: "create", group: "Mira", label: "Create issue", shortcut: "C", run: then(() => ui.setComposer(true)) },
    { id: "theme", group: "Mira", label: "Toggle theme", run: then(() => ui.toggleTheme()) },
    { id: "shortcuts", group: "Mira", label: "Keyboard shortcuts", shortcut: "?", run: then(() => ui.setCheatsheet(true)) },
  );
  return out;
}
```

`web/src/palette/CommandPalette.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Command } from "cmdk";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { Issue, SearchResult } from "../api/types";
import { Avatar } from "../components/Avatar";
import { PriorityIcon } from "../components/PriorityIcon";
import { StatusIcon } from "../components/StatusIcon";
import { appStore, uiStore, useAppState, useUi } from "../store";
import { sidebarTeamIds } from "../store/ui";
import { buildCommands } from "./commands";
import { PICKER_PLACEHOLDER, pickerOptions } from "./pickers";
import { searchIssues } from "./search";

const itemClass = "flex h-(--size-palette-item) cursor-default items-center gap-3 rounded-control px-3 text-small text-ink-1 data-[selected=true]:bg-hover data-[disabled=true]:opacity-50";
const groupClass = "[&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:px-3 [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:py-1.5 [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:text-mini [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:text-ink-3";
const NO_RESULTS: SearchResult = { issues: [], teams: [], projects: [] };

function Kbd({ keys }: { keys?: string }) {
  if (!keys) return null;
  return (
    <span className="ml-auto flex gap-1">
      {keys.split(" ").map((k) => <kbd key={k} className="rounded border border-line-2 px-1.5 text-mini text-ink-3">{k}</kbd>)}
    </span>
  );
}

export function CommandPalette() {
  const palette = useUi((u) => u.palette);
  const joined = useUi((u) => u.joinedTeamIds);
  const hidden = useUi((u) => u.hiddenTeamIds);
  const app = useAppState();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [remote, setRemote] = useState<SearchResult>(NO_RESULTS);
  const [labels, setLabels] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const targets = palette.targetIds.map((id) => app.issues[id]).filter((i): i is Issue => Boolean(i));
  const local = useMemo(() => searchIssues(app.issues, query), [app.issues, query]);
  const teams = useMemo(() => sidebarTeamIds(app.yourTeamIds, joined, hidden).map((id) => app.teams[id]).filter(Boolean),
    [app.yourTeamIds, app.teams, joined, hidden]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setQuery("");
    setRemote(NO_RESULTS);
    setLabels([]);
  }, [palette.open, palette.picker]);
  useEffect(() => { // Rally lookups the cache can't answer: issues and teams, workspace people (D16), labels
    const text = query.trim();
    const s = appStore.getState();
    let job: (() => void) | null = null;
    if (palette.picker === "assignee" && text.length >= 2) job = () => void s.searchUsers(text);
    else if (palette.picker === "labels" && text) job = () => void s.searchLabels(text).then(setLabels);
    else if (!palette.picker && text.length >= 2) job = () => void s.searchRemote(text).then(setRemote);
    if (!job) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(job, 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [query, palette.picker]);

  const ui = uiStore.getState();
  const close = () => ui.closePalette();
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const chip = targets.length === 1 ? `${targets[0].key} · ${targets[0].title}` : targets.length ? `${targets.length} issues` : null;

  let body;
  if (palette.picker) {
    const options = pickerOptions(palette.picker, app, targets, labels).filter((o) => o.label.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    body = options.map((o) => (
      <Command.Item key={o.id} value={o.id} disabled={o.disabled} className={itemClass}
        onSelect={() => {
          const changes = targets.flatMap((issue) => {
            const patch = o.apply(issue);
            return patch ? [{ id: issue.id, patch }] : [];
          });
          void appStore.getState().updateMany(changes);
          close();
        }}>
        <span aria-hidden className="flex">
          {o.status && <StatusIcon status={o.status} />}
          {o.priority !== undefined && <PriorityIcon priority={o.priority} />}
          {o.user !== undefined && <Avatar user={o.user} />}
        </span>
        <span>{o.label}</span>
        {o.hint && <span className="ml-auto text-mini text-ink-3">{o.hint}</span>}
      </Command.Item>
    ));
  } else {
    const commands = buildCommands({ targets, teams }).filter((c) => c.label.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    const groups = [...new Set(commands.map((c) => c.group))];
    const remoteIssues = remote.issues.filter((r) => !local.some((l) => l.id === r.id));
    const remoteTeams = remote.teams.filter((t) => !teams.some((x) => x.id === t.id));
    const issueItem = (issue: Issue) => (
      <Command.Item key={issue.id} value={`issue-${issue.id}`} className={itemClass} onSelect={() => { close(); ui.openIssue(issue.key); }}>
        <span aria-hidden className="flex"><StatusIcon status={issue.status} /></span>
        <span className="w-20 text-ink-3">{issue.key}</span>
        <span className="truncate">{issue.title}</span>
      </Command.Item>
    );
    body = (
      <>
        {groups.map((g) => (
          <Command.Group key={g} heading={g} className={groupClass}>
            {commands.filter((c) => c.group === g).map((c) => (
              <Command.Item key={c.id} value={c.id} className={itemClass} onSelect={c.run}>
                <span>{c.label}</span>
                <Kbd keys={c.shortcut} />
              </Command.Item>
            ))}
          </Command.Group>
        ))}
        {local.length > 0 && <Command.Group heading="Issues" className={groupClass}>{local.map(issueItem)}</Command.Group>}
        {remoteIssues.length > 0 && <Command.Group heading="From Rally" className={groupClass}>{remoteIssues.map(issueItem)}</Command.Group>}
        {remoteTeams.length > 0 && (
          <Command.Group heading="Teams" className={groupClass}>
            {remoteTeams.map((t) => (
              <Command.Item key={t.id} value={`team-${t.id}`} className={itemClass}
                onSelect={() => { close(); ui.navigate({ name: "team", teamId: t.id, tab: "active" }); }}>
                <span>Go to team {t.name}</span>
              </Command.Item>
            ))}
          </Command.Group>
        )}
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Command.Dialog open={palette.open} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && close()} label="Command palette" shouldFilter={false}
      overlayClassName="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-black/10"
      contentClassName="fixed left-1/2 top-[14%] z-50 w-(--size-palette) -translate-x-1/2 overflow-hidden rounded-palette border-[0.5px] border-line-2 bg-surface shadow-palette">
      {chip && (
        <div className="px-3 pt-3">
          <span className="rounded-full bg-hover px-2 py-0.5 text-mini text-ink-2">{chip}</span>
        </div>
      )}
      <Command.Input value={query} onValueChange={setQuery}
        placeholder={palette.picker ? PICKER_PLACEHOLDER[palette.picker] : "Type a command or search…"}
        className="h-(--size-palette-input) w-full bg-transparent px-3 text-small text-ink-1 outline-none placeholder:text-ink-4" />
      <Command.List className="max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto border-t border-line-1 p-1.5">
        <Command.Empty className="px-3 py-6 text-center text-ink-3">No results</Command.Empty>
        {body}
      </Command.List>
    </Command.Dialog>
  );
}
```

The palette's icons are `aria-hidden`, so an option's accessible name is just its label (for example "High"). The flows match on that.

```tsx
import { useUi } from "../store";

const ROWS: [string, string][] = [
  ["J / K", "Move down / up"], ["Space", "Peek"], ["Enter", "Open"], ["X", "Select"], ["C", "Create issue"],
  ["S", "Status"], ["P", "Priority"], ["A", "Assign to…"], ["I", "Assign to me"], ["L", "Labels"],
  ["⇧P", "Project"], ["⇧C", "Cycle"], ["⇧E", "Estimate"], ["F", "Filter"], ["⌘K", "Command palette"], ["⌘.", "Copy ID"],
  ["G then M / P / N", "My issues / Projects / Initiatives"], ["?", "This list"], ["Esc", "Back / close"],
];

export function Cheatsheet() {
  const open = useUi((u) => u.cheatsheetOpen);
  if (!open) return null;
  return (
    <div role="dialog" aria-label="Keyboard shortcuts" className="fixed bottom-14 left-3 z-40 w-80 rounded-palette border border-line-2 bg-surface p-3 shadow-palette">
      <h2 className="mb-2 font-medium text-ink-1">Keyboard shortcuts</h2>
      <dl className="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-4 gap-y-1">
        {ROWS.map(([keys, what]) => (
          <div key={keys} className="contents">
            <dt className="text-mini text-ink-3">{keys}</dt>
            <dd className="text-ink-2">{what}</dd>
          </div>
        ))}
      </dl>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/issue/SubIssueList.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { SubIssue } from "../api/types";
import { SubIssueRow } from "../components/SubIssueRow";

export function SubIssueList({ subs }: { subs: SubIssue[] }) {
  if (!subs.length) return null;
  const done = subs.filter((t) => t.status === "done").length;
  const hours = subs.reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.status === "done" ? 0 : t.hours ?? 0), 0);
  return (
    <section aria-label="Sub-issues" className="mt-8">
      <h3 className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2 px-3 font-medium text-ink-2">
        Sub-issues <span className="font-normal text-ink-3">{done}/{subs.length} · {hours}h to do</span>
      </h3>
      {subs.map((t) => <SubIssueRow key={t.id} sub={t} />)}
    </section>
  );
}
```

`web/src/issue/IssueDetail.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { PRIORITY_LABEL, STATUS_LABEL, type Issue } from "../api/types";
import { Avatar } from "../components/Avatar";
import { PriorityIcon } from "../components/PriorityIcon";
import { StatusIcon } from "../components/StatusIcon";
import { Empty } from "../components/ViewFrame";
import { DescriptionEditor } from "../editor/DescriptionEditor";
import { appStore, uiStore, useAppState } from "../store";
import type { Picker } from "../store/ui";
import { SubIssueList } from "./SubIssueList";

function Pill({ label, onClick, children }: { label: string; onClick(): void; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" aria-label={label} onClick={onClick}
      className="flex h-(--size-pill) w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-full px-2.5 text-left font-medium text-ink-2 transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-hover">
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function PropGroup({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section className="mb-5">
      <h3 className="mb-1 px-2 font-medium text-ink-3">{label}</h3>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">{children}</div>
    </section>
  );
}

function TitleInput({ issue }: { issue: Issue }) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(issue.title);
  useEffect(() => setValue(issue.title), [issue.title]);
  const commit = () => {
    const next = value.trim();
    if (next && next !== issue.title) void appStore.getState().updateIssue(issue.id, { title: next });
    else setValue(issue.title);
  };
  return (
    <input aria-label="Title" value={value} onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)} onBlur={commit}
      onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === "Enter") e.currentTarget.blur(); }}
      className="mb-4 w-full bg-transparent text-title font-medium text-ink-1 outline-none" />
  );
}

/** Spec §8 issue page, Phase 2 subset: title, description, sub-issues, and property pills that open the pickers. */
export function IssueDetail({ id }: { id: number }) {
  const app = useAppState();
  const issue = app.issues[id];
  useEffect(() => {
    void appStore.getState().loadIssueDetail(id);
  }, [id]);
  const subs = useMemo(() => Object.values(app.subIssues).filter((t) => t.issue_id === id).sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index), [app.subIssues, id]);
  if (!issue) return <Empty>Loading…</Empty>;
  const project = issue.project_id ? app.projects[issue.project_id] : undefined;
  const cycle = issue.cycle_id ? app.cycles[issue.cycle_id] : undefined;
  const open = (picker: Picker) => uiStore.getState().openPalette(picker, [id]);
  const estimate = issue.estimate === null ? "No estimate" : `${issue.estimate}`;
  const labels = issue.labels.join(", ") || "No labels";
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1">
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-10 py-8">
        <TitleInput issue={issue} />
        <DescriptionEditor key={issue.id} html={issue.description}
          onSave={(html) => void appStore.getState().updateIssue(id, { description: html })} />
        <SubIssueList subs={subs} />
      </div>
      <aside aria-label="Properties" className="w-(--size-props) shrink-0 overflow-y-auto border-l border-line-1 px-3 py-8">
        <PropGroup label="Properties">
          <Pill label={`Status: ${STATUS_LABEL[issue.status]}`} onClick={() => open("status")}>
            <StatusIcon status={issue.status} />{STATUS_LABEL[issue.status]}
          </Pill>
          <Pill label={`Priority: ${PRIORITY_LABEL[issue.priority]}`} onClick={() => open("priority")}>
            <PriorityIcon priority={issue.priority} />{PRIORITY_LABEL[issue.priority]}
          </Pill>
          <Pill label={`Assignee: ${issue.assignee?.name ?? "No assignee"}`} onClick={() => open("assignee")}>
            <Avatar user={issue.assignee} />{issue.assignee?.name ?? "No assignee"}
          </Pill>
          <Pill label={`Estimate: ${estimate}`} onClick={() => open("estimate")}>{estimate}{issue.estimate !== null && " points"}</Pill>
          <Pill label={`Blocked: ${issue.blocked ? "Yes" : "No"}`} onClick={() => void appStore.getState().updateIssue(id, { blocked: !issue.blocked })}>
            {issue.blocked ? <span className="text-blocked">Blocked</span> : "Not blocked"}
          </Pill>
        </PropGroup>
        <PropGroup label="Labels">
          <Pill label={`Labels: ${labels}`} onClick={() => open("labels")}><span className="truncate">{labels}</span></Pill>
        </PropGroup>
        <PropGroup label="Project">
          <Pill label={`Project: ${project?.name ?? "No project"}`} onClick={() => open("project")}>
            <span className="truncate">{project?.name ?? "No project"}</span>
          </Pill>
        </PropGroup>
        <PropGroup label="Cycle">
          <Pill label={`Cycle: ${cycle?.name ?? "No cycle"}`} onClick={() => open("cycle")}>{cycle?.name ?? "No cycle"}</Pill>
        </PropGroup>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/issue/IssuePage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Empty } from "../components/ViewFrame";
import { appStore, uiStore, useApp, useUi } from "../store";
import { IssueDetail } from "./IssueDetail";

/** /issue/:key. Opens from the cache, or loads by key; an unknown key says so (review focus 2). */
export function IssuePage({ issueKey }: { issueKey: string }) {
  const id = useApp((s) => Object.values(s.issues).find((i) => i.key === issueKey)?.id ?? null);
  const issue = useApp((s) => (id === null ? undefined : s.issues[id]));
  const team = useApp((s) => (issue ? s.teams[issue.team_id] : undefined));
  const back = useUi((u) => u.backRoute);
  const [missing, setMissing] = useState<"not-found" | "error" | null>(null);
  const known = id !== null;
  useEffect(() => {
    setMissing(null);
    if (known) return;
    let live = true;
    void appStore.getState().loadIssueDetail(issueKey).then((r) => {
      if (live && typeof r !== "number") setMissing(r);
    });
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [issueKey, known]);
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
      <header className="flex h-(--size-header) shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-line-1 px-4">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => uiStore.getState().navigate(back)} className="text-ink-3 hover:text-ink-1">{team?.name ?? "Back"}</button>
        <ChevronRight size={14} className="text-ink-4" />
        <span className="font-medium text-ink-1">{issueKey}</span>
        {issue && <span className="truncate text-ink-3">{issue.title}</span>}
      </header>
      {missing === "not-found" ? <Empty>Issue {issueKey} not found.</Empty>
        : missing === "error" ? <Empty>Couldn't load {issueKey}.</Empty>
        : id === null ? <Empty>Loading {issueKey}…</Empty>
        : <IssueDetail id={id} />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { uiStore, useApp, useUi } from "../store";
import { IssueDetail } from "./IssueDetail";

/** Space opens the issue as a side panel (spec §8). */
export function IssuePanel() {
  const id = useUi((u) => u.peekId);
  const key = useApp((s) => (id === null ? undefined : s.issues[id]?.key));
  if (id === null) return null;
  return (
    <aside aria-label="Issue preview" className="fixed bottom-2 right-2 top-2 z-30 flex w-[640px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line-2 bg-panel shadow-palette">
      <header className="flex h-(--size-header) shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-line-1 px-3">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => key && uiStore.getState().openIssue(key)} className="rounded-control px-2 py-1 text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover hover:text-ink-1">
          Open full page
        </button>
        <button type="button" aria-label="Close preview" onClick={() => uiStore.getState().openPeek(null)} className="ml-auto rounded-control p-1.5 text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover">
          <X size={14} />
        </button>
      </header>
      <IssueDetail id={id} />
    </aside>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `web/src/components/Composer.tsx`**

```tsx
import * as Dialog from "@radix-ui/react-dialog";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { PRIORITIES, PRIORITY_LABEL, STATUS_LABEL, type Priority, type Project, type Status } from "../api/types";
import { ESTIMATES } from "../palette/pickers";
import { appStore, uiStore, useAppState, useUi } from "../store";
import type { AppState } from "../store/store";
import { sidebarTeamIds } from "../store/ui";

const field = "h-(--size-pill) rounded-full border border-line-2 bg-transparent px-2.5 text-small text-ink-2";
const LAST = (team: number) => `mira-last-project:${team}`;

export function lastProject(team: number): number | null {
  try {
    const v = Number(localStorage.getItem(LAST(team)));
    return Number.isInteger(v) && v > 0 ? v : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function rememberProject(team: number, project: number): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(LAST(team), String(project));
  } catch {
    /* storage blocked: no default next time */
  }
}

/** A story always needs a project (D15). In demo mode only [DEMO] projects can take new issues. */
export function assignableProjects(app: Pick<AppState, "projects" | "meta">, teamId: number | null): Project[] {
  if (teamId === null) return [];
  const prefix = app.meta?.writeGuard === "demo" ? app.meta.demoPrefix : null;
  return Object.values(app.projects)
    .filter((p) => p.team_id === teamId && p.status_category !== "completed" && (prefix === null || p.name.startsWith(prefix)))
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}

/** Spec §8 composer, Phase 2 subset: team, title, status, priority, assignee, project (required), estimate. */
export function Composer() {
  const open = useUi((u) => u.composerOpen);
  const route = useUi((u) => u.route);
  const joined = useUi((u) => u.joinedTeamIds);
  const hidden = useUi((u) => u.hiddenTeamIds);
  const app = useAppState();
  const meta = app.meta;
  const prefix = meta?.writeGuard === "demo" ? meta.demoPrefix : "";
  const teamIds = useMemo(() => sidebarTeamIds(app.yourTeamIds, joined, hidden), [app.yourTeamIds, joined, hidden]);
  const [teamId, setTeamId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(prefix);
  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [chose, setChose] = useState(false); // the person picked a project themselves
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>("backlog");
  const [priority, setPriority] = useState<Priority>(0);
  const [assigneeId, setAssigneeId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [estimate, setEstimate] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const here = route.name === "team" ? route.teamId : null;
    setTeamId(here ?? (meta?.writeGuard === "demo" ? meta.demoTeamId : null) ?? teamIds[0] ?? null);
    setTitle(prefix);
    setChose(false);
    setStatus(route.name === "team" && route.tab === "active" ? "todo" : "backlog"); // Todo inside Active (spec §8)
    setPriority(0);
    setAssigneeId(route.name === "my" ? meta?.me.id ?? null : null);                  // you, from My issues
    setEstimate(null);
  }, [open]); // reset each time the composer opens

  useEffect(() => { // the team's projects arrive with its load
    if (open && teamId !== null && !appStore.getState().teamState[teamId]) void appStore.getState().loadTeam(teamId);
  }, [open, teamId]);

  const projects = useMemo(() => assignableProjects(app, teamId), [app.projects, app.meta, teamId]);
  const projectIds = projects.map((p) => p.id).join(",");
  useEffect(() => { // default to the last project used for this team, until the person picks one
    if (!open || teamId === null) return;
    const last = lastProject(teamId);
    setProjectId((current) => (chose && projects.some((p) => p.id === current) ? current
      : projects.some((p) => p.id === last) ? last : projects[0]?.id ?? null));
  }, [open, teamId, projectIds, chose]);

  const close = () => uiStore.getState().setComposer(false);
  const canSubmit = teamId !== null && projectId !== null && title.trim().length > prefix.trim().length && title.startsWith(prefix) && !busy;
  const submit = async () => {
    if (!canSubmit || teamId === null || projectId === null) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const created = await appStore.getState().createIssue({
      team_id: teamId, title: title.trim(), project_id: projectId, status, priority, assignee_id: assigneeId, estimate,
      position: status === "backlog" ? "bottom" : "top",
    });
    setBusy(false);
    if (created) {
      rememberProject(teamId, projectId);
      close();
      uiStore.getState().select(created.id);
    }
  };
  const teamName = teamId !== null ? app.teams[teamId]?.name ?? "Team" : "Team";

  return (
    <Dialog.Root open={open} onOpenChange={(o) => !o && close()}>
      <Dialog.Portal>
        <Dialog.Overlay className="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-black/10" />
        <Dialog.Content aria-describedby={undefined}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === "Enter" && (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey)) {
              e.preventDefault();
              void submit();
            }
          }}
          className="fixed left-1/2 top-[14%] z-50 w-[600px] -translate-x-1/2 rounded-palette border-[0.5px] border-line-2 bg-surface p-4 shadow-palette">
          <Dialog.Title className="mb-3 font-medium text-ink-3">{teamName} › New issue</Dialog.Title>
          <input aria-label="Issue title" autoFocus value={title} placeholder="Issue title" onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
            className="mb-3 w-full bg-transparent text-regular font-medium text-ink-1 outline-none" />
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <select aria-label="Team" value={teamId ?? ""} className={field}
              onChange={(e) => { setTeamId(Number(e.target.value)); setChose(false); }}>
              {teamIds.map((id) => <option key={id} value={id}>{app.teams[id]?.name ?? `Team ${id}`}</option>)}
            </select>
            <select aria-label="Status" value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value as Status)} className={field}>
              {(["backlog", "todo"] as Status[]).map((s) => <option key={s} value={s}>{STATUS_LABEL[s]}</option>)}
            </select>
            <select aria-label="Priority" value={priority} onChange={(e) => setPriority(Number(e.target.value) as Priority)} className={field}>
              {PRIORITIES.map((p) => <option key={p} value={p}>{PRIORITY_LABEL[p]}</option>)}
            </select>
            <select aria-label="Assignee" value={assigneeId ?? ""} className={field}
              onChange={(e) => setAssigneeId(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null)}>
              <option value="">No assignee</option>
              {meta && <option value={meta.me.id}>{meta.me.name}</option>}
            </select>
            <select aria-label="Project" value={projectId ?? ""} className={field}
              onChange={(e) => { setProjectId(Number(e.target.value)); setChose(true); }}>
              {projects.length === 0 && <option value="">No project</option>}
              {projects.map((p) => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>)}
            </select>
            <select aria-label="Estimate" value={estimate ?? ""} className={field}
              onChange={(e) => setEstimate(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null)}>
              <option value="">No estimate</option>
              {ESTIMATES.map((n) => <option key={n} value={n}>{n === 1 ? "1 point" : `${n} points`}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          {teamId !== null && projects.length === 0 && (
            <p className="mt-3 text-mini text-blocked">Rally needs a project for every issue, and this team has no {prefix ? "[DEMO] " : "open "}projects.</p>
          )}
          <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
            <button type="button" onClick={close} className="rounded-control px-3 py-1.5 text-ink-3 hover:bg-hover">Cancel</button>
            <button type="button" disabled={!canSubmit} onClick={() => void submit()}
              className="rounded-control bg-ink-1 px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-surface disabled:opacity-40">
              Create issue <kbd className="ml-1 text-mini opacity-70">⌘↵</kbd>
            </button>
          </div>
        </Dialog.Content>
      </Dialog.Portal>
    </Dialog.Root>
  );
}
```
